Real clinical text is constrained by privacy and ethics, while existing benchmarks focus on isolated interactions, failing to capture cross-session reasoning. We introduce a framework for synthesizing high-quality, long-term medical dialogues with LLMs. Our approach entails a knowledge-guided decomposition into three stages: constructing synthetic patient profiles with diverse disease and complication trajectories, generating multi-turn dialogues per encounter, and integrating them into a coherent longitudinal history dataset, MediLongChat. We establish three benchmark tasks-In-dialogue Reasoning, Cross-dialogue Reasoning, and Synthesis Reasoning-to evaluate the memory capabilities of healthcare agents. To assess data quality, we introduce a multi-dimensional evaluation framework combining vector-based metrics with LLM-as-a-judge assessments. Specifically, we define automatic measures-Faithfulness, Coherence, and Diversity-together with two LLM-based evaluations: Correctness and Realism. Benchmark experiments show that even state-of-the-art LLMs struggle with MediLongChat. These findings highlight the benchmark's applicability and underscore the need for tailored methods to advance healthcare agents.

</details>


### [36] [Towards Trust Calibration in Socially Interactive Agents: Investigating Gendered Multimodal Behaviors Generation with LLMs](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.19798)
*Lucie Galland,Chloé Clavel,Magalie Ochs*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 本文探索了大语言模型生成多模态行为以反映信任维度（能力和善意）的能力，提出了一种自动生成方法，验证了GPT-5.4能产生连贯的多模态行为且符合理论预期，但发现模型会复制性别刻板印象，这一发现对信任校准社交智能体的设计具有重要的伦理和应用启示。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 随着社交智能体日益融入日常生活，需要将用户信任校准到智能体的实际能力水平，以确保对这些智能体的适当使用。

Method: 提出一种自动生成多模态行为的方法，使行为与能力和善意两个信任维度的特定水平对齐；利用随机森林特征重要性分析验证行为与理论预期的一致性；通过Prolific平台进行被试内设计的用户研究进行验证。

Result: GPT-5.4能够生成跨文本、语调、面部表情和手势等模态的一致行为；生成的行为符合能力与善意的理论预期；但当提示中指定性别时，LLM倾向于复制社会性别刻板印象，将男性智能体行为与高能力关联，女性智能体行为与高善意关联；用户研究验证了参与者能感知到生成行为中不同水平的能力和善意。

Conclusion: LLMs能够生成反映能力和善意水平的连贯多模态行为，为信任校准的社交互动奠定基础；但需警惕模型在性别指定时复制社会刻板印象，这需要在实际应用中加以约束和引导。

Abstract: As Socially Interactive Agents (SIAs) become increasingly integrated into daily life, the ability to calibrate user trust to an agent's actual capabilities would help ensure appropriate usage of these agents. In this paper, we explore the capacity of Large Language Models (LLMs) to generate multimodal behaviors (verbal, vocal, gestural, and facial expression modalities) that reflect varying levels of ability and benevolence, two key dimensions of trustworthiness. We propose a novel method for automatically generating behaviors aligned with specific levels of these traits, a first step towards enabling nuanced and trust-calibrated interactions. By analyzing a large dataset of multimodal transcripts generated by LLMs, we demonstrate that GPT-5.4 is able to produce coherent behavior across different modalities (text, intonation, facial expression, and gesture). Using Random Forest feature importance analysis, we show that the generated behaviors align with theoretical expectations for ability and benevolence. However, we also find that when gender is specified in the prompt, LLMs tend to reproduce societal gender stereotypes, associating male agents' behaviors with high ability and female agents' behaviors with high benevolence. To validate our approach, we conducted a user study on Prolific using a within-subjects design. Participants perceived different levels of ability and benevolence in the generated behaviors align with the intended instructions.

</details>


### [37] [Chunking German Legal Code](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.19806)
*Max Prior,Natalia Milanova,Andreas Schultz*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 本研究评估了多种文本分块策略在德国制定法检索增强生成中的应用，发现与法律内在结构对齐的分块方法（如按章节、条款分块）在召回率和计算效率上均优于复杂的语义分块技术。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 检索增强生成（RAG）在法律领域的应用面临文档结构化分割的挑战，现有方法往往忽略法律文本的内在层级结构，导致检索精度与计算成本之间的权衡不明确。

Method: 使用《德国民法典》作为基准语料库，实现并对比了7种分块策略：结构分块（章节/条款/句子/命题）、固定窗口分块、上下文分块、语义聚类、Lumber分块和RAPTOR层次检索，评估指标包括召回率、查询延迟、索引构建时间和存储开销。

Result: 与法律结构对齐的分块方法（特别是章节和条款级）获得最高召回率，而覆盖原结构的方法表现更差；结构分块在计算效率上也优于LLM密集型方法（如上下文分块、RAPTOR、Lumber）。

Conclusion: 在法律信息检索中，保持领域特定结构比语义富集更重要，需在语义丰富性与运营成本之间权衡。

Abstract: This paper investigates chunking strategies for retrieval-augmented generation on German statutory law, using the German Civil Code as a structured benchmark corpus. We implement and compare a range of segmentation approaches, including structural units (sections, subsections, sentences, propositions), fixed-size windows, contextual chunking, semantic clustering, Lumber-style chunking, and RAPTOR-based hierarchical retrieval. All methods are evaluated on a legal question-answering dataset with section-level gold labels, measuring recall, query latency, index build time, and storage requirements. Results show that chunking strategies aligned with the inherent legal structure - particularly section and subsection - based retrieval-achieve the highest recall, while more complex approaches that override this structure perform worse. These simpler methods also offer favorable computational efficiency compared to LLM-intensive techniques such as contextual chunking, RAPTOR, and Lumber. The findings highlight a key trade-off between semantic enrichment and operational cost, and demonstrate that preserving domain-specific structure is critical for effective legal information retrieval.

</details>


### [38] [LP-Eval: Rubric and Dataset for Measuring the Quality of Legal Proposition Generation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.19815)
*Shanshan Xu,Johan Lindholm,Amogh Raina,Henrik Palmer Olsen,Daniel Hershcovich*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: This paper explores automatic generation of legal propositions from CJEU decisions using LLMs, introducing LP-Eval—a three-step expert co-designed rubric—and a dataset of 100 annotated propositions. Findings indicate LLMs generate quality propositions (especially from established cases), and rubric-guided LLM evaluation aligns better with experts than direct scoring, though still missing finer distinctions.


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: Legal proposition generation is under-explored in Legal NLP despite being central to legal reasoning and doctrinal scholarship; need for systematic evaluation frameworks.

Method: LP-Eval three-step evaluation rubric (formal validity + substantive dimensions); expert annotation of 100 LLM-generated propositions; comparative analysis of LLM vs. human evaluators.

Result: LLMs can generate well-formed, high-quality legal propositions; quality is higher for propositions from established case law than recent cases; rubric-guided LLM evaluation outperforms direct scoring but still lags expert-level granularity.

Conclusion: LLM judgments guided by rubric align better with experts than direct scoring, but still miss finer-grained distinctions; expert-annotated dataset released for future research.

Abstract: Legal proposition generation is central to legal reasoning and doctrinal scholarship, yet remain under-examined in Legal NLP. This paper investigates the automatic generation and evaluation of legal propositions from decisions of the Court of Justice of the European Union using large language models (LLMs). We introduce LP-Eval, a three-step evaluation rubric co-designed with legal experts that decomposes legal proposition quality into formal validity and substantive dimensions. Using this rubric, we release a dataset of two experts' annotations for 100 LLM-generated legal propositions. Our results show that LLMs can generate predominantly well-formed and high-quality propositions, while expert evaluations reveal higher quality for propositions derived from well established cases than from recent ones. We further examine LLMs as evaluators and find that rubric-guided LLM judgments align more closely with expert assessments than direct overall scoring, but remain insensitive to finer-grained distinctions captured by human experts.

</details>


### [39] [Are Tools Always Beneficial? Learning to Invoke Tools Adaptively for Dual-Mode Multimodal LLM Reasoning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.19852)
*Qinghe Ma,Zhen Zhao,Yiming Wu,Jian Zhang,Lei Bai,Yinghuan Shi*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 本文提出AutoTool，一种在多模态大语言模型中自适应决定是否调用工具的推理框架，通过强化学习结合双模式推理策略，在保证精度的同时显著提升效率。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有工具增强推理研究仅关注如何调用工具，却忽视了何时应调用工具。盲目调用工具会增加推理开销甚至误导预测，因此需要模型自适应决策工具使用必要性。

Method: 在强化学习框架下设计显式双模式推理策略（工具辅助模式与纯文本模式），并设置模式特定奖励函数；训练过程中联合探索两种模式防止过早收敛至单一模式，后期促进自由探索。

Result: 在V*基准上较基线模型提升21.8%准确率，在POPE基准上较现有工具增强方法提升44.9%效率。

Conclusion: AutoTool通过自适应决策机制实现了精度与效率的良好平衡，证明了并非所有问题都需要工具调用，适度使用工具或仅依靠文本推理可能更为高效。

Abstract: Tool-augmented reasoning has emerged as a promising direction for enhancing the reasoning capabilities of multimodal large language models (MLLMs). However, existing studies mainly focus on enabling models to perform tool invocation, while neglecting the necessity of invoking tools. We argue that tool usage is not always beneficial, as redundant or inappropriate invocations largely increase reasoning overhead and even mislead model predictions. To address this issue, we introduce AutoTool, a model that adaptively decides whether to invoke tools according to the characteristics of each query. Within a reinforcement learning framework, we design an explicit dual-mode reasoning strategy with mode-specific reward functions to guide the model toward producing accurate responses. Moreover, to prevent premature bias toward a single reasoning mode, AutoTool jointly explores and balances tool-assisted and text-centric reasoning throughout training, and promotes free exploration in later stages. Extensive experiments demonstrate that AutoTool exhibits outstanding performance and high efficiency, yielding a 21.8\% accuracy gain on V* benchmark compared to the base model, and a 44.9\% improvement in efficiency over existing tool-augmented methods on POPE benchmark. Code is available at https://github.com/MQinghe/AutoTool.

</details>


### [40] [What Are LLMs Doing to Scientific Communication? Measuring Changes in Writing Practices and Reading Experience](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.19936)
*Filip Miletić,Neele Falk*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 研究调查了大语言模型(LLM)是否改变了学术写作风格。通过分析ACL语料库中的37,000篇论文并创建3,000个人机对比文本,发现词频和使用语境显著变化,LLM修改的文本词汇更复杂但多样性降低。专家评价表明LLM改进的文本更易理解且更有吸引力,但对AI辅助写作持负面态度。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: Investigate whether the growing use of LLMs in scientific writing has altered the style and characteristics of scientific communication, particularly in the NLP domain.

Method: Diachronic lexical analyses on 37k paper corpus; stylistic feature modeling comparing human vs LLM-modified texts; expert annotation study (n=20).

Result: Significant temporal changes in word frequency and usage contexts; LLM-modified texts show more complex/longer words, lower lexical diversity, and specific syntactic patterns; experts rate LLM-improved texts as more understandable and exciting, but express negative attitudes toward AI assistance.

Conclusion: LLM-assisted writing is changing scientific communication style; while texts are rated as more understandable and engaging, negative attitudes toward AI persist, indicating a disconnect between objective text quality improvements and subjective reading experience.

Abstract: Has the style of scientific communication changed due to the growing use of large language models in the writing process? We address this question in the domain of Natural Language Processing by leveraging two data resources we create: a naturalistic corpus of over 37,000 papers from the ACL Anthology (2020-2024); and a synthetic dataset of 3,000 human-written passages and their LLM-generated improvements. We first implement a series of diachronic lexical analyses, showing that both word frequency and usage contexts have changed significantly over time, indicating semantic specialization in some cases and generalization in others. Broadening our perspective, we then model a range of more complex stylistic features and find that LLM-modified texts more frequently contain certain syntactic constructions, more complex and longer words and a lower lexical diversity. Finally, we connect these changes in writing practices to subjective reading experience through a pilot annotation study with 20 domain experts. They overall rate LLM-improved texts as more understandable and exciting, but also express negative qualitative attitudes towards LLMs, highlighting the strongly subjective effect of AI-assisted writing on reading experience.

</details>


### [41] [FlexDraft: Flexible Speculative Decoding via Attention Tuning and Bonus-Guided Calibration](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.20022)
*Yaojie Zhang,Jianuo Huang,Junlong Ke,Yuhang Han,Yongji Long,Tianchen Zhao,Biqing Qi,Linfeng Zhang*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 本文提出FlexDraft框架，通过注意力调优、奖励引导校准和灵活解码三大设计，解决现有并行推测解码方法在大批量场景下吞吐量崩溃的问题，实现无损的LLM推理加速。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有并行推测解码方法在大批量场景下面临两个核心问题：奖励标记和接受长度的的不确定性导致草案验证不匹配，使吞吐量增益崩溃；同时这些方法要么需要昂贵的持续预训练且存在质量下降问题，要么接受率较低。因此需要一种能够适应不同批量大小、无质量损失的推测解码框架。

Method: 提出FlexDraft框架，包含三大设计：(1)注意力调优：仅微调最后几层的注意力投影器处理掩码标记，保持自回归路径冻结以保留目标分布，实现块扩散草案生成；(2)奖励引导校准：使用轻量级MLP基于已解析的奖励标记校准草案logits；(3)灵活解码：根据批大小动态切换并行/顺序草案验证策略，并根据草案置信度调整验证长度。

Result: FlexDraft实现了无损的推测解码加速，能够灵活适应不同批量大小，显著提升了大批量场景下的吞吐量。

Conclusion: FlexDraft通过注意力调优实现高质量块扩散草案，通过奖励引导校准缓解草案验证不匹配问题，通过灵活解码在不同批大小下选择最优执行策略，实现了无损的推测解码加速，显著提升了大规模批量场景下的吞吐量。

Abstract: Speculative decoding accelerates memory-bound LLM inference without quality degradation by using a fast drafter to propose multiple candidate tokens and the target model to verify them in parallel. However, conventional sequential speculative decoding suffers from mutual waiting between drafting and verification, and repeated exchange of intermediate states further increases memory access overhead. Parallel speculative decoding addresses this limitation by performing drafting and verification within a single target forward pass, allowing future drafts to be prepared while current candidates are being verified. Although effective at small batch sizes, existing parallel speculative decoding methods either require costly continual pretraining with quality degradation or suffer from low acceptance rates. More importantly, this paradigm inherently suffers from uncertainty in both the bonus token and the accepted length, leading to draft verification mismatch and causing throughput gains to collapse at large batch sizes. To address these limitations, we introduce FlexDraft, a lossless speculative decoding framework that flexibly adapts to varying batch sizes through three key designs. (1) Attention Tuning enables block diffusion drafting by tuning only the attention projectors of the final few layers on mask tokens, while keeping the autoregressive path frozen to preserve the target distribution and produce high quality drafts with minimal trainable parameters. (2) Bonus-guided Calibration uses a lightweight MLP conditioned on the resolved bonus token to calibrate draft logits, mitigating draft verification mismatch caused by bonus token uncertainty. (3) Flex Decoding dynamically switches between parallel draft and verify at small batch sizes and sequential draft then verify at large batch sizes, and adjusts verification length based on draft confidence to eliminate redundant computation.

</details>


### [42] [Mind Your Moras: Orthography-Aware Error Analysis of Neural Japanese Morphological Generation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.20043)
*Wen Zhang*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 该研究对日语过去时态形态变位进行了正字法感知的错误分析，发现尽管字符级seq2seq模型准确率高，但存在系统性错误，尤其是促音化相关错误占75-80%，且错误模式在不同架构间高度一致，表明假名正字法表示、形态结构与数据频率的交互作用对模型泛化有稳健影响。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 字符级模型在日语形态任务中虽达到高准确率，但存在系统性错误。假名不仅是转录媒介，更是编码形态音位区分的表示系统，这可能影响模型泛化。

Method: 使用SIGMORPHON 2020和2023共享任务格式的数据集，评估两种字符级序列到序列架构在日语过去时态构成上的表现；构建了包含7种主要失败模式的错误分类法。

Result: 尽管整体准确率高，模型表现出系统性、可语言学解释的错误，聚集在假名的特定正字法特征上。75-80%的残余错误与促音化相关，尤其在词干以e元音结尾且需在过去时态后缀前添加促音的动词中。错误模式在不同架构和随机种子间高度一致。

Conclusion: 研究强调了正字法感知评估对于理解形态复杂语言中神经泛化的必要性，指出假名表示系统与形态结构的交互作用对模型泛化有重要影响。

Abstract: We present an orthography-aware error analysis of Japanese past-tense morphological inflection, treating hiragana not merely as a transcriptional medium, but as a representational system encoding morphophonological distinctions that may influence model generalization. We evaluate two character-level sequence-to-sequence architectures on past-tense formation using datasets formatted according to the SIGMORPHON 2020 and 2023 shared task conventions. Despite high aggregate accuracy, models exhibit systematic, linguistically interpretable errors that cluster around specific orthographic properties of hiragana. We introduce a concise error taxonomy capturing seven primary failure modes and provide both quantitative and qualitative analyses. Gemination-related errors dominate residual failures, accounting for 75-80% of errors, particularly in verbs whose stems end in the vowel e and require gemination before the past-tense suffix. Error patterns remain highly consistent across architectures and random seeds, suggesting a robust interaction between orthographic representation, morphological structure, and data frequency effects in shaping model generalization. These results underscore the necessity of orthography-aware evaluation for understanding neural generalization in morphologically complex languages.

</details>


### [43] [Language Mutations Sustain the Persistences of Conspiracy Theories on Social Media](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.20050)
*Calvin Yixiang Cheng,Dorian Quelle,Scott A. Hale*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 研究发现阴谋论的语言变异越大越难被消除，心理语言学和结构（AAT）层面的简化与同化是两种主要变异模式，内容审核策略应转向识别核心主张而非追踪表面措辞变化。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 阴谋论如何在社交媒体上持久传播且难以被审核机制遏制的现象尚缺乏深入理解。

Method: 计算语言学分析 + 生存建模，基于X平台三年数据集，辅以定性分析识别两种主要变异模式。

Result: 心理语言学特征（代词、社交词汇、认知过程术语、风险/健康词汇）及AAT结构的语义变异均与更长存续期显著相关；质性分析发现简化与同化两种主要变异模式。

Conclusion: 研究发现语义变异越大阴谋论传播时间越长；内容审核应关注核心主张而非表面措辞变异，聚焦可应对多种变体的基础内容。

Abstract: This study investigates how language mutations affect the persistent diffusion of conspiracy theories on social media. Drawing on a three-year dataset of conspiracy-related posts from X, and applying computational linguistic analysis alongside survival modelling, we find that conspiracy claims with greater semantic mutations have substantially longer lifespans. Mutations in psycholinguistic properties, including pronouns, social reference words, cognitive process terms, risk- and health- related vocabularies, are associated with extended lifespans. Mutations in actor, action and target (AAT) categories are associated with longer lifespans as well. Qualitative analysis identifies two predominant mutation patterns: simplification and assimilation, at both linguistic and AAT structural levels. Taken together, the results advance our understanding of how language mutations contribute to conspiracy persistence online and shed lights on longitudinal content moderation strategies. We argue that content moderation should consider the mutability of conspiracy claims and focus on the core claims that can address their potential variations.

</details>


### [44] [PromptRad: Knowledge-Enhanced Multi-Label Prompt-Tuning for Low-Resource Radiology Report Labeling](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.20052)
*Ying-Jia Lin,Tzu-Chin Lo,Ping-Chien Li,Chi-Tung Cheng,Chien-Hung Liao,Hung-Yu Kao*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: PromptRad提出一种知识增强的多标签提示微调方法，利用UMLS同义词库丰富类别表示，在仅有32个标注样本的低资源场景下即可有效识别CT报告中的临床发现，性能可与GPT-4竞争。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: Rule-based labelers struggle with diverse descriptions in clinical reports, while fine-tuning pre-trained language models requires large amounts of labeled data that are often unavailable in clinical settings.

Method: PromptRad reformulates multi-label classification as masked language modeling and incorporates synonyms from UMLS Metathesaurus into a multi-word verbalizer to enrich category representations, enabling fine-tuning of pre-trained language models without additional classification layers.

Result: With only 32 labeled training examples, PromptRad outperforms dictionary-based and fine-tuning baselines, achieves competitive performance with GPT-4, and captures complex negation patterns more effectively than existing methods.

Conclusion: PromptRad provides a promising solution for automatic radiology report labeling in data-scarce clinical scenarios, outperforming dictionary-based and fine-tuning baselines with minimal training data (32 examples) and achieving competitive performance with GPT-4 while using a much smaller model.

Abstract: Automatic report labeling facilitates the identification of clinical findings from unstructured text and enables large-scale annotation for medical imaging research. Existing rule-based labelers struggle with the diverse descriptions in clinical reports, while fine-tuning pre-trained language models (PLMs) requires large amounts of labeled data that are often unavailable in clinical settings. In this paper, we propose PromptRad, a knowledge-enhanced multi-label \textbf{prompt}-tuning approach for \textbf{rad}iology report labeling under low-resource settings. PromptRad reformulates multi-label classification as masked language modeling and incorporates synonyms from the UMLS Metathesaurus into a multi-word verbalizer to enrich category representations. By fine-tuning the PLM without additional classification layers, PromptRad requires substantially less labeled data than conventional fine-tuning. Experiments on liver CT reports show that PromptRad outperforms dictionary-based and fine-tuning baselines with only 32 labeled training examples, and achieves competitive performance with GPT-4 despite using a much smaller model. Further analysis demonstrates that PromptRad captures complex negation patterns more effectively than existing methods, making it a promising solution for report labeling in data-scarce clinical scenarios. Our code is available at https://github.com/ila-lab/PromptRad.

</details>


### [45] [Text-to-SPARQL Generation with Reinforcement Learning: A GRPO-based Approach on DBLP](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.20066)
*Jann Pfeifer,Debayan Banerjee,Ricardo Usbeck*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 本研究探索在缺乏标注查询的情况下，使用基于结果的强化学习（GRPO）训练小模型（Qwen3-1.7B）完成零样本Text-to-SPARQL生成任务。实验表明执行奖励是主要收益来源，GRPO显著优于零样本基线，但监督式DoRA微调仍保持更高整体准确率。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有知识图谱问答方法依赖大型模型或需要大量标注查询（gold query annotations）进行全监督训练，缺乏标注数据时难以应用。

Method: 采用Group-Relative Policy Optimization (GRPO)算法对Qwen3-1.7B模型进行训练，使用DBLP-QuAD学术领域数据集，通过自然语言问题结合实体和关系符号提示的prompt模板，奖励信号包括执行反馈、结构约束和答案级奖励，并设计了融入gold-query reward shaping的变体模型进行对比。

Result: GRPO大幅超越零样本基线，在未见模板的泛化任务上表现具有竞争力；监督式DoRA微调在相同模型规模下取得更高整体准确率；消融分析表明执行奖励贡献了大部分收益，额外的reward shaping收益有限。

Conclusion: 当gold queries不可用于token级监督时，基于结果的强化学习是一种可行的训练策略；执行反馈而非gold query shaping是性能提升的主要驱动力。

Abstract: Knowledge graph question answering seeks to translate natural language questions into executable queries over knowledge graphs, but existing approaches often rely on large models or full supervision in the form of gold query annotations. This study examines whether reinforcement learning with outcome-based rewards can train a small instruction-tuned language model to perform zero-shot Text-to-SPARQL generation in the scholarly domain. Group-Relative Policy Optimization (GRPO) is applied to the Qwen3-1.7B model on DBLP-QuAD, using prompts that combine natural language questions with symbolic hints about entities and relations. Training relies on execution feedback, structural constraints, and answer-level rewards, with an additional variant that incorporates gold-query-based shaping. The resulting models are compared to the unmodified zero-shot baseline and to a supervised DoRA-finetuned baseline across answer-level accuracy, execution accuracy, category-wise scores, and generalization to held-out templates. GRPO substantially improves over the zero-shot baseline and exhibits competitive generalization, while supervised DoRA finetuning achieves higher overall accuracy on the same model scale. Ablation analyses indicate that execution-based rewards account for most gains, with additional shaping yielding limited additional benefit, suggesting that outcome-based reinforcement learning is a viable training strategy when gold queries are unavailable for token-level supervision.

</details>


### [46] [CopT: Contrastive On-Policy Thinking with Continuous Spaces for General and Agentic Reasoning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.20075)
*Dachuan Shi,Hanlin Zhu,Xiangchi Yuan,Wanjia Zhao,Kejing Xia,Wen Xiao,Wenke Lee*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: CopT 是一种创新的推理框架，它反转了传统 CoT 的顺序——先产生草稿答案，再基于该答案进行反思性思考，并使用对比验证器评估答案可靠性，动态控制思考深度，从而在多个任务中实现了精度与效率的双重提升。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 传统 CoT 将思考视为回答的前提步骤，但这会导致：当模型实际上能够快速识别答案时，过长的思考过程会造成响应延迟和不必要的 token 消耗，即"表演性推理"问题。

Method: CopT 提出两种 KL 散度估计器：(1) 反向 KL 估计器——通过对比离散 token 输入与连续 embedding 输入下模型对同一生成 token 的支持度，来估计答案可靠性；(2) 动态控制估计器——控制草稿答案的可见性，保留有用部分信息同时降低不可靠内容的误导风险。

Result: 实验结果表明 CopT 可在保持或提升精度的前提下，显著降低 token 消耗。

Conclusion: CopT 在数学、编程和智能体推理任务中实现了最高 23% 的精度提升，在相同或更高精度下将 token 使用量降低高达 57%，且无需额外训练。

Abstract: Chain-of-thought (CoT) is a standard approach for eliciting reasoning capabilities from large language models (LLMs). However, the common CoT paradigm treats thinking as a prerequisite for answering, which can delay access to plausible answers and incur unnecessary token costs even when the model is able to identify an answer before extended thinking, a behavior known as performative reasoning. In this paper, we introduce CopT, a reformulated reasoning pipeline that reverses the usual order of thinking and answering. Instead of thinking before answering, CopT first elicits a draft answer and then invokes subsequent on-policy thinking conditioned on its own draft answer for reflection and correction. To assess whether the draft answer should be trusted, CopT recasts continuous embeddings as inference-time contrastive verifiers. Specifically, it contrasts the model's support for the same generated tokens under discrete-token inputs and continuous-embedding inputs, yielding a sequence-level reverse KL estimator for answer reliability. Our analysis shows that under certain assumptions, the expected estimate equals the mutual information between the unresolved latent state and the emitted answer token, explaining why it captures answer-relevant uncertainty rather than arbitrary uncertainty in the latent state. When the answer is deemed insufficiently reliable, CopT performs further on-policy thinking, where a second KL estimator dynamically controls draft-answer visibility, preserving useful partial information while reducing the risk of being misled by unreliable content. Across mathematics, coding, and agentic reasoning tasks, CopT improves peak accuracy by up to 23% and reduces token usage by up to 57% at comparable or higher accuracy, without any additional training. The code is available at https://github.com/sdc17/CopT.

</details>


### [47] [BalanceRAG: Joint Risk Calibration for Cascaded Retrieval-Augmented Generation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.20084)
*Zijun Jia,Yuanchang Ye,Sen Jia,Yiyao Qian,Haoning Wang,Baojie Chen,Diyin Tang,Jinsong Yu,Zhiyuan Wang*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: BalanceRAG是一种级联RAG框架，通过序贯图形测试对LLM-only和RAG分支的阈值对进行联合校准，在目标风险水平下识别安全操作点，实现覆盖率与检索效率的平衡优化。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 并非所有查询都需要RAG增强，全量启用RAG造成不必要的计算开销。现有级联RAG的分阶段校准可能过于保守，因为最终效用取决于两个分支的联合不确定性阈值决策。

Method: 将LLM-only和RAG两个分支的阈值对建模为二维格点上的操作点，使用序贯图形测试(sequential graphical testing)识别安全操作点，实现目标风险水平下的阈值对认证。

Result: 在3个开放域QA基准、多种LLM backbone上的实验表明：BalanceRAG满足预设风险水平；保持更高覆盖率；接纳更多正确样本；减少不必要的检索调用。

Conclusion: BalanceRAG通过图形测试框架实现风险自适应的阈值校准，在控制接受样本错误率的同时保留更多正确样本并减少不必要的检索调用。

Abstract: Large language models (LLMs) can enhance factuality via retrieval-augmented generation (RAG), but applying RAG to every query is unnecessary when the model-only answer is reliable. This motivates cascaded RAG: each query is first handled by an LLM-only branch, escalated to a RAG fallback only if the primary branch is uncertain, and abstained from when neither branch is sufficiently trustworthy. However, calibrating such cascades stage by stage may be conservative, since the final utility depends on joint uncertainty thresholding of LLM-only and RAG. In this work, we develop BalanceRAG to certify threshold pairs at a target risk level. Given uncertainty scores from the two branches, BalanceRAG frames each threshold pair as an operating point on a two-dimensional lattice and identifies safe operating points using sequential graphical testing. This enables risk-adaptive threshold calibration, controlling the system-level error rate among accepted points, while retaining more examples. Furthermore, BalanceRAG extends to multi-risk calibration, allowing retrieval usage to be bounded together with the selection-conditioned risk. Experiments on three open-domain question answering (QA) benchmarks across multiple LLM backbones demonstrate that BalanceRAG meets prescribed risk levels, preserves higher coverage and more accepted correct examples, and reduces unnecessary retrieval calls compared with always-on RAG.

</details>


### [48] [ThoughtTrace: Understanding User Thoughts in Real-World LLM Interactions](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.20087)
*Chuanyang Jin,Binze Li,Haopeng Xie,Cathy Mengying Fang,Tianjian Li,Shayne Longpre,Hongxiang Gu,Maximillian Chen,Tianmin Shu*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: ThoughtTrace是首个将真实人机对话与用户自报告思维配对的大规模数据集，包含超百万级标注，通过验证思维作为新数据模态的价值，为构建更具理解力和适应性的AI助手奠定基础。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有数据集仅捕获用户说了什么，而不了解其背后的想法；需填补人机交互认知动态研究的空白，理解用户发送提示的原因及对助手回复的反应。

Method: 引入ThoughtTrace数据集，收集1,058位用户、2,155段对话、17,058轮交互、跨越20个语言模型的10,174条思维注释，将用户自报告的思维与真实多轮人机对话配对。

Result: ThoughtTrace成功捕获长时域、主题多样的交互；思维在语义上与消息不同，难以被前沿LLM从上下文推断，内容多样且与对话阶段相关；思维可作为推理时上下文提升用户行为预测，并为训练个性化助手提供细粒度对齐信号。

Conclusion: ThoughtTrace为研究人机交互背后的认知动态提供了新的数据模态，并作为构建更好理解并适应用户潜在目标、偏好和需求的个性化助手的基础。

Abstract: Conversational AI has now reached billions of users, yet existing datasets capture only what people say, not what they think. We introduce ThoughtTrace, the first large-scale dataset that pairs real-world multi-turn human--AI conversations with users' self-reported thoughts: their reasons for sending prompts and reactions to assistant responses. ThoughtTrace comprises 1,058 users, 2,155 conversations, 17,058 turns, and 10,174 thought annotations collected across 20 language models. Our analysis shows that ThoughtTrace captures long-horizon, topically diverse interactions, and that thoughts are semantically distinct from messages, difficult for frontier LLMs to infer from context, diverse in content, and tied to conversation stages. We further demonstrate the utility of thoughts for downstream modeling. First, thoughts improve user-behavior prediction as inference-time context. Second, thought-guided rewrites provide fine-grained alignment signals for training personalized assistants. Together, ThoughtTrace establishes user thoughts as a new data modality for studying the cognitive dynamics behind human--AI interaction and provides a foundation for building assistants that better understand and adapt to users' latent goals, preferences, and needs.

</details>


### [49] [MixRea: Benchmarking Explicit-Implicit Reasoning in Large Language Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.20128)
*Yuanqing Cai,Ziyi Huang,Minhao Liu,Lixin Duan,Wen Li,Yanru Zhang*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 这篇论文探讨了大语言模型在决策过程中是否会像人类一样出现无意盲视现象。研究团队创建了MixRea基准测试来评估模型在显性-隐性推理任务中的表现，结果显示即便是最先进的模型也存在严重的注意力缺陷。研究提出的PRCP方法能有效缓解这一问题，但该局限性在多种推理任务中的普遍性表明，我们需要开发更具认知对齐性的模型。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 受人类认知中无意盲视理论启发，研究大型语言模型是否具有类似局限：在明确任务指令下无法注意到细微但重要的上下文线索。

Method: 构建了显性-隐性推理任务和MixRea基准测试（包含2246道选择题，覆盖9种推理类型），评估了21个先进大语言模型的表现。

Result: 即使表现最佳的推理模型Gemini 2.5 Pro仅达到42.8%的一致性，揭示了大语言模型中普遍存在的无意盲视现象。

Conclusion: 提出潜力关系补全提示法（PRCP）来缓解注意力失明问题，该方法通过恢复被忽略的因果关系来改善推理能力。该局限性在多种多源推理任务中持续存在，强调了开发更具认知对齐性的模型的必要性。

Abstract: Large language models (LLMs) are increasingly integrated into high-stakes decision-making. Inspired by the theory of \emph{inattentional blindness} in human cognition, we investigate whether LLMs, trained on human-preferred corpora that embed attentional biases, exhibit a similar limitation: \emph{failing to attend to subtle yet important contextual cues under explicit task instructions}. To evaluate this, we introduce the task of \textbf{explicit-implicit reasoning} and present \textbf{MixRea}, a benchmark of 2,246 multiple-choice questions across 9 reasoning types with varying distributions of explicit and implicit information. Evaluation of 21 advanced LLMs shows that even the best-performing reasoning model (Gemini 2.5 Pro) achieves only 42.8\% consistency, revealing widespread inattentional blindness. To mitigate this, we propose \textbf{Potential Relation Completion Prompting (PRCP)}, a prompting method that improves reasoning by recovering overlooked causal relations. Further analysis shows that this limitation persists across diverse multi-source reasoning tasks, highlighting the need for more cognitively aligned models.

</details>


### [50] [Less Back-and-Forth: A Comparative Study of Structured Prompting](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.20149)
*Saurav Ghosh,Gabriella Polach,Abdou Sow*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 研究结构化提示词设计对大语言模型响应质量和用户交互效率的影响，发现检查清单式提示词效果最佳。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 开放性任务中，不完整或模糊的提示词会导致LLM回答质量下降，并增加用户交互次数，存在效率与质量之间的权衡问题。

Method: 对比三种提示词条件：原始提示词、清单优化提示词、澄清问题提示词；在四类任务（摘要、规划、解释、编程）上评估ChatGPT、Claude、Grok三个LLM系统；采用统一评分标准（任务完成度、正确性、合规性、清晰度）进行评分。

Result: 清单优化提示词平均得分最高（7.50/8），原始提示词5.67分，澄清问题提示词6.67分；清单式提示词平均使用的token数最少，在质量-努力权衡上表现最佳。

Conclusion: 简单的提示词检查清单可提升LLM响应质量并减少不必要交互，建议用户在实际应用中采用结构化提示词设计。

Abstract: Large language models (LLMs) are widely used for open-ended tasks, but underspecified prompts can lead to low-quality answers and additional interaction. This paper studies whether structured prompt design improves response quality while reducing user effort. We compare three prompt conditions: a raw prompt, a checklist-improved prompt, and a clarifying-question prompt. We evaluate these conditions across four task types--summarization, planning, explanation, and coding--using three LLM systems: ChatGPT, Claude, and Grok. Each output is scored with a unified rubric covering task completion, correctness, compliance, and clarity. Checklist-improved prompts achieved the highest mean rubric score, 7.50 out of 8, compared with 5.67 for raw prompts and 6.67 for clarifying-question prompts. Checklist prompts also produced the best quality-effort tradeoff, using fewer average tokens than both raw and clarifying prompts. These results suggest that a simple prompt checklist can improve LLM responses while reducing unnecessary interaction.

</details>


### [51] [KoRe: Compact Knowledge Representations for Large Language Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.20170)
*Davide Cavicchini,Fausto Giunchiglia,Jacopo Staiano*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 提出了KoRe方法，通过将1-hop子图编码为紧凑的离散知识令牌并注入LLM主千，实现知识图谱与LLM的高效整合，无需大规模重训练即可提升性能。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: LLMs encode world knowledge within parameters, which is opaque, hard to debug/update, and prone to hallucinations. Existing KG integration techniques require extensive retraining/finetuning.

Method: KoRe encodes 1-hop sub-graphs into compact discrete knowledge tokens and injects them into a LLM backbone, avoiding the need for extensive retraining or finetuning.

Result: Competitive performance on three established benchmarks with significant token usage reduction (up to 10x).

Conclusion: Compact discrete KG representations (via KoRe) can efficiently and effectively ground modern LLMs, achieving competitive performance with significant token reduction (up to 10x).

Abstract: Modern Large Language Models (LLMs) have shown impressive performances in user-facing tasks such as question answering, as well as consistent improvements in reasoning capabilities. Still, the way these models encode knowledge seems inherently flawed: by design, LLMs encode world-knowledge within their parameters. This way of representing knowledge is inherently opaque, difficult to debug and update, and prone to hallucinations. On the other hand, Knowledge Graphs can provide human-readable and easily editable world knowledge representations, and their application in knowledge-intensive tasks has consistently proven beneficial to downstream performance. Nonetheless, current integration techniques require extensive retraining or finetuning. To overcome this issue, we introduce KoRe, a methodology to encode 1-hop sub-graphs into compact discrete knowledge tokens and inject them into a LLM backbone. We test the proposed approach on three established benchmarks, and report competitive performances coupled with a significant reduction (up to 10x) in token usage. Our results show that compact discrete KG representations can efficiently and effectively be used to ground modern LLMs.

</details>


### [52] [From Seeing to Thinking: Decoupling Perception and Reasoning Improves Post-Training of Vision-Language Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.20177)
*Juncheng Wu,Hardy Chen,Haoqin Tu,Xianfeng Tang,Freda Shi,Hui Liu,Hanqing Lu,Cihang Xie,Yuyin Zhou*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 本研究揭示了视觉-语言模型中感知能力的重要性，证明了将视觉感知、视觉推理和文本推理分阶段训练比合并训练更有效。视觉感知更适合通过强化学习而非监督微调来学习，且应作为基础能力先于推理能力进行巩固。这一能力分级训练策略与传统的难度分级课程正交，组合使用可进一步提升模型性能。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 当前视觉-语言模型（VLM）强调长链思维推理能力，但研究发现其视觉任务表现主要受限于视觉感知能力不足，而非推理能力本身。这提示需要重新审视VLM后训练中感知与推理的训练策略。

Method: 研究采用三阶段解耦训练框架，将VLM能力分解为视觉感知、视觉推理和文本推理三个独立阶段，针对每个阶段使用专门的训练数据。对比了分阶段训练与合并训练的效果差异，以及强化学习（RL）与基于字幕的有监督微调（SFT）对视觉感知学习的效果。同时验证了能力分级训练与难度分级课程的互补性。

Result: 分阶段训练在多个VLM上一致提升视觉感知和推理性能，模型在保持1.5%更高推理精度的同时，推理痕迹缩短20.8%，表明更好的感知能力减少了对过度推理的依赖。能力分级训练与难度分级课程具有正交性，结合使用可获得额外收益。最终模型在WeMath上提升5.2%，RealWorldQA上提升3.7%，在开源VLM中达到领先水平。

Conclusion: 该研究证明了将感知与推理能力解耦、分阶段训练的有效性，为VLM后训练提供了一种新的范式。视觉感知不仅是基础能力，其训练方式（RL vs SFT）和训练顺序对模型最终性能有显著影响。这一发现为未来VLM训练策略的设计提供了重要指导。

Abstract: Recent advances in vision-language models (VLMs) emphasize long chain-of-thought reasoning; yet, we find that their performance on visual tasks is primarily limited by a lack of visual perception as opposed to reasoning itself. In this work, we systematically study the interplay between perception and reasoning in VLM post-training by decomposing their capabilities into three separate training stages: visual perception, visual reasoning, and textual reasoning, incorporating specialized training data. We demonstrate that visual perception (a) requires targeted optimization with specialized data; (b) serves as a fundamental scaffold that should be solidified through staged training before refining visual reasoning; and (c) is more effectively learned via RL than caption-based SFT. Our experiments across multiple VLMs demonstrate that staged training consistently improves both visual perception and reasoning performance over merged training. Notably, models trained with our approach achieve 1.5% higher reasoning accuracy with 20.8% shorter reasoning traces, suggesting that superior perception reduces the need for excessive reasoning. Furthermore, we show that this capability-based staging represents a new curriculum dimension orthogonal to traditional difficulty-based curricula, and combining both yields further additive gains. Our staged-training models achieve superior performance among open-weight VLMs, establishing advanced results on several visual math and perception (e.g., +5.2% on WeMath and +3.7% on RealWorldQA) tasks compared with the base counterpart.

</details>


### [53] [TIDE: Efficient and Lossless MoE Diffusion LLM Inference with I/O-aware Expert Offload](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.20179)
*Zhiben Chen,Youpeng Zhao,Yang Sui,Jun Wang,Yuzhang Shang*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: TIDE利用扩散过程中专家激活的时间稳定性，通过I/O感知的区间专家刷新策略和数学规划优化，实现无训练损失的dLLM推理加速。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: As dLLMs scale up with MoE architectures, deployment on resource-constrained devices remains challenging. Existing AR-based methods incur either prohibitive I/O overhead or significant compute bottlenecks, creating a need for efficient inference systems for diffusion-based models.

Method: TIDE introduces an interval-based expert refresh strategy that updates expert placement in an I/O-aware fashion, and formulates inference scheduling as a mathematical programming problem to solve for the optimal interval that minimizes I/O traffic and CPU computation.

Result: In single GPU-CPU systems, TIDE achieves up to 1.4× and 1.5× throughput improvements over prior baselines on LLaDA2.0-mini and LLaDA2.0-flash models, respectively.

Conclusion: TIDE provides a "free lunch" acceleration for dLLM inference without any model training, making large-scale dLLMs more accessible for deployment on resource-constrained devices.

Abstract: Diffusion Large Language Models (dLLMs) have emerged as a competitive alternative to autoregressive (AR) models, offering better hardware utilization and bidirectional context through parallel block-level decoding. However, as dLLMs continue to scale up with mixture-of-experts (MoE) architectures, their deployment on resource-constrained devices remains an open challenge. Existing AR-based methods often incur either prohibitive I/O overhead or significant compute bottlenecks. In this work, we propose TIDE, a novel resource-efficient inference system that leverages the temporal stability of expert activations during the diffusion process within the block. Specifically, we leverage the temporal stability of expert activations during the diffusion process within the block and introduce an interval-based expert refresh strategy that updates the expert placement in an I/O-aware fashion. To ensure optimal performance, we formulate the inference scheduling as a mathematical programming problem, solving for the optimal interval that minimizes I/O traffic and CPU computation. Most importantly, TIDE is a lossless optimization that requires no model training, providing a "free lunch" acceleration for dLLM inference. In a single GPU-CPU system, we demonstrate that TIDE achieves up to 1.4$\times$ and 1.5$\times$ throughput improvements over prior baselines on LLaDA2.0-mini and LLaDA2.0-flash models, respectively.

</details>


### [54] [IMLJD: A Computational Dataset for Indian Matrimonial Litigation Analysis](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.19346)
*Joy Bose*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: IMLJD是一个包含3,613份印度法院判决书的数据集，涵盖2000-2024年最高法院和2018-2024年卡纳塔克邦高等法院的婚姻纠纷案件。数据集提供结构化标签、元数据和知识图谱，旨在支持法律NLP研究。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 法律大数据在司法决策研究中的应用日益重要，但针对印度婚姻纠纷领域的标注数据集较为匮乏。研究者需要大规模、结构化的法律判决数据来支持实证分析和机器学习模型开发。

Method: 数据集收集了印度最高法院和卡纳塔克邦高等法院的婚姻相关判决，包括IPC第498A条、《家庭暴力保护法》和《刑事诉讼法》第482条案件。研究者构建了结构化结果标签、元数据指标和知识图谱，并采用倾向得分匹配方法进行时期调整分析。

Result: 研究发现最高法院驳回申诉成功率为57.6%，卡纳塔克邦高等法院为39.7%，差距为17.9个百分点。在2018-2024年匹配期间，差距扩大至19.6个百分点，表明最高法院对原告更为有利。

Conclusion: IMLJD数据集为印度法律NLP研究提供了重要资源，揭示了两级法院在婚姻纠纷案件处理上的显著差异，有助于理解司法实践中的系统性因素。

Abstract: We present IMLJD, an open dataset of 3,613 Indian court judgments covering matrimonial disputes under IPC Section 498A, the Protection of Women from Domestic Violence Act, and CrPC Section 482. The dataset covers the Supreme Court of India from 2000 to 2024 (1,474 cases) and the Karnataka High Court from 2018 to 2024 (2,139 cases), with structured outcome labels, metadata-derived indicators, and a knowledge graph. We find that 57.6% of quashing petitions succeed at the Supreme Court level compared to 39.7% at the Karnataka High Court level. On a matched 2018 to 2024 period, the SC quash rate is 59.3%, widening the differential to 19.6 percentage points and confirming the finding is robust to temporal adjustment. The dataset, code, and knowledge graph are released openly at https://github.com/joyboseroy/imljd and https://huggingface.co/datasets/joyboseroy/imljd.

</details>


<div id='cs.MA'></div>

### [55] [CASPIAN: Online Detection and Attribution of Cascade Attacks in LLM Multi-Agent Systems via Cross-Channel Causal Monitoring](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.19240)
*Kavana Venkatesh,Jafar Isbarov,Saad Amin,Murat Kantarcioglu,Jiaming Cui*

Main category: cs.MA

TL;DR: CASPIAN是首个针对LLM多智能体系统中级联攻击的统一跨通道因果分析框架，通过在线监测动态影响传播来检测级联发生。核心方法采用后交互条件转移熵(LI-CTE)估计动态因果影响矩阵，能从涌现的系统级结构而非孤立异常中识别级联 onset。进一步支持在线因果归因，可识别级联起源、桥接和放大智能体，并重建主要传播路径。在多个多智能体框架和基准测试中一致优于语义护栏、LLM判断器和基于图的异常检测器，同时保持亚1%相对延迟开销。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: LLM多智能体系统中的级联攻击具有信号分散、跨通道紧密耦合、局部看似良性但会快速展开（单轮或多轮）的特点。现有防御方法多为局部的、仅关注文本，无法捕捉跨通道、时间协调的级联传播动态。

Method: 1) 建立跨通道统一动态因果影响矩阵建模多智能体交互；2) 采用后交互条件转移熵(LI-CTE)高效估计该矩阵；3) 通过监测涌现的系统级结构变化检测级联 onset；4) 实现在线因果归因，识别origin、bridge、amplifier智能体。

Result: CASPIAN在检测准确性和早期级联识别方面一致优于语义护栏、LLM判断器和基于图的异常检测器；在多种多智能体框架和基准测试上表现稳定；计算开销低于1%相对延迟。

Conclusion: 统一跨通道因果建模对于可靠检测和理解LLM多智能体系统中的级联故障至关重要。该工作为LLM-MAS安全提供了首个可解释的级联检测与溯源方案。

Abstract: Cascade attacks in LLM multi-agent systems (MAS) arise when adversarial influence propagates across agents and leads to escalated system-level failures through complex agent interactions. Detecting such cascades is challenging, as their signals are distributed, tightly coupled across interaction channels, and often appear plausibly benign locally but may unfold quickly either within a single turn or gradually across multiple turns. Existing defenses, being largely local and text-centric, fail to capture such cross-channel, temporally coordinated dynamics of cascade propagation. Therefore, we propose CASPIAN, the first framework that provides a unified, cross-channel causal analysis of cascade behavior in LLM-MAS through online monitoring of dynamic influence propagation across agents. CASPIAN models multi-agent interactions using a unified, dynamic causal influence matrix across channels, estimated efficiently via a late-interaction conditional transfer entropy (LI-CTE) formulation, thereby enabling the detection of cascade onset from emergent system-level structure rather than isolated anomalies. It further performs online causal attribution, identifying the origin, bridge, and amplifier agents driving the cascade and reconstructing its principal propagation pathways, capabilities not supported by existing methods. Across diverse multi-agent frameworks and benchmarks, CASPIAN consistently outperforms semantic guardrails, LLM-based judges, and graph-based anomaly detectors in both detection accuracy and early cascade identification while operating with sub-1% relative overhead latency. These results demonstrate that unified cross-channel causal modeling is essential for reliably detecting and understanding cascade failures in LLM multi-agent systems.

</details>


### [56] [STAR-PólyaMath: Multi-Agent Reasoning under Persistent Meta-Strategic Supervision](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.19338)
*Jiaao Wu,Xian Zhang,Hanzhang Liu,Sophia Zhang,Fan Yang,Yinpeng Dong*

Main category: cs.MA

TL;DR: STAR-PólyaMath introduces a multi-agent framework for mathematical reasoning that uses meta-level supervision and a persistent Meta-Strategist to address hallucination, memory fragmentation, and reasoning-tool imbalances. It achieves state-of-the-art results on 8 major competition benchmarks, including perfect scores on several benchmarks, with ablation studies confirming that orchestration design—not model diversity—is the primary driver of performance gains.


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: Existing frontier AI models and multi-agent systems suffer from fundamental reliability issues for long-horizon mathematical reasoning: hallucination accumulation, memory fragmentation, and imbalanced reasoning-tool trade-offs.

Method: STAR-PólyaMath is a multi-agent framework structured as an orchestrated state machine with nested challenge-step-replan loops. It uses a reasoning-free Python orchestrator that separates control from inference and bounds error propagation through trace-back and re-planning. A persistent Meta-Strategist maintains cross-attempt memory and exercises meta-level control via high-level strategic guidance or mandatory directives to escape unproductive loops.

Result: Achieves SOTA on all eight tested competition benchmarks. Gets perfect scores on AIMEs, Putnam, and HMMT. Shows largest margin on Apex 2025, scoring 93.75% vs 80.21% by GPT-5.5 baseline.

Conclusion: Ablation studies confirm performance gains derive from framework orchestration rather than model-level diversity—removing key components or using mixed backbones consistently degrades results. Code available at https://github.com/Julius-Woo/STAR-PolyaMath.

Abstract: Frontier AI models and multi-agent systems have led to significant improvements in mathematical reasoning. However, for problems requiring extended, long-horizon reasoning, existing systems continue to suffer from fundamental reliability issues: hallucination accumulation, memory fragmentation, and imbalanced reasoning-tool trade-offs. In this paper, we introduce STAR-PólyaMath, a multi-agent framework that systematically addresses these challenges through meta-level supervision and structured Reasoner-Verifier interaction. STAR-PólyaMath is structured as an orchestrated state machine with nested challenge-step-replan loops, governed by a reasoning-free Python orchestrator that separates control from inference and bounds error propagation through trace-back and re-planning. Our key innovation is a persistent Meta-Strategist that maintains cross-attempt memory and exercises meta-level control by issuing high-level strategic guidance or mandatory directives, so the system can escape unproductive loops rather than stagnate or over-rely on tools. STAR-PólyaMath achieves state-of-the-art results on all eight top-tier competition benchmarks: AIME 2025-2026, MathArena Apex Shortlist, MathArena Apex 2025, Putnam 2025, IMO 2025, HMMT February 2026, and USAMO 2026. It obtains perfect scores on AIMEs, Putnam, and HMMT, and shows its largest margin on Apex 2025, scoring 93.75% compared with 80.21% by the strongest baseline GPT-5.5. Ablation studies show that the gains arise from the framework's orchestration rather than from model-level diversity since removing key components or substituting in mixed backbones consistently weakens performance. Code is available at https://github.com/Julius-Woo/STAR-PolyaMath.

</details>


### [57] [PAVE: A Cognitive Architecture for Legitimate Violation in Generative Agent Societies](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.19351)
*Ahmad Yehia,Abduallah Mohamed,Kun Qian,Tianyi Wang,Jiseop Byeon,Omar Hassanin,Christian Claudel*

Main category: cs.MA

TL;DR: 本文提出PAVE认知架构，使大语言模型智能体能够在紧急情境中进行合理的规则违反决策。通过感知、评估、裁决、执行四模块设计，结合硬性合法性门控机制，确保智能体仅在必要时且符合比例原则的情况下违规，同时保持对权威的服从、限制违规范围，并在触发条件消除后恢复正常行为。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 当前生成式智能体在合作场景中能复现人类行为，但在紧急情境（如火灾疏散或权威监督的应急场景）中如何处理"可能需要违反规则"的推理问题仍未被充分研究。

Method: 提出PAVE框架，包含四个模块：①感知模块提取结构化情境（权威距离、同伴行为、严重程度标记）；②评估模块沿五个标量评分，含合法性判断；③裁决模块通过硬性合法性门控决定遵从或违规；④执行模块实施裁决并限定违规范围。在Voville（基于Smallville的交通环境）中实例化，跨三个场景、四个LLM backbone进行评估，并进行针对性消融实验。

Result: PAVE智能体同时满足四项属性：合法违规（仅在触发条件正当时）、权威服从（官员指令优先）、有限范围（违规限于目标规则）、恢复能力（触发结束后恢复基线）。在四项属性上均优于基线方法，人类评估者认为其决策更合理。

Conclusion: PAVE agents achieve more structured, interpretable decisions than vanilla approaches across all four properties, with human evaluators rating them as more plausible. Ablating the legitimacy gate reproduces vanilla-like failures, confirming the importance of this gate.

Abstract: Generative agents based on large language models reproduce believable human behavior in cooperative settings, but how they should reason in situations where rule-breaking may be required, such as fire evacuation or authority-supervised emergency, remains poorly characterized. We propose PAVE (Perception, Assessment, Verdict, Emulation), a novel four-module cognitive architecture that addresses this gap end to end: (i) Perception extracts a structured context with explicit authority distance, peer behaviors, and severity-tagged situational cues; (ii) Assessment scores the context along five scalars including an explicit legitimacy judgment that checks necessity, proportionality, and absence of alternatives; (iii) Verdict decides to comply or violate under a hard legitimacy gate, with a per-agent threshold elicited from the persona; (iv) Emulation enacts the verdict and scopes the violation to the rule the trigger justifies. We instantiate PAVE in Voville, a tile-based traffic environment forked from Smallville, and evaluate across three scenarios, four LLM backbones, and a focused ablation. PAVE agents satisfy four properties simultaneously: legitimate violation (only when a trigger justifies it), authority deference (officer instructions override even high legitimacy), bounded scope (violations confined to the targeted rule), and recovery (baseline restored once the trigger ends). PAVE agents make more structured and interpretable decisions than vanilla across all four properties, and human evaluators rate them as more plausible. Ablating the legitimacy gate reproduces vanilla-like failures. We release Voville, the PAVE prompts and code, and the evaluation pipeline.

</details>
