### [58] [Operational Memory Architecture for Kubernetes:Preserving Causal Context Across the Evidence Horizon](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.18755)
*Shamsher Khan*

Main category: cs.DC

TL;DR: 本文提出操作内存架构(OMA)来解决Kubernetes事件保留问题。该架构通过Go监控器、SQLite存储和查询接口，在证据消失前(证据视界)捕获因果链。实验表明因果边构建延迟低于1ms，处理吞吐量约2.8事件/秒，内存占用不足10MB。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: Kubernetes' native event retention discards diagnostically valuable context, particularly the LastTerminationState field which records container failures. During high-frequency crash loops, the "evidence horizon" may be crossed multiple times before inspection, permanently losing critical failure evidence needed for root cause analysis.

Method: Implementation uses Go-based Kubernetes watcher for event collection, SQLite for operational memory storage, with query interface for causal reconstruction. Three causal chain patterns are defined: P001 (OOMKill chain), P002 (ConfigMap variable misconfiguration), P003 (ConfigMap volume mount propagation). Evaluation conducted on Minikube and AKS with 30-run latency benchmarks and stress tests up to 20 crash-looping pods.

Result: Mean latency for causal edge construction below 1 ms. Event processing rate of ~2.8 events/sec. Memory footprint under 10 MB. System demonstrates minimal overhead while effectively preserving evidence across crash loop scenarios.

Conclusion: OMA provides practical open-source solution for evidence preservation in Kubernetes with minimal overhead. Three pattern types (OOMKill chain, ConfigMap variable misconfiguration, ConfigMap volume mount propagation) address common failure modes. System achieves sub-millisecond causal edge construction with low resource footprint, making it viable for production environments.

Abstract: Kubernetes clusters generate rich operational events during pod lifecycle transitions, yet the platform's native event retention model discards the most diagnostically valuable context. The LastTerminationState field, which records a container's last failure, is overwritten shortly after a pod restart. We define this as the evidence horizon. During high-frequency crash loops, this horizon may be crossed multiple times before inspection, permanently losing critical evidence.
  This paper introduces the Operational Memory Architecture (OMA) to preserve causal failure evidence before event rotation. OMA encodes evidence retention and causal reconstruction as explicit architectural requirements. It captures operational events into causal chains using three patterns: P001 (OOMKill chain), P002 (ConfigMap variable misconfiguration), and P003 (ConfigMap volume mount propagation).
  We implement OMA as an open-source system with a Go-based Kubernetes watcher, SQLite operational memory store, and a simple query interface. Experiments on Minikube and AKS include a 30-run latency analysis and stress tests with up to 20 crash-looping pods. Causal edges are built with mean latency below 1 ms. The collector processes ~2.8 events/sec while using under 10 MB memory, showing minimal overhead and effective evidence preservation.

</details>


### [59] [Unleashing the Power of Tree-of-Thoughts for Edge-Enabled AIGC Service Provisioning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.19108)
*Zhang Liu,Shanhao Zhan,Shaowei Shen,Lianfen Huang,Qiao Xiang,Ying-Jun Angela Zhang,Dusit Niyato*

Main category: cs.DC

TL;DR: 本文研究移动边缘计算环境下使用ToT提示的AIGC服务部署问题。针对ToT推理中多路径生成带来的计算资源挑战，通过DAG模型表征推理过程，并以用户可调质量约束下的延迟最小化为目标，提出结合扩散模型的DSAC算法进行最优thought分配决策。实验表明该方法在保证质量的前提下显著降低生成延迟。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: ToT提示通过同时探索多条候选推理路径来提升AIGC服务质量，但生成多样化推理路径需要多次调用计算密集型GenAI模型，在资源受限的用户设备上面临重大挑战。

Method: 1. 建立输出token数量与生成延迟及质量的数学关系（基于Qwen 2.5-7B-Instruct实验）
2. 提出DAG模型表征ToT推理过程（顶点为thought，边为连续thought间的转换）
3. 最小化生成延迟的DAG-based thought assignment问题（受用户可调质量约束）
4. 提出DSAC算法，将扩散模型与软Actor-Critic算法创新性结合以确定最优thought分配决策

Result: 1. 延迟降低：DSAC相比PPO降低8.32%、SAC降低11.57%、DDQN降低36.09%
2. 效率提升：在严格质量要求下相比完全本地生成基线延迟降低超过80%
3. 验证了在移动边缘计算架构下通过智能资源分配实现AIGC服务优化的可行性

Conclusion: 所提出的DSAC算法在生成延迟方面相比PPO、SAC和DDQN分别实现了8.32%、11.57%和36.09%的降低，在严格质量要求下相比完全本地生成基线降低超过80%的延迟。

关键创新点：
- 引入DAG模型精确表征ToT推理过程
- 创新性地将扩散模型与SAC算法结合用于决策优化
- 在资源受限的移动设备上实现高质量低延迟的AIGC服务

Abstract: Delivering AI-generated content (AIGC) services fundamentally relies on the reasoning capabilities of generative AI (GenAI) models. Chain-of-Thought (CoT) enhances such reasoning by guiding models through intermediate steps, while Tree-of-Thoughts (ToT) further extends CoT by exploring multiple candidate reasoning paths simultaneously, thereby greatly improving AIGC service quality. However, generating diverse reasoning paths requires separate calls to computationally intensive GenAI models, posing significant challenges for resource constrained user devices. In this paper, we investigate mobile edge computing-enabled AIGC service provisioning with ToT prompting. Specifically, using creative writing AIGC tasks as a case study, we first characterize the number of output tokens as a measure of computational resources in GenAI models and establish its relationship with generation delay and quality through experiments with Qwen 2.5-7B-Instruct. Afterward, we introduce a directed acyclic graph (DAG) model to accurately characterize the reasoning process of ToT prompting, where each vertex represents a thought and each directed edge denotes a transition between consecutive thoughts. We then formulate a DAG-based thought assignment problem aimed at minimizing generation delay subject to a user-adjustable quality constraint. To address this problem, we propose a diffusion-based soft actor-critic (DSAC) algorithm that innovatively integrates diffusion models to determine optimal thought assignment decisions. Through extensive simulations, we demonstrate that the proposed DSAC achieves total generation delay reductions of up to 8.32% over PPO, 11.57% over SAC, and 36.09% over DDQN across various simulation settings, while reducing latency by over 80% compared to the fully local generation baseline even under stringent quality requirements.

</details>


### [60] [Meta-Theorems for Cuttable Distributed Problems](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.19157)
*Marthe Bonamy,Avinandan Das,Cyril Gavoille,Timothé Picavet,Jukka Suomela,Alexandra Wesolek*

Main category: cs.DC

TL;DR: 将平面图上的常数轮近似LOCAL算法推广到有界欧拉亏格图和其他更一般的图类。通过定义"可切割"最小化问题，给出将α-近似算法转换为(3α+1)-近似算法的通用框架，将MDS的近似比从24g+O(1)改进到(34+ε)，并将结果扩展到其他问题和图类。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 平面图上已有较好的近似算法，但有界欧拉亏格图上的现有结果（24g+O(1)和91+ε）仍需改进。

Method: 通过一系列关于"可切割"最小化问题的元定理，将平面图上的常数轮近似LOCAL算法系统化地转换为有界亏格图上的算法。核心思想是从图中系统地提取小子图，其局部解与全局解在邻域中的限制成比例。

Result: 将α-近似平面图算法转换为f(g)轮的(3α+1)-近似算法；对MDS给出(34+ε)-近似（有界亏格图）；对最小k元支配集等其他问题同样适用；推广到"局部好"图类。

Conclusion: 通过将平面图上的结果推广到有界欧拉亏格图及更一般的"局部好"图类，显著改进了有界亏格图的近似比，并给出了处理其他组合优化问题的通用框架。

Abstract: We prove that given any $α$-approximation LOCAL algorithm for Minimum Dominating Set (MDS) on planar graphs, we can construct an $f(g)$-round $(3α+1)$-approximation LOCAL algorithm for MDS on graphs embeddable in a given Euler genus-$g$ surface. Heydt et al. [European Journal of Combinatorics (2025)] gave an algorithm with $α=11+\varepsilon$, from which we derive a $(34 +\varepsilon)$-approximation algorithm for graphs of genus $g$, therefore improving upon the current state of the art of $24g+O(1)$ due to Amiri et al. [ACM Transactions on Algorithms (2019)]. It also improves the approximation ratio of $91+\varepsilon$ due to Czygrinow et al. [Theoretical Computer Science (2019)] in the particular case of orientable surfaces.
  We generalize this result into two directions: (1) by considering other graph problems studied in Distributed Computing such as Minimum $k$-Tuple Dominating Set, for which constant-round approximation algorithms were known for planar graphs, but not for graphs of bounded genus; and (2) by considering graph classes beyond bounded genus graphs, called locally nice, and relying on the asymptotic dimension of the class. We prove these results by a series of meta-theorems about cuttable minimization problems with constant-round approximation LOCAL algorithms. Roughly speaking, in cuttable problems, one can systematically extract small subgraphs whose solutions are in proportion to the global solution restricted to the neighbourhood of the subgraph.

</details>


### [61] [Generalized Compare-and-Swap and Space-Efficient Universal Constructions for the Infinite-Arrival Model](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.19237)
*Vassos Hadzilacos,Myles Thiessen,Sam Toueg*

Main category: cs.DC

TL;DR: 本文提出了GCAS（广义比较并交换），将传统CAS的固定相等测试推广为参数化的比较器（<, =, >），并基于此提出了两个空间高效的wait-free通用构造，适用于进程数未知或无限的场景。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有的wait-free通用构造在无限到达模型（infinite-arrival model）下空间复杂度较高。本文旨在通过引入GCAS这一新的同步原语，设计空间复杂度更优的wait-free通用构造。

Method: 1. 提出GCAS作为CAS的自然推广；2. 利用GCAS设计两个通用构造：第一个基于参与进程数的线性空间复杂度；第二个基于点争用（point contention）的线性空间复杂度，需假设有界并发。

Result: 两个wait-free通用构造均达到线性空间复杂度。据作者所知，这是首次在无限到达模型下实现此类空间复杂度的wait-free通用构造。

Conclusion: 第二个构造中的内存回收方案可推广应用于其他场景，具有更广泛的通用价值。

Abstract: We introduce GCAS, a natural generalization of the well-known compare-and-swap (CAS) object. Intuitively, GCAS just replaces the fixed equality test of CAS with a parametrized comparator chosen from $\{<, =, >\}$. To showcase the utility of GCAS, we present two space-efficient wait-free universal constructions for systems where the number of participating processes is unknown and may be infinite (the infinite-arrival model). The first has space-complexity linear in the number of processes that have participated so far, while the second has space-complexity linear in the point contention but assumes bounded concurrency. To the best of our knowledge, these are the first wait-free universal constructions that achieve this space complexity in the infinite-arrival model. To achieve space complexity linear in the point contention, our second universal construction uses a novel memory recycling scheme that works in the infinite-arrival model with bounded concurrency. The ideas behind this recycling scheme could be of more general use.

</details>


### [62] [LatentBox: An Efficient Latent-First Storage System for AI-Generated Images](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.19385)
*Zirui Wang,Yunjia Zheng,Tingfeng Lan,Zhaoyuan Su,Haoran Ni,Juncheng Yang,Yue Cheng*

Main category: cs.DC

TL;DR: LatentBox introduces a latent-first storage system for AI-generated images, storing compressed latent tensors instead of full-resolution pixels and using on-demand GPU reconstruction. Evaluated on a 35-month, 2-billion-request production trace, it achieves 78.7% storage reduction with competitive latency.


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: The exponential growth of AI-generated images on platforms like Midjourney and Adobe Firefly creates severe storage sustainability challenges. Conventional blob storage persists full-resolution pixel data, consuming massive storage and bandwidth resources. This inefficiency is particularly problematic given that AI-generated images can be deterministically reconstructed from compact latent representations, making persistent pixel storage fundamentally redundant.

Method: LatentBox employs a latent-first storage architecture that stores compressed latent tensors as durable objects and uses on-demand GPU reconstruction for read operations. The design incorporates an adaptive two-tier caching strategy that keeps frequently accessed images in pixel format while storing less active content as compressed latents, with continuous runtime adjustment of cache tier splits based on access patterns. The system was prototyped and evaluated using the same 35-month production trace.

Result: LatentBox achieves 78.7% reduction in persistent storage requirements while maintaining competitive or improved mean and tail latency compared to traditional image-based storage systems.

Conclusion: LatentBox demonstrates an effective approach to reducing persistent storage requirements for AI-generated images while maintaining competitive latency. By treating compressed latents as first-class storage objects and using adaptive caching strategies based on real production workload patterns, the system achieves a practical balance between storage efficiency and access performance.

Abstract: The explosive growth of AI-generated images has created a sustainability challenge for storage infrastructure. Platforms like Midjourney and Adobe Firefly already host billions of generative images, yet conventional object stores persist them as blobs with full-resolution pixels, consuming huge amounts of storage capacity and bandwidth. Unlike natural photos, however, AI-generated images can be deterministically reconstructed from compact, model-native latent tensors, making persistent image storage fundamentally redundant.
  This paper presents LatentBox, a latent-first storage system for AI-generated images. LatentBox treats compressed latents as durable storage objects and uses on-demand GPU reconstruction on the read path to trade inexpensive compute for large persistent storage savings. Our design is guided by the first large-scale analysis of AI-generated image access we are aware of, based on a 35-month, 2-billion-request production trace from a major generative-content platform. Motivated by the trace analysis, LatentBox keeps frequently accessed images in decoded pixel format for fast hits, stores less-active objects as compressed latents to expand effective cache capacity, and continuously adjusts the splits between the image and latent cache to optimize user-perceived access latency.We build a LatentBox prototype and evaluate it with the production trace. LatentBox reduces persistent storage by 78.7% with competitive or even lower mean and tail latency over a pure image-based storage.

</details>


### [63] [Resilient Byzantine Agreement with Predictions](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.19452)
*Julien Dallot,Darya Melnyk,Tijana Milentijevic,Stefan Schmid,Patrik Welters*

Main category: cs.DC

TL;DR: 该论文研究了带预测器的拜占庭协议问题，完全刻画了一致性与鲁棒性的权衡关系，并证明算法韧性随预测误差呈线性退化（认证场景退化率减半）。所有结果均为紧界。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 传统拜占庭协议要求容忍不超过1/3的恶意节点，存在过度保守的问题。若引入能标记可疑节点的预测器，理论上可突破这一限制，但预测器的准确性会动态影响算法的容错能力，需要建立预测精度与算法韧性之间的精确权衡关系。

Method: 通过构建最坏情况下的构造性算法和证明下界，分析了预测器正确/错误两种极端情况以及连续退化过程中的韧性变化。具体分析了三类参数：容忍故障节点数的一致性保证（预测器正确时α·n）、鲁棒性保证（预测器任意错误时）、以及平滑性（每单位预测误差对韧性的边际损失）。

Result: 得出三个核心结论：(1) 一致性-鲁棒性权衡：预测器正确时可容忍α·n个故障节点；预测器完全错误时，非认证场景下为(1-α)·n/2 - 1，认证场景下为(1-α)·n - 1；(2) 权衡上界紧致：故障节点数再多1个即不可解；(3) 平滑性分析：非认证场景每1次错误预测损失1单位韧性，认证场景需2次错误预测才损失1单位韧性，线性退化规律在错误数不超过常数比例n时成立。

Conclusion: 该研究给出了拜占庭协议中预测器辅助的完整理论框架，证明了算法的韧性严格依赖于预测精度，且在认证/非认证场景下呈现不同的线性退化特征。这些结果为设计实用化容错协议提供了精确的边界条件。

Abstract: This paper studies the Byzantine Agreement problem where the nodes have access to a predictor that flags nodes for suspicion of faulty (Byzantine) behavior. We focus on algorithmic resilience -- the maximum number of faulty nodes an algorithm can tolerate -- and present algorithms and impossibility results whose resilience depend on the accuracy of the predictor. As our first main result, we bring a complete characterization of the consistency--robustness trade-offs in both the non-authenticated and authenticated settings: for $n$ nodes and a parameter $α\in [0, 1]$, we present algorithms that tolerate up to $α\cdot n$ faulty nodes when the predictor is correct (consistency), and up to $\frac{1-α}{2} \cdot n - 1$ faulty nodes when the predictor is arbitrarily wrong (robustness); in the authenticated setting the robustness bound improves to $(1-α) \cdot n - 1$. These trade-offs are exactly tight as we show that one additional faulty node renders the problem impossible. Our second main result characterizes smoothness: the rate at which resilience degrades as the predictor becomes less accurate. We show that resilience linearly decreases in the number of wrong predictions as long as that number stays within a constant fraction of $n$. Concretely, in the non-authenticated setting each additional wrong prediction loses one unit of resilience, whereas in the authenticated setting the decline is halved since two wrong predictions are needed to lose one unit of resilience.

</details>


### [64] [A parallel wakeup problem and multi-room light switch strategies](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.19488)
*John Haslegrave,Paul A. Russell,Mark Walters*

Main category: cs.DC

TL;DR: 本文研究了分布式计算中唤醒问题的对称协议设计。通过分析最小开关状态需求和对称场景下的可解性条件，解决了Kane和Kominers提出的开放问题，并精确刻画了在何种处理器数量和寄存器数量组合下问题可解。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 分布式计算中的唤醒问题要求设计对称协议，使处理器能够在没有全局时钟的情况下，仅通过共享寄存器确保所有其他处理器已采取行动。Kane和Kominers研究了多房间版本的更通用形式，并提出了关于囚犯问题中所需最小开关状态的开放问题。

Method: 通过分析共享寄存器的切换状态数量，结合对称协议设计，证明了对称唤醒场景的可解性条件。

Result: 回答了Kane和Kominers关于囚犯解决问题所需最小开关状态的开放问题；完全确定了在给定的处理器数量和寄存器数量下，对称唤醒版本问题是否可解。

Conclusion: 完全确定了在哪些处理器数量和寄存器数量的组合下，问题可解。解决了Kane和Kominers提出的关于最小开关状态数量的开放问题。证明了在对称唤醒场景下，问题可解的精确条件。

Abstract: The wakeup problem in distributed computing asks for a symmetric protocol that enables one of several processors to eventually guarantee that all (or, in a more general setting, enough) other processors have acted, using a shared register but no global clock. Dropping the symmetry requirement gives a well-known exercise often phrased in terms of prisoners entering, in an unknown sequence, a room equipped with a single binary switch, and using it to communicate. Kane and Kominers recently analysed a more general version of the latter with multiple parallel and indistinguishable rooms. We answer some open questions of Kane and Kominers regarding the minimum number of switch states needed for the prisoners to solve the problem. We also consider the symmetric ``wakeup'' version of this scenario, and establish exactly for which numbers of processors and registers a solution is possible.

</details>


### [65] [Conflict-Freedom as a Progress Condition](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.19531)
*Petr Kuznetsov,Pierre Sutra,Guillermo Toyos-Marfurt*

Main category: cs.DC

TL;DR: 本文提出冲突自由度作为阻塞自由度的新泛化，该条件只要求不完整的冲突操作阻止进度，而非所有并发操作。文章证明任何顺序对象都可以通过基于新型提交-采纳对象的通用构造实现读-写冲突自由线性化。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 传统阻塞自由的定义过于保守，实际上并发操作的存在本身并不一定会阻止进度，只有不完整的冲突（非交换）操作才会阻止进度。现有阻塞自由条件无法充分利用对象语义可交换性带来的效率提升潜力。

Method: 基于新型通用提交-采纳（commit-adopt）对象构建冲突自由线性化实现。该方法扩展了传统提交-采纳对象的概念，使其支持读-写冲突自由的实现。

Result: 证明了每个顺序对象都存在读-写冲突自由线性化实现，且该构造基于新提出的通用提交-采纳对象。

Conclusion: 提出了冲突自由作为阻塞自由的自然泛化，通过基于新型提交-采纳对象的通用构造证明了任何顺序对象都可以实现读写冲突自由线性化。该研究为具有语义可交换性特征的对象提供了高效的并发实现方案。提出了冲突自由作为阻塞自由的自然泛化，通过基于新型提交-采纳对象的通用构造证明了任何顺序对象都可以实现读写冲突自由线性化。该研究为具有语义可交换性特征的对象提供了高效的并发实现方案。

Abstract: An obstruction-free implementation guarantees progress to every operation that is given enough time to take steps in isolation. But, as we show in this paper, the mere presence of concurrent operations alone does not have to prevent progress; only incomplete conflicting (non-commuting) operations may do so. This progress condition, that we call conflict-freedom, is a natural generalization of obstruction-freedom that promises efficient implementations for objects exhibiting semantic commutativity. We show that, as with obstruction-freedom, every sequential object has a read-write conflict-free linearizable implementation. Our conflict-free universal construction is based on a novel generalization of the instrumental commit-adopt object, interesting in its own right.

</details>


### [66] [Understanding Inference Scaling for LLMs: Bottlenecks, Trade-offs, and Performance Principles](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.19775)
*Moiz Arif,Avinash Maurya,Sudharshan Vazhkudai,Bogdan Nicolae*

Main category: cs.DC

TL;DR: 本文系统分析了从标准生成式AI到推理中心架构的范式转变，通过对8B至671B参数模型的评估，揭示了数据并行、Tensor并行和Pipeline并行在推理工作负载下的关键瓶颈，发现KV-cache碎片化导致容量陷阱，MoE模型与稠密模型具有不同的并行策略优化方向。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 随着CoT推理模型生成大量推理token，系统从compute-bound转向capacity-bound regime，标准扩展策略失效，现有系统设计无法有效支撑长链推理工作负载，亟需新的架构决策框架。

Method: 在GPU集群上对8B至671B参数模型进行系统性评估，探究Data、Tensor、Pipeline三种并行策略的交互作用，识别各规模模型的关键瓶颈。

Result: 数据并行对小模型吞吐高效，但推理工作负载中KV-cache碎片化导致容量陷阱和计算利用率不足；Tensor并行可释放 stranded memory，32B参数规模附近收益递减；稠密模型（如Llama-405B）受限于互联带宽和内存带宽，偏好高维度TP；稀疏MoE模型（如DeepSeek-R1）受路由和同步延迟限制，适合混合策略。

Conclusion: 推理工作负载需要重新审视并行策略选择标准，KV-cache管理是关键瓶颈，稠密与稀疏模型需差异化优化策略，为下一代推理基础设施提供系统性决策框架。

Abstract: The transition from standard generative AI to \emph{reasoning-centric architectures}, exemplified by models capable of extensive Chain-of-Thought~(CoT) processing, marks a fundamental paradigm shift in system requirements. Unlike traditional workloads dominated by compute-bound prefill, reasoning workloads generate long chains of reasoning tokens that shift inference into a \emph{Capacity-Bound regime}. This paper presents a comprehensive system characterization, evaluating models ranging from 8B to 671B parameters on GPUs clusters. By systematically exploring the interplay between Data, Tensor, and Pipeline parallelism, we identify critical bottlenecks that defy standard scaling heuristics. Our analysis reveals that data parallelism is throughput efficient for small models but hits a capacity trap on reasoning workloads as KV-cache fragmentation forces early throttling resulting in sub-optimal compute utilization. Tensor parallelism unlocks stranded memory and delivers sublinear gains near the 32B crossover. At frontier scale, dense models (e.g., Llama-405B) are interconnect and memory-bandwidth bound and favor high-degree TP, while sparse Mixture-of-Experts (MoE) models (e.g., DeepSeek-R1) are limited by routing and synchronization latency and benefit from hybrid strategies. These insights provide a rigorous decision framework for navigating the reasoning cliff, establishing new architectural imperatives for the next generation of inference infrastructure.

</details>


### [67] [Deep Tech to Space: Space Data Centers and AI Revolution at the Edge](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.19892)
*Jonas Weiss,Patricia Sagmeister,Gabriel Maiolini Capez,Dinesh Verma,Roberto Garello,Alberto Perotti,Dawid Lazaj,Alicja Musial,Jakub Nalepa,Thomas Morf,Martin Schmatz,Marek Krawczyk,Mateusz Przeliorz,Kevin Roche,Sagar Tayal,Mahalakshmi Lakshminarayanan,Nicolas Longépé,Pierre-Philippe Mathieu,Agata Wijata*

Main category: cs.DC

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种基于低轨道空间数据中心（SDC）卫星星座的架构设计，用于在轨处理卫星数据以应对传统地面站传输面临的链路拥塞、延迟和扩展性受限等问题，并通过技术路线图和预测模型验证了其技术可行性和经济可行性，辅以地球观测和月球探索的应用案例进行说明。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: The rapid increase in satellite numbers and space-generated data poses challenges for traditional space-to-Earth data transmission, including link congestion, increased latency, capacity constraints, complex scheduling logistics, and restricted visibility windows for ground station networks, which limit scalability and increase costs.

Method: The paper designs the architecture of a Low Earth Orbit SDC satellite constellation, covering orbital design, inter-satellite links and network topology, computational resource organization, and software service orchestration. Technical feasibility and economic viability are analyzed using forecasting models informed by technology roadmaps.

Result: Space Data Centers (SDCs) as software-driven, multi-tenant AI-based service platforms can process data in orbit to generate actionable insights for client satellites and ground users, effectively addressing data transmission challenges and enabling new applications in Earth observation and lunar exploration.

Conclusion: The paper provides architectural design for LEO SDC constellation and demonstrates its feasibility and economic viability through use cases in Earth observation and lunar exploration, offering a viable solution to satellite data transmission bottlenecks.

Abstract: Dramatic cost reductions driven by private sector innovations have led to a rapid increase in the number of satellites in orbit and a corresponding surge in space-generated data. As this trend continues, transmitting large volumes of data to Earth for processing may become increasingly costly and challenging due to potential space-to-Earth link congestion and increased latency. Moreover, traditional ground station networks may face difficulties accommodating growing data flows and workloads because of capacity constraints, complex scheduling logistics, and restricted visibility windows, which can limit scalability. Space Data Centers (SDCs) -- software-driven, multi-tenant artificial intelligence-based service platforms capable of processing data in orbit to generate actionable insights for client satellites and ground users -- represent a promising approach to address these challenges.
  This article presents the architecture of a Low Earth Orbit SDC satellite constellation, considering orbital design, inter-satellite links and network topology, computational resource organization, and software service orchestration. We analyze the potential technical feasibility and economic viability of SDCs using forecasting models informed by technology roadmaps and illustrate the concept through Earth observation and lunar exploration use cases.

</details>


### [68] [GEM: GPU-Variability-Aware Expert to GPU Mapping for MoE Systems](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.19945)
*Sourish Wawdhane,Avinash Kumar,Poulami Das*

Main category: cs.DC

TL;DR: GEM是一个GPU变异性感知的MoE专家映射框架，通过非均匀负载分配和一致性/时序性专家分离策略，解决分布式推理中的straggler瓶颈问题，实现7.9%~16.5%的延迟优化。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: MoE模型推理中，同步屏障导致整体性能受限于最慢GPU(straggler)。现有专家放置方法忽视了GPU性能差异，将高负载专家部署在慢GPU上，加剧了straggler问题。

Method: GEM采用两步策略：(1)基于GPU性能变异性进行非均匀负载分配；(2)将一致性专家和时序性专家分离部署在不同GPU，避免慢GPU成为瓶颈。

Result: 实验表明GEM相比基线方法平均提升7.9%延迟性能，最高提升16.5%。

Conclusion: GEM通过GPU变异性感知和专家分类映射策略，有效缓解了MoE推理中的straggler问题，验证了考虑硬件异构性对分布式推理优化的重要性。

Abstract: Mixture-of-Expert (MoE) models enable efficient inference by employing smaller experts and activating only a subset of them per token. MoE serving engines distribute experts across multiple GPUs and route tokens to appropriate GPUs at inference time based on experts activated. They process tokens in lock-step fashion, where tokens within a batch must finish processing before proceeding to the next layer. This synchronization barrier acts as a critical bottleneck because the performance of MoE models is limited by the straggler GPU that finishes last. Stragglers emerge when too many heavily used experts are placed on the same GPU or the slowest GPU. While prior works place experts that balance token loads across GPUs, they all overlook GPU variability and often place highly used experts on the slowest GPUs.
  We propose GEM, GPU-variability-aware Expert Mapping, a framework for GPU variability-aware expert to GPU mapping for MoE models. GEM exploits two insights. First, we must place experts such that each GPU receives non-uniform token loads based on their variability and they all finish processing a layer at about the same time. Our studies show that there are two types of experts: consistent that are used most of the time and temporal that are often used together for the remaining time. Our second insight is that we must place simultaneously used consistent and temporal experts on different GPUs and avoid placing them on slower GPUs to reduce slowdown. GEM gathers the variability profile of GPUs for each model and task and uses the token load distributions per task to map experts to GPUs. Our experiments show that GEM improves end-to-end latency by 7.9% on average and by up to 16.5% compared to the baseline.

</details>

### [69] [Near-Resolution of the Tradeoff Conjecture in Distributed Proof Labeling Schemes](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.19078)
*Arnold Filtser,Orr Fischer*

Main category: cs.DS

TL;DR: This paper resolves the Tradeoff Conjecture for proof labeling schemes. It proves that if a property has a 1-PLS with label cost p, it admits an O(ceil(p/t)) cost t-PLS for general graphs (with an extra O(t log n) neighborhood) and minor-free graphs (with an O(log n) additive term). A previously suggested stronger variant of the conjecture is also disproven.


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: The Tradeoff Conjecture (DISC 18) hypothesizes that for any property P, if a 1-PLS exists with cost p, then a t-PLS should exist with cost O(ceil(p/t)). Previous work only verified this for specific graph classes (trees, cycles, grids) or achieved weaker bounds like O(ceil(Δp/sqrt(t))) for fixed minor-free graphs.

Method: The authors prove the conjecture by constructing explicit transformations from 1-PLS to t-PLS with the claimed cost bounds, handling both general graphs and the more constrained minor-free graph class separately.

Result: General graphs: O(t log n)-PLS with cost O(ceil(p/t)). Minor-free graphs: t-PLS with cost O(ceil(p/t) + log n). Stronger conjecture variant is false—large t-hop neighborhoods alone cannot guarantee better tradeoffs.

Conclusion: The Tradeoff Conjecture is resolved up to a single logarithmic factor. For general graphs, the existence of a 1-PLS with cost p implies the existence of an O(t log n)-PLS with cost O(ceil(p/t)); for minor-free graphs, it implies a t-PLS with cost O(ceil(p/t) + log n). A stronger variant of the conjecture is refuted.

Abstract: In the $t$-Proof Labeling Scheme model ($t$-PLS model), our goal is to certify that a network of nodes satisfies a given property $P$. A prover assigns a label to each node, and each node decides to accept or reject based on its labeled $t$-hop neighborhood. If $P$ holds, there exists a labeling that makes all nodes accept. If $P$ does not hold, in all labelings at least one node rejects. The cost of a scheme is its maximum label size.
  The Tradeoff Conjecture [Feuilloley, Fraigniaud, Hirvonen, Paz, and Perry, DISC 18, Dist. Comput.~21] hypothesizes that the existence of a $1$-PLS for a property $P$ with cost $p$ implies the existence of a $t$-PLS for $P$ with cost $O(\lceil p/t \rceil)$. The conjecture was initially shown to hold for specific graph classes, such as trees, cycles, and grids. Later, a weaker $\widetilde{O}(\lceil Δp/\sqrt{t} \rceil)$ cost was shown for fixed minor-free graphs, where $Δ$ is the maximum degree.
  In this work we resolve the Tradeoff Conjecture, up to a single logarithmic factor. In general graphs, we show that the existence of a $1$-PLS with cost $p$ implies the existence of an $O(t\log{n})$-PLS with cost $O(\lceil p/t \rceil)$ for the same property. For fixed minor-free graphs (which include e.g. planar graphs), we show that the existence of a $1$-PLS with cost $p$ implies the existence of a $t$-PLS with cost $O(\lceil p/t \rceil+\log{n})$ for the same property. We also refute a previously suggested stronger variant of the Tradeoff Conjecture, and show that having very large $t$-hop neighborhoods is an insufficient condition for obtaining a tradeoff better than $O(\lceil p/t \rceil)$.

</details>


### [70] [Deterministic Volume Estimation of Truncated Hypercubes](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.19809)
*Kyra Gunluk*

Main category: cs.DS

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种确定性多项式时间算法，用于估计被一类特殊约束（凸单调函数求和形式）截断的单位超立方体体积。尽管单个线性约束已是#P难问题，但通过利用函数的结构特性（凸性、单调性），算法在poly(n, 1/ε, L, L_o)时间内实现(1+ε)近似。该方法可处理包括背包约束、范数球约束等在内的多种实际约束类型。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 单个线性约束（半空间）与单位超立方体的交集体积估计已是#P难问题，但在许多实际应用（背包约束、范数球约束等）中，约束函数具有更好的结构特性，需要探索这些结构能否带来计算上的简化。

Method: 设计了一种确定性多项式时间算法，利用约束函数的特殊结构（非负、单调、凸）进行体积估计。

Result: 能够对满足条件的一系列约束（包括背包约束、范数球约束等）所截断的单位超立方体交集体积，计算出(1+ε)乘数近似估计。

Conclusion: 通过利用函数的特殊结构（非负、单调、凸）绕过了一般#P难问题的计算复杂度障碍，在确定性和多项式时间内实现了体积估计的近似方案。

Abstract: We present a deterministic polynomial-time algorithm for estimating the volume of a hypercube intersected by a fixed number of constraints of the type $f(x) \leq b$, where $f$ is the sum of univariate functions that are each nonnegative, monotone, and convex. Such constraints include knapsack and norm-ball constraints. The case of the unit hypercube truncated by a single linear constraint (halfspace) is already #P-hard. Given $k$ such constraints in dimension $n$, with total input length of at most $L$ bits, total output length of at most $L_o$ bits, and an error parameter $\varepsilon > 0$, our algorithm computes a $(1 + \varepsilon)$-multiplicative approximation of the volume of their intersection with the unit hypercube $[0,1]^n$ in time poly$_k(n, 1/\varepsilon, L,L_o)$.

</details>


### [71] [Linear Kernels for $l$-Exact Component Order Connectivity](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.19853)
*Yuxi Liu,Mingyu Xiao*

Main category: cs.DS

TL;DR: Summary generation failed


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: Motivation analysis unavailable

Method: Method extraction failed

Result: Result analysis unavailable

Conclusion: Conclusion extraction failed

Abstract: The \textsc{$l$-Exact Component Order Connectivity} problem asks whether, given an input graph $G$ and an integer $k$, there exists a vertex subset $S\subseteq V(G)$ of size at most $k$ such that every connected component in $G - S$ has exactly $l$ vertices. In this paper, we present an $O(kl)$-vertex kernel for this problem, computable in $|V(G)|^{O(l)}$ time. This is the first known linear kernel for each fixed $l\geq 3$. For $l=1$, this problem reduces to the classical \textsc{Vertex Cover}, and our result matches the best-known $2k$-vertex kernel. For $l=2$ (known as \textsc{Deletion to Induced Matching}), we can get a $(3k + 1)$-vertex kernel, improving the previously known result of $6k$ vertices. Our kernelization algorithm is built upon on an extended crown decomposition combined with linear programming and other techniques.

</details>


### [72] [Deterministic Single Exponential Time Algorithms for Co-Path Packing and Co-Path Set Parameterized by Treewidth](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.19870)
*Yuxi Liu,Kangyi Tian,Mingyu Xiao*

Main category: cs.DS

TL;DR: 本文解决了两个重要图编辑问题的确定性算法设计问题。通过提供参数化为树宽的确定性单指数时间算法，填补了"Cut & Count"技术无法去随机化所带来的理论空白。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 当前最优算法依赖随机化的"Cut & Count"技术，无法去随机化，因此是否存在参数化为树宽的确定性单指数时间算法仍为开放问题。

Method: 采用确定性算法设计，绕过了随机化的"Cut & Count"技术，可能基于代数或组合方法的创新应用。

Result: 成功为Co-Path Packing和Co-Path Set两个问题提供了确定性单指数时间算法（参数化为树宽）。

Conclusion: 首次提出参数化为树宽的确定性单指数时间算法，填补了该领域的重要空白，为相关生物信息学应用提供了更可靠的算法基础。

Abstract: The \textsc{Co-Path Packing} (resp., \textsc{Co-Path Set}) problem asks whether a given graph can be edited to a collection of induced paths by deleting at most $k$ vertices (resp., $k$ edges). Both are fundamental problems with significant applications in bioinformatics and have been extensively studied within the framework of exact and parameterized algorithms. Currently, the state-of-the-art approach utilizes the randomized ``Cut \& Count'' technique, which solves \textsc{Co-Path Set} in $O^*(4^{\mathbf{tw}})$ time and \textsc{Co-Path Packing} in $O^*(5^{\mathbf{pw}})$ time, where $\mathbf{tw}$ is treewidth and $\mathbf{pw}$ is pathwidth. However, as there is no known method to derandomize the ``Cut \& Count'' technique, the existence of deterministic single exponential time algorithms for these problems parameterized by treewidth has remained an open question. In this paper, we resolve this gap by providing deterministic single exponential time algorithms for both problems when parameterized by treewidth.

</details>

# cs.AI [[Back]](#toc)

### [73] [Position: Let's Develop Data Probes to Fundamentally Understand How Data Affects LLM Performance](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.18801)
*Shiqiang Wang,Herbert Woisetschläger,Hans Arno Jacobsen,Mingyue Ji*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文是一篇立场论文，倡导开发系统性方法论来生成合成序列（称为"数据探针"），用于揭示数据特征如何影响LLM在训练、调优、对齐、上下文学习等不同阶段的行为，旨在超越当前依赖大规模实验的经验性方法。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 当前理解特定数据如何驱动LLM行为的方法严重依赖大规模实验和公共数据集，这种方法计算密集，且缺乏原则性途径来理解数据的本质作用。

Method: 采用立场论文形式，倡导开发从适当定义的随机过程中生成合成序列的系统性方法论，这些序列被称为"数据探针"，并借助典型集等理论概念来描述LLM的行为特征。

Result: 通过观察LLM在数据探针上的行为，研究者可以系统性地研究数据特征如何影响模型性能、泛化能力和鲁棒性。探针序列具有可借由理论概念（如典型集）分析的统计特性。

Conclusion: 提出"数据探针"概念，通过从适当定义的随机过程中生成合成序列，为研究数据在LLM训练和推理中的作用提供系统性方法论，超越现有的经验性启发式方法。

Abstract: Data is fundamental to large language models (LLMs). However, understanding of what makes certain data useful for different stages of an LLM workflow, including training, tuning, alignment, in-context learning, etc., and why, remains an open question. Current approaches rely heavily on extensive experimentation with large public datasets to obtain empirical heuristics for data filtering and dataset construction. These approaches are compute intensive and lack a principled way of understanding the essence of how specific data characteristics drive LLM behavior. In this position paper, we advocate for the need of developing systematic methodologies for generating synthetic sequences from appropriately defined random processes, with the goal that these sequences can reveal useful characteristics when they are used in one or multiple stages of the LLM workflow. We refer to such sequences as data probes. By observing LLM behavior on data probes, researchers can systematically conduct studies on how data characteristics influence model performance, generalization, and robustness. The probing sequences exhibit statistical properties that can be viewed using theoretical concepts, such as typical sets, which are generalized to describe the behaviors of LLMs. This data-probe approach provides a pathway for uncovering foundational insights into the role of data in LLM training and inference, beyond empirical heuristics.

</details>


### [74] [Operationalizing Document AI: A Microservice Architecture for OCR and LLM Pipelines in Production](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.18818)
*Yao Fehlis,Benjamin Bengfort,Zhangzhang Si,Vahid Eyorokon,Prema Roman,Patrick Deziel,Devon Slonaker,Steve Veldman,Ben Johnson,Joyce Rigelo,Michael Wharton,Steve Kramer*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文提出了一个微服务架构，用于封装文档理解流水线（分类、OCR、大语言模型结构化提取），并在生产环境中实现每小时处理数千份多页文档的规模。文章揭示了两个关键发现：OCR而非语言模型解析主导端到端延迟；系统并发上限由共享GPU推理容量决定而非工作线程数。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 学术研究侧重于文档理解新模型的开发，但在模型定义与生产级规模部署之间存在显著差距，缺乏将模型有效运营化的具体架构指导。

Method: 设计了微服务架构，包含四大核心设计决策：混合分类策略、GPU密集型推理与CPU密集型编排的分离、异步处理IO密集型操作、独立水平扩展策略，并通过批处理性能分析进行评估。

Result: 通过生产环境运行验证，发现：(1) OCR而非LLM解析主导端到端延迟；(2) 系统饱和点由共享GPU推理容量决定，而非worker数量。

Conclusion: 为从业者提供了构建超越基准测试的文档理解系统的具体架构模式，强调GPU资源管理是生产部署的关键瓶颈。

Abstract: Academic research tends to focus on new models for document understanding creating a wide gap in the literature between model definition and running models at production scale. To close that gap, we present a microservice architecture that encapsulates pipelines of multiple models for classification, optical character recognition (OCR), and large language model structured field extraction as well as our experience running this pipeline on thousands of multi-page documents per hour. We describe our primary design decisions, including a hybrid classification, separation of GPU-bound inference from CPU-bound orchestration, use of asynchronous processing for the many IO-bound operations in the pipeline, and an independent, horizontal scaling strategy. Using batch profiling, we identified two surprising qualitative findings that shape production deployments: OCR, not language-model parsing, dominates end-to-end latency, and the system saturates at a concurrency determined by shared GPU-inference capacity rather than worker count. Our goal is to provide practitioners with concrete architectural patterns for building document understanding systems that work beyond the benchmark; effectively operationalizing models in production.

</details>


### [75] [Evaluating the Utility of Personal Health Records in Personalized Health AI](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.18937)
*Rory Sayres,Kejia Chen,Ayush Jain,Matthew Thompson,Jonathan Richina,Xiang Yin,Jimmy Hu,Fan Zhang,Bob Lou,Mike Sanchez,Ines Mezerreg,Meredith Schreier,Hamsa Subramaniam,I-Ching Lee,Yugang Jia,Daniel Mcduff,Yossi Matias,Avinatan Hassidim,Dale Webster,Yun Liu,Jackie Barr,Quang Duong*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本研究评估了大语言模型（Gemini 3.0 Flash）在提供个人健康记录（PHR）上下文时，回答患者健康问题的效果。通过2,257个来自不同来源的患者查询，发现PHR数据显著提升回答的有用性（p<0.001），同时开发了针对PHR特定错误模式的新评估框架。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 患者管理型个人健康记录（PHR）虽然有赋权患者的潜力，但其信息复杂性可能阻碍患者获取有效洞察。研究旨在探索LLM是否能帮助患者更好地理解其健康记录中的信息。

Method: 研究使用3种不同分布的2,257个患者查询（网页搜索查询、聊天机器人模板问题、患者呼叫问题），匹配1,945份去标识化PHR。生成三组Gemini回答：（1）无PHR上下文；（2）基础摘要（人口统计、疾病、用药）；（3）完整详细临床笔记。采用SHARP评估框架及新开发的PHR特定错误模式框架，使用自动评估和临床医生评估（n=95）进行评价。

Result: PHR数据显著提升所有问题类型的回答有用性（p<0.001，配对t检验）。在安全性、准确性、相关性和个性化方面均有潜在提升。新评估框架识别出复杂PHR理解中的差距，如时间定向障碍和罕见但有意义的虚构问题。

Conclusion: PHR数据有潜力帮助满足广泛的用户需求；新评估框架可用于监测基于PHR上下文的LLM回答质量差距。研究结果为后续评估和实现PHR赋权患者的益处提供了基础。

Abstract: Patient-managed Personal Health Records (PHRs) promises to empower patients to better understand their health; but information in the record is complex, potentially hindering insights. In this study, we assess the potential of large language models (LLMs, Gemini 3.0 Flash) to provide helpful answers to user health queries, when provided clinical data from PHRs as context. A total of 2,257 user queries were drawn from 3 different distributions to represent patient questions: shorter web search queries, longer questions derived from templates of chatbot conversations, and questions patients asked to their healthcare team (patient calls). Queries were matched with de-identified PHRs (from a pool of 1,945). Gemini responses were generated (1) without PHR context; (2) with a basic summary of demographics, conditions, and medications; (3) with full, extensive clinical notes. For evaluation, we leveraged an existing rating framework (SHARP), and developed a new framework for specific error modes when interpreting PHRs. Evaluation was performed using autoraters for the full set, and with clinician ratings for a subset (n=95), with both sets of raters knowing the full PHR context. We see significant improvements in the helpfulness of answers to all question types with PHR data (p < 0.001, paired t-test). We also observe potential gains in safety, accuracy, relevance and personalization of answers. Our PHR evaluation framework further identifies gaps in LLM understanding of particular aspects of complex PHRs, such as temporal disorientation, and rare but meaningful confabulations. These results suggest potential for PHR data to help people with a wide range of user needs; and provide a framework for monitoring for gaps in LLM answers based on PHR context. This study motivates further work to assess and realize potential benefits to users from understanding their health records.

</details>


### [76] [Learn-by-Wire Training Control Governance: Bounded Autonomous Training Under Stress for Stability and Efficiency](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.19008)
*Anis Radianis*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: LBW-Guard是一种运行在AdamW之上的有界自主训练控制治理层，通过观察训练遥测数据、解释不稳定性敏感状态并应用有界控制来优化器执行，从而提升大规模语言模型训练的稳定性和效率。在Qwen2.5-7B上实现困惑度从13.21降至10.74（提升18.7%），训练速度提升1.10倍，并在高学习率压力测试中保持可训练性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现代语言模型训练在激进的学习率、规模和运行时压力条件下，越来越多地面临不稳定性、退化运行和计算浪费的问题。传统的梯度裁剪等方法无法解决这些问题，需要一种新的训练控制机制。

Method: 提出LBW-Guard（Learn-by-Wire Guard），一个在AdamW之上运行的有界自主训练控制治理层。该方法不替代优化器更新规则，而是通过观察训练遥测数据、解释不稳定性敏感区域，对优化器执行应用有界控制，同时保持固定的训练目标。在Qwen2.5-centered压力与鲁棒性测试套件中进行评估，涵盖WikiText-103数据集、Qwen2.5-7B/3B/14B不同规模模型对比、学习率压力测试、梯度裁剪基线和TinyLlama-1B全参数检查。

Result: 在7B参考设置下，LBW-Guard将最终困惑度从13.21降至10.74（提升18.7%），端到端时间从392.54秒降至357.02秒（加速1.10倍）。在更强的学习率压力测试中，AdamW在LR=3e-3时退化至1885.24困惑度、LR=1e-3时退化至659.76，而LBW-Guard仍可训练至11.57和10.33。梯度裁剪基线无法复现此效果。

Conclusion: 稳定性敏感的大语言模型训练可从优化器上层的治理平面中受益。有界运行时控制可以在压力条件下保持有效计算，同时与优化器替换和局部梯度抑制有本质区别。

Abstract: Modern language-model training is increasingly exposed to instability, degraded runs, and wasted compute, especially under aggressive learning-rate, scale, and runtime-stress conditions. This paper introduces Learn-by-Wire Guard (LBW-Guard), a bounded autonomous training-control governance layer that operates above AdamW. Rather than replacing the optimizer update rule, LBW-Guard observes training telemetry, interprets instability-sensitive regimes, and applies bounded control to optimizer execution while preserving fixed training objectives.
  We evaluate LBW-Guard in a Qwen2.5-centered stress-and-robustness suite using WikiText-103, with Qwen2.5-7B as the empirical anchor, model-size comparisons against Qwen2.5-3B and Qwen2.5-14B, learning-rate stress tests, gradient-clipping baselines, and a no-LoRA TinyLlama-1B full-parameter sanity check. In the 7B reference setting, LBW-Guard reduces final perplexity from 13.21 to 10.74, an 18.7% improvement, while reducing end-to-end time from 392.54s to 357.02s, a 1.10x speedup. Under stronger learning-rate stress, AdamW degrades to 1885.24 final perplexity at LR=3e-3 and 659.76 at LR=1e-3, whereas LBW-Guard remains trainable at 11.57 and 10.33, respectively. Gradient-clipping baselines do not reproduce this effect.
  These results support a scoped systems conclusion that stability-sensitive LLM training can benefit from a governance plane above the optimizer. LBW-Guard provides evidence that bounded runtime control can preserve productive compute under stress while remaining distinct from optimizer replacement and local gradient suppression.

</details>


### [77] [AgentNLQ: A General-Purpose Agent for Natural Language to SQL](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.19010)
*Olena Bogdanov,Yeunji Jung,Chandra Dhir,Pareekshitreddy Gaddam,Saurabh Jain,Lakshmi Tumati,Vijay Parthasarathy,Anup Shirgaonkar*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种基于多智能体系统的自然语言转SQL (NL2SQL)方法，在BIRD基准测试上达到78.1%的语义准确率。该方法通过优化的编排器、高级模式 enrichment 方法和业务规则整合，生成准确的SQL查询。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 尽管大语言模型发展迅速，但NL2SQL仍未达到人类专家水平，存在准确率差距，需要进一步的算法改进以提升性能。

Method: 设计了一个优化的多智能体编排器，使用LLM进行规划、编排、反思和自纠正；开发了高级模式enrichment方法，创建上下文感知元数据；整合用户提供的业务规则。

Result: 在BIRD-SQL基准测试上达到78.1%语义准确率，证明了方法在不同领域和数据集上的准确性和泛化能力。

Conclusion: 多智能体架构结合模式enrichment和业务规则整合可有效提升NL2SQL准确率，为实际应用中的数据库查询提供更可靠的解决方案。

Abstract: Natural language to SQL (NL2SQL) conversion is an important problem for researchers and enterprises due to the ubiquitous importance of relational databases in broad-ranging practical problems. Despite the rapid advancements in the capabilities of LLMs, NL2SQL has not reached parity in accuracy with human expert SQL writers, hence needing additional improvements in NL2SQL algorithms. This study presents a new multi-agent method for NL2SQL that achieves 78.1% semantic accuracy on the BIg Bench for LaRge-scale Database (BIRD) benchmark. Our method leverages a semantically enriched representation of user-provided schema, adds user-provided business rules, and produces accurate SQL queries. The main contributions of this study are (a) We designed an optimized new orchestrator in a multi-agent solution that uses LLMs to plan, orchestrate, reflect, and self-correct to generate accurate SQL queries, (b) We developed an advanced schema enrichment method that creates context-aware metadata to improve accuracy, and (c) We demonstrated the accuracy and generalizability of the method across different domains and datasets by evaluating it on the BIRD-SQL benchmark.

</details>


### [78] [DecisionBench: A Benchmark for Emergent Delegation in Long-Horizon Agentic Workflows](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.19099)
*Yuxuan Gao,Megan Wang,Yi Ling Yu,Zijian Carl Ma,Ao Qu*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: DecisionBench是一个用于评估长时域智能体工作流中委托能力的基准测试框架，包含任务套件、模型池、委托接口和多功能指标体系。通过对11个模型、7个厂商家族的23,375个任务实例进行五条件参考扫描，发现：质量指标在有无委托意识条件下无显著差异，但路由保真度和委托覆盖率存在大幅提升空间。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 当前对智能体委托能力（emergent delegation）的评估缺乏系统性基准。现有评估往往仅关注最终任务质量，无法捕捉编排（orchestration）层面的信号，导致无法有效区分不同委托策略的实际效果。

Method: 研究者设计了一个模块化基准框架，包含：(1) 固定任务套件（GAIA、tau-bench、BFCL multi-turn）；(2) peer模型池（11个模型，7个厂商家族）；(3) 委托接口（call_model + 可选read_profile通道）；(4) 确定性技能标注层；(5) 多维度指标套件（质量、成本、延迟、委托率、路由保真度-k、厂商自偏好、反事实委托上限）。对完整模型池进行五条件参考扫描（n=23,375任务实例）。

Result: 三个基准级发现：(i) 四种意识条件下端任务质量无统计显著差异（|beta|≤0.010, p≥0.21），说明仅用质量评估会遗漏编排信号；(ii) 路由保真度-at-1在7.5%-29.5%之间，交付渠道（按需工具调用vs预加载描述）影响大于描述内容本身；(iii) 反事实上限显示完美委托比实测性能高15-31个百分点，表明存在大量未实现的改进空间。

Conclusion: DecisionBench提供了一个标准化、可扩展的委托评估框架，揭示了当前智能体在路由选择和委托决策上的显著不足，为未来编排方法研究提供了明确的改进方向和评估标准。

Abstract: We introduce DecisionBench, a benchmark substrate for emergent delegation in long-horizon agentic workflows. The substrate fixes a task suite (GAIA, tau-bench, BFCL multi-turn), a peer-model pool (11 models, 7 vendor families), a delegation interface (call_model plus an optional read_profile channel), a deterministic skill-annotation layer, and a multi-axis metric suite covering quality, cost, latency, delegation rate, routing fidelity-at-k, vendor self-preference, and a counterfactual-delegation ceiling. The substrate is agnostic to how peer information is generated or delivered, so learned routers, richer peer memories, adaptive profile construction, and multi-step delegation can all be evaluated against it. We characterize the substrate with a five-condition reference sweep on the full pool (n=23,375 task instances). Three benchmark-level findings emerge: (i) mean end-task quality is statistically indistinguishable across the four awareness conditions (|beta| <= 0.010, p >= 0.21), so quality-only evaluation would miss the orchestration signal; (ii) routing fidelity-at-1 ranges from 7.5% to 29.5% across conditions at near-equal mean quality, with delivery channel (on-demand tool vs. preloaded description) dominating description content; (iii) a counterfactual ceiling places perfect delegation 15-31 percentage points above measured performance on every suite, locating large unrealized headroom for future orchestration methods. We release the substrate, annotation layer, reference intervention suite, analysis pipeline, and 220 per-condition run archives.

</details>


### [79] [How Far Are We From True Auto-Research?](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.19156)
*Zhengxin Zhang,Ning Wang,Sainyam Galhotra,Claire Cardie*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: ResearchArena评估了三个主流AI agent（Claude Code、Codex、Kimi Code）在自主完成完整科研流程后的论文质量。通过117篇论文、三层评估体系（仅稿件评审SAR、含工件对等评审PR、人工元评审）发现：仅看SAR时AI论文看似具有竞争力，但实际存在严重的实验严谨性问题（伪造结果、实验不足、计划-执行不匹配），且各agent失败率差异巨大（5% vs 77%）。结论：当前AI生成的论文距顶级会议接收标准仍有显著差距，"全自动科研"尚为时过早。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 当前auto-research系统虽能产出完整论文，但缺乏系统性评估——可行性不等于质量。现有研究未能系统检验AI agent生成的论文究竟有多好，且缺少工件层面的验证。

Method: 构建ResearchArena最小化脚手架，让三个off-the-shelf agent自主完成完整研究闭环（构思、实验、写稿、自 refinement），覆盖13个CS种子任务，每对agent-领域组合运行3次，共生成117篇论文。采用三层互补评估：仅稿件评审（SAR）、工件感知的对等评审（PR，agent同时审查代码/数据等工件）、人工元评审。

Result: SAR评估下前景看似乐观——Claude Code得分最高，超越FARS、匹配ICLR 2025加权平均分数。但人工审查揭示真实情况：SAR评分与实际接收决策对齐度差，会奖励表面合理但缺乏实验验证的论文。PR评分大幅下降，人工审计识别出实验严谨性是主要瓶颈，表现为三种失败模式（伪造结果、实验不足、计划-执行不匹配），且各agent差异悬殊（Codex 5%/8% vs Kimi Code 77%/72%），背后是不同agent发展出截然不同的"研究人格"。117篇论文无一达到顶级会议接收标准。

Conclusion: 当前轻量引导的AI agent可生成表面看起来有竞争力的论文，但在实验严谨性层面存在根本性缺陷，距离真正的"全自动科研"仍有显著差距。该研究为AI科研能力评估提供了多维度基准和方法论框架。

Abstract: Recent auto-research systems can produce complete papers, but feasibility is not the same as quality, and the field still lacks a systematic study of how good agent-generated papers actually are. We introduce ResearchArena, a minimal scaffold that lets off-the-shelf agents (Claude Code using Opus 4.6, Codex using GPT-5.4, and Kimi Code using K2.5) carry out the full research loop themselves (ideation, experimentation, paper writing, self-refinement) under only lightweight guidance. Across 13 computer science seeds and 3 trials per agent-domain pair, ResearchArena yields 117 agent-generated papers, each evaluated under three complementary lenses: a manuscript-only reviewer (SAR), an artifact-aware peer review (PR) in which agents inspect the workspace alongside the manuscript, and an human conducted meta-review. Under SAR alone the picture is optimistic: Claude Code obtains the highest score, outperforms Analemma's FARS, and matches the weighted-average human ICLR 2025 submission, suggesting that minimally scaffolded agents can produce papers that look competitive on manuscript-only review. Manual inspection, however, reveals this picture is overstated: SAR scores are poorly aligned with its actual acceptance decisions and reward plausible framing without verifying experimental substance. Under artifact-aware PR scores drop sharply, and manual auditing identifies experimental rigor as the major bottleneck, decomposing into three failure modes (fabricated results, underpowered experiments, and plan/execution mismatch) that are highly agent-dependent: Codex 5%/8% paper-vs-artifact mismatch / fabricated references versus Kimi Code 77%/72%, a $\sim$15$\times$ spread that tracks distinct research personas the agents develop. None of the 117 agent-generated papers reaches the acceptance bar of a top-tier venue. This suggests that we are still gapped from the true auto-research.

</details>


### [80] [CogScale: Scalable Benchmark for Sequence Processing](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.19758)
*Yannis Bendi-Ouis,Romain de Coudenhove,Xavier Hinaut*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: CogScale是一个轻量级基准测试，包含14个可扩展任务，用于快速验证新架构；评估显示传统RNN/ESN擅长基础记忆任务，而Transformer和Mamba在复杂推理任务中表现更优。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现代模型评估计算成本高、耗时长，新架构测试需要大规模数据集和模型。

Method: 提出CogScale基准测试，包含14个可扩展的合成任务，用于隔离和评估特定认知和记忆能力；在严格参数预算（1k、10k、100k）下评估7种架构。

Result: 在14个合成任务上对7种架构进行了系统比较，发现不同架构在不同难度和规模下表现各异。

Conclusion: 传统RNN和ESN在严格参数预算下表现优异，而注意力机制和现代状态空间模型在推理复杂度和任务难度增加时能持续保持高性能。

Abstract: The ability to maintain and manipulate information over time is a fundamental aspect of living beings and Artificial Intelligence. While modern models have achieved remarkable success in tasks like natural language processing, evaluating the capacity of novel architectures to process sequential information remains computationally expensive and time-consuming. Testing a new architecture often requires scaling up to massive datasets and models, leading to vast computational costs and slow iteration cycles. In this paper, we propose CogScale, a benchmark of 14 scalable synthetic tasks designed to isolate and evaluate specific cognitive and memory abilities at different parametrizable scales. By providing a standardized, lightweight framework, CogScale allows researchers to rapidly validate architectural innovations before committing to large-scale training. To establish a solid baseline, we evaluate seven distinct architectures: Gated Recurrent Unit (GRU), Long Short-Term Memory (LSTM), xLSTM, Echo State Network (ESN), Mamba, Transformer Decoder, and Transformer Encoder-Decoder. These evaluations are conducted under strict parameter budgets (1k, 10k, and 100k) and across different difficulty levels and scales. Our results show that while classical RNNs and Echo State Networks excel at basic retention within strict parameter budgets, only attention mechanisms and modern state-space models consistently maintain high performance as reasoning complexity and task difficulty scale.

</details>


### [81] [KAN-MLP-Mixer: A comprehensive investigation of the usage of Kolmogorov-Arnold Networks (KANs) for improving IMU-based Human Activity Recognition](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.19031)
*Mengxi Liu,Sizhen Bian,Vitor Fortes,Francisco Calatrava Nicolas,Daniel Geißler,Maximilian Kiefer-Emmanouilidis,Bo Zhou,Paul Lukowicz*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文针对KAN在噪声环境下性能下降的问题，提出了一种混合KAN-MLP架构用于人类活动识别(HAR)，通过在8个公开数据集上的实验验证了该混合模型相比纯MLP模型平均提升5.33%的macro F1分数。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: KAN在干净、低维数据上表现优异，但在噪声和真实世界的复杂数据集上性能下降严重；传统MLP虽精度略低，但对噪声更鲁棒且计算效率更高。将KAN直接替代MLP用于HAR模型会导致精度和效率下降，因此需要探索如何结合两者的优势。

Method: 提出一种混合KAN-MLP架构，策略性地融合两种范式的优势：采用KAN-based输入嵌入层保留细粒度特征提取能力，使用MLP层进行中间层特征混合以增强噪声鲁棒性，引入专门的LarctanKAN模块用于最终活动分类。通过在8个公开HAR数据集上评估，验证混合架构的有效性，并将其集成到其他先进HAR架构中测试泛化能力。

Result: 混合KAN-MLP模型在8个公开HAR数据集上相比纯MLP模型实现平均5.33%的macro F1相对提升，显著优于单独的KAN和MLP基线模型。将该混合策略集成到其他SOTA HAR架构中也一致性地提升了性能。

Conclusion: 精心设计的KAN、MLP或其他传统神经组件组合能够产生更鲁棒、更准确的HAR模型，适用于真实世界的可穿戴传感环境。混合架构是平衡KAN的表达能力与MLP的噪声鲁棒性的有效策略。

Abstract: Kolmogorov-Arnold Networks (KANs) have demonstrated an exceptional ability to learn complex functions on clean, low-dimensional data but struggle to maintain performance on noisy and imperfect real-world datasets. In contrast, conventional multi-layer perceptrons (MLPs) are far more tolerant to noise and computationally efficient. Replacing all MLP components with KANs in HAR models often degrades accuracy and computation efficiency, highlighting an open challenge: how to combine KANs' precision with MLPs' noise robustness and efficiency. To address this, we systematically explore various placements of KAN modules within deep HAR networks and propose a hybrid architecture that strategically synergizes the strengths of both paradigms, which uses a KAN-based input embedding layer, retains MLP layers for intermediate feature mixing, and introduces a specialized LarctanKAN module for final activity classification. Across eight public HAR datasets, the hybrid KAN-MLP model achieves an average macro F1 score relative improvement of 5.33\% compared pure-MLP model, significantly outperforming standalone KAN and MLP baselines. Furthermore, integrating this hybrid strategy into other state-of-the-art HAR architectures consistently boosts their performance. Our findings demonstrate that a carefully orchestrated combination of KAN, MLP, or other conventional neural components yields more robust and accurate HAR models for real-world wearable sensing environments.

</details>


### [82] [AQuaUI: Visual Token Reduction for GUI Agents with Adaptive Quadtrees](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.19260)
*Yuankai Li,Tinghui Zhu,Ha Min Son,Zhe Zhao,Xin Liu,Muhao Chen*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: AquaUI是一种无需训练的推理时token压缩方法，利用自适应四叉树对GUI截图进行信息密度感知的token裁剪，在GUI-Owl-1.5-32B-Instruct上实现13.22%加速和29.52%更少视觉token，同时保留99.06%的全token性能。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有大视觉模型处理GUI截图时面临空间信息密度高度不均匀的问题：大面积区域视觉同质化、信息密度低，而关键文本和图标需要高视觉保真度。现有方法需额外训练或依赖注意力压缩，忽略了GUI截图的结构化布局和空间冗余特性。

Method: AQuaUI构建两步方案：1）自适应四叉树分解：对每张截图输入构建自适应四叉树，每个叶节点保留一个代表性合并token，保留token空间位置以确保位置编码一致；2）条件四叉树算法：利用连续截图间的连续性，以前一棵四叉树为参考优化当前四叉树，保持跨多步GUI交互的时序一致性。

Result: 在标准定位和导航基准测试中，AQuaUI始终展现更优的精度-效率权衡。最佳情况下（GUI-Owl-1.5-32B-Instruct），实现13.22%加速、29.52%更少视觉token，同时保留99.06%全token性能。

Conclusion: 实验证明，GUI截图的空间冗余可在无需重训练的情况下于推理时被有效利用。AquaUI为GUI agent模型提供了一种高效、训练无关的token压缩方案。

Abstract: Large Multimodal Models (LMMs) have recently emerged as promising backbones for GUI-agent models, where high-resolution GUI screenshots are introduced to the prompts at each iteration step. However, these screenshots exhibit highly non-uniform spatial information density: large regions may carry little information and are visually homogeneous, while key text and icons may require high visual fidelity. Existing approaches to this problem either require additional training or rely on attention-based token compression, ignoring the structured layout and spatial redundancy of GUI screenshots. To fill the gap, this paper proposes AquaUI, a training-free inference-time token reduction method for GUI agent models that utilizes the non-uniform information density in screenshots. AQuaUI constructs an adaptive quadtree on each screenshot input and keeps one representative merged token per leaf of the quadtree. AQuaUI preserves the spatial positions of retained tokens throughout the pipeline to ensure that all position-encoding stages remain consistent. To further improve temporal consistency across multi-step GUI interactions, we propose a conditional quadtree algorithm that leverages the continuity between consecutive screenshots within a single request. Specifically, it refines the current quadtree using previous quadtrees as references, helping preserve fine-grained regions across static or mildly shifted GUI states. We implement AQuaUI on state-of-the-art GUI agent models and conduct experiments on standard grounding and navigational benchmarks. AQuaUI consistently shows improved accuracy-efficiency trade-offs over prior baselines. Notably, on GUI-Owl-1.5-32B-Instruct, AQuaUI achieves up to 13.22% speedup and 29.52% fewer visual tokens while retaining 99.06% of full-token performance, suggesting that the spatial redundancy of GUI screenshots can be exploited at inference without retraining.

</details>


### [83] [Swimming with Whales: Analysis of Power Imbalances in Stake-Weighted Governance](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.19264)
*Yuzhe Zhang,Manvir Schneider,Qin Wang,Davide Grossi*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 研究PoS区块链中质押加权投票机制下的权力失衡问题，采用Penrose-Banzhaf权力指数量化分析，证明了完美权力对齐的不可达性，同时指出在特定条件下可在期望意义下近似实现。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: PoS区块链中质押加权投票范式存在权力扭曲问题：少数大额质押用户可能完全控制决策，即便他们并不拥有全部质押。

Method: 使用Penrose-Banzhaf权力指数量化权力，运用计算社会选择理论框架进行分析；实证部分采用Project Catalyst的真实链上治理数据。

Result: 证明完美权力对齐一般不可达，但可在特定条件下近似实现；揭示了当前质押加权治理系统中实际发生的权力失衡程度。

Conclusion: 完美对齐权力与相对质押所有权通常不可实现，但在特定条件下可在期望意义下近似达成；通过Project Catalyst真实链上治理数据揭示了当前质押加权治理系统中可能发生的权力失衡问题的细粒度特征。

Abstract: Voting methods weighted by stakes are the fundamental governance paradigm in Proof-of-Stake (PoS) blockchains. Such a paradigm is known to be prone to power distortions: a few users possessing large stakes may completely control decision making, even without owning the totality of the stakes. We study this phenomenon through the lens of computational social choice, focusing on the extent of power imbalances in stake-weighted voting when power is quantified using the Penrose-Banzhaf power index. Our work presents both analytical and empirical contributions. Analytically, we demonstrate that while a perfect alignment between power and relative stake ownership is generally unattainable, it can be approximated in expectation under specific conditions. Empirically, using data from a real-world on-chain governance system (Project Catalyst), we provide a more fine-grained understanding of the power imbalances that are likely to occur in current stake-weighted governance systems.

</details>


### [84] [EngiAI: A Multi-Agent Framework and Benchmark Suite for LLM-Driven Engineering Design](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.19743)
*Gioele Molinari,Florian Felten,Soheyl Massoudi,Mark Fuge*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 该论文提出了一个针对LLM多智能体工程设计系统的基准测试套件(EngiBench)，包含工作流、RAG和HPC三个评估维度，并配套提供EngiAI参考实现。实验表明专有模型表现优异(96-97%)，条件分支任务最具挑战性，检索增强对参数选择至关重要，长工作流中的多步骤指令遵循会退化。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 大型语言模型智能体在工程设计任务中的应用日益增多，但现有评估框架未能充分涵盖结合仿真、检索和制造准备的多智能体系统，缺乏系统性的评估方法。

Method: 构建了包含三个评估维度的基准测试套件：(1) 工作流基准测试，含七种提示风格针对不同认知需求；(2) RAG基准测试，通过门控评分隔离检索贡献；(3) HPC基准测试，评估SLURM集群上的端到端ML训练编排。同时提出基于LangGraph构建的EngiAI多智能体系统参考实现，通过监督者架构协调七个专业智能体。

Result: 专有模型在Beams2D上达到96-97%的平均任务完成率，而4B参数开源模型为55-78%，存在明显的代际改进。条件分支最具挑战性，Photonics2D上完成率降至20-53%。RAG门控验证了近乎完美的检索增强分数(~1.0)对比无检索时的近零分数。HPC编排中，一模型100%完成所有步骤，另一模型降至50%。

Conclusion: 多步骤指令遵循在长运行工作流中会退化。条件分支是最具挑战性的任务类型。该基准测试有效验证了评估设计的合理性，为工程设计领域的LLM多智能体系统提供了系统性评测方案。

Abstract: Large Language Model (LLM) agents are increasingly applied to engineering design tasks, yet existing evaluation frameworks do not adequately address multi-agent systems that combine simulation, retrieval, and manufacturing preparation. We introduce a benchmark suite with three evaluation dimensions: (1) a workflow benchmark with seven prompt styles targeting distinct cognitive demands-including direct tool use, semantic disambiguation, conditional branching, and working-memory tasks; (2) a Retrieval-Augmented Generation (RAG) benchmark with gated scoring isolating retrieval contributions to parameter selection; and (3) an High Performance Computing (HPC) benchmark evaluating end-to-end ML training orchestration on a SLURM cluster. Alongside the benchmark we present EngiAI, a Multi-Agent System (MAS) reference implementation built on LangGraph that operationalizes the benchmark by coordinating seven specialized agents through a supervisor architecture, unifying topology optimization, document retrieval, HPC job orchestration, and 3D printer control. Across four LLM backends and two EngiBench problems, proprietary models achieve 96-97% average task completion on Beams2D, while open-source 4B-parameter models reach 55-78%, with clear generational improvement. Conditional branching proves most challenging, with task completion dropping to 20-53% for the conditional style on Photonics2D. RAG gating confirms near-perfect retrieval-augmented scores ($\approx 1.0$) versus near-zero without retrieval, validating the evaluation design. On HPC orchestration, one model completes all pipeline steps in 100% of runs while another drops to 50%, revealing that multi-step instruction following degrades over long-running workflows.

</details>


### [85] [Memory-Augmented Reinforcement Learning Agent for CAD Generation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.19748)
*Yin Xiaolong,Liu Yu,Shen Jiahang,Lu Xingyu,Ni Jingzhe,Fan Fengxiao,Sang Fan*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文提出一种记忆增强强化学习框架，用于解决复杂CAD模型自动生成问题。该框架通过结构化工具链、双轨记忆模块和动态效用检索算法，配合强化学习实现无需大规模标注数据的在线自纠正和持续进化，显著提升复杂CAD模型生成的成功率和几何一致性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有基于大语言模型的CAD生成方法难以处理复杂模型（长操作序列、多样化操作类型、强几何约束），主要原因是推理链断裂和缺乏有效的错误纠正机制

Method: 记忆增强强化学习框架；封装几何内核为结构化工具链；设计案例库与技能库的双轨记忆模块；提出动态效用检索算法；将强化学习引入检索与策略优化

Result: 成功率和几何一致性显著提升

Conclusion: 显著提升复杂CAD模型生成任务的成功率和几何一致性，展示了无需大规模标注数据的在线自纠正和持续进化能力

Abstract: Automatic generation of computer-aided design (CAD) models is a core technology for enabling intelligence in advanced manufacturing. Existing generation methods based on large language models (LLMs) often fall short when handling complex CAD models characterized by long operation sequences, diverse operation types, and strong geometric constraints, primarily because reasoning chains break and effective error-correction mechanisms are lacking. To address this problem, this paper proposes a memory-augmented reinforcement learning framework for CAD generation agents. The framework encapsulates the underlying geometric kernel into a structured toolchain callable by the agent and builds a closed-loop mechanism of design intent understanding, global planning, execution, and multi-dimensional verification. It also designs a dual-track memory module consisting of a case library and a skill library, and proposes a dynamic utility retrieval algorithm. By introducing reinforcement learning into retrieval and policy optimization, the agent can effectively avoid retrieval traps in which examples are semantically similar but geometrically infeasible, enabling online self-correction and continual evolution without additional large-scale annotated data. Experiments show that the proposed method significantly improves both the success rate and geometric consistency on complex CAD model generation tasks.

</details>


### [86] [Not all uncertainty is alike: volatility, stochasticity, and exploration](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.19215)
*Payam Piray*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文揭示了环境不确定性的两种来源（波动性和随机性）对探索行为具有相反影响：波动性促进探索，而随机性抑制探索。通过将Gittins指数框架扩展到具有潜动态的高斯状态空间 bandit 问题，并推导出因果感知的不确定性敏感探索（CAUSE）算法，证明了这一不对称性。实验表明CAUSE在异构噪声环境中表现优异，且该框架对精神疾病的计算建模具有启示意义。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 传统决策理论将环境不确定性视为统一概念，忽略了不同类型不确定性对探索行为的差异化影响。然而现实中，奖励可能因环境变化而漂移（波动性），也可能因信号噪声而模糊（随机性）。这种区分对于构建更真实的人工和生物智能系统至关重要。

Method: 1. 将Gittins指数框架扩展到具有潜动态的高斯状态空间 bandit 模型；2. 通过控制即推理（control-as-inference）框架推导出闭环形式的探索奖励 CAUSE；3. 在具有异构噪声结构的多种环境中进行算法对比实验。

Result: 形式化证明波动性增强探索而随机性抑制探索的不对称性；CAUSE 算法在异构噪声环境中优于标准探索策略，且在 restless bandit 设置中优于传统 per-arm Gittins 策略；发现异常的噪声推理会导致"反转"而非仅仅是受损的探索行为。

Conclusion: 学习与探索受相同的不确定性-推理不对称性支配。该框架为理解精神疾病中的探索缺陷提供了计算模型，暗示某些精神障碍可能源于噪声推理机制的异常，而非简单的探索能力缺失。

Abstract: Adaptive decision-making in biological and artificial intelligence requires balancing the exploitation of known outcomes with the exploration of uncertain alternatives. Although prior work suggests that uncertainty generally promotes exploration, it has typically treated distinct sources of environmental uncertainty as equivalent. We consider environments with latent reward states that drift over time (volatility) and are observed through noisy outcomes (stochasticity). Both increase posterior uncertainty, yet we show they drive optimal exploration in opposite directions: volatility enhances it, stochasticity suppresses it. We establish this asymmetry formally by extending the Gittins index framework to Gaussian state-space bandits with latent dynamics. We further derive Cause-Aware Uncertainty-Sensitive Exploration (CAUSE), a closed-form exploration bonus obtained via control-as-inference that inherits the same monotonicities. CAUSE outperforms standard exploration strategies in environments with heterogeneous noise structure, and also improves on a Gittins-per-arm policy whose rested-bandit optimality does not transfer to restless settings. Learning and exploration are governed by the same noise-inference asymmetry, and the framework predicts that pathological noise inference produces \emph{reversed} rather than merely impaired exploration, with implications for computational accounts of psychiatric conditions.

</details>


### [87] [MOCHA: Multi-Objective Chebyshev Annealing for Agent Skill Optimization](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.19330)
*Md Mehrab Tanjim,Jayakumar Subramanian,Xiang Chen,Branislav Kveton,Subhojyoti Mukherjee,Anlan Zhang,Sungchul Kim,Somdeb Sarkhel,Sunav Choudhury*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文提出MOCHA方法解决LLM Agent技能的多目标优化问题。现有提示优化器在处理技能设计的多目标权衡（任务性能 vs. 平台约束）时存在不足，尤其是无法覆盖非凸Pareto前沿区域。MOCHA采用Chebyshev标量化替代加权求和以覆盖完整Pareto前沿，并结合指数退火实现从探索到利用的过渡。实验表明，在6个多样化Agent技能任务中，当现有方法在4个任务上1000次rollout后无改进时，MOCHA在所有任务上均实现突破，平均正确率相对提升7.5%（最高14.9%），并发现约两倍的Pareto最优变体。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: LLM agents rely on "skills" - structured natural-language specifications that control reasoning, retrieval, and responses. Skill optimization faces inherent multi-objective challenges: skills must simultaneously maximize task performance while satisfying platform constraints (description truncation for routing, compact instruction bodies, and limited context windows for co-resident skills). Existing prompt optimizers either ignore these trade-offs or use weighted-sum scalarization, which fails to discover Pareto-optimal variants in non-convex objective regions.

Method: MOCHA (Multi-Objective Chebyshev Achievement) employs two key innovations: (1) Chebyshev scalarization instead of weighted sum for multi-objective selection, enabling coverage of the full Pareto front including non-convex regions; (2) exponential annealing that transitions from exploration to exploitation during optimization. All methods in the experiment share the same multi-objective mutation operator and receive identical per-objective textual feedback.

Result: MOCHA achieves 7.5% relative improvement in mean correctness over the strongest baseline, with improvements reaching 14.9% on FEVER and 10.4% on TheoremQA. It also discovers approximately twice as many Pareto-optimal skill variants compared to existing optimizers.

Conclusion: MOCHA consistently outperforms existing optimizers across all six agent skills, achieving 7.5% average relative improvement in task correctness (up to 14.9% on FEVER, 10.4% on TheoremQA), and discovers approximately twice as many Pareto-optimal skill variants. The method effectively solves the multi-objective optimization problem for LLM agent skills by leveraging Chebyshev scalarization to cover the full Pareto front, including non-convex regions, combined with exponential annealing to balance exploration and exploitation. This breakthrough is significant given that existing methods fail to improve over the seed skill on 4 out of 6 tasks despite 1000 rollouts.

Abstract: LLM agents organize behavior through skills - structured natural-language specifications governing how an agent reasons, retrieves, and responds. Unlike monolithic prompts, skills are multi-field artifacts subject to hard platform constraints: description fields are truncated for routing, instruction bodies are compacted via progressive disclosure, and co-resident skills compete for limited context windows. These constraints make skill optimization inherently multi-objective: a skill must simultaneously maximize task performance and satisfy platform limits. Yet existing prompt optimizers either ignore these trade-offs or collapse them into a weighted sum, missing Pareto-optimal variants in non-convex objective regions. We introduce MOCHA (Multi-Objective Chebyshev Annealing), which replaces single-objective selection with Chebyshev scalarization - covering the full Pareto front, including non-convex regions - combined with exponential annealing that transitions from exploration to exploitation. In our experiments across six diverse agent skills - where all methods share the same multi-objective mutation operator and baselines receive identical per-objective textual feedback - existing optimizers fail to improve the seed skill on 4 of 6 tasks: 1000 rollouts yield zero progress. MOCHA breaks through on every task, achieving 7.5% relative improvement in mean correctness over the strongest baseline (up to 14.9% on FEVER and 10.4% on TheoremQA) while discovering twice as many more Pareto-optimal skill variants.

</details>


### [88] [Can Large Language Models Revolutionize Survey Research? Experiments with Disaster Preparedness Responses](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.19229)
*Yan Wang,Ziyi Guo,Christopher McCarty*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: The paper presents a five-stage LLM integration framework for survey research, proposes A-TLM (Anchored Marginal Theory-Informed LLM) that achieves superior imputation performance under MNAR conditions, and establishes PMT-based causal structure integration and subgroup-stratified bias auditing as key methodological contributions.


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: Survey research faces structural challenges: declining response rates, sample bias, block-wise missingness (MNAR), and fraudulent completions; existing LLM evaluations are not rigorous across full workflow, especially in disaster contexts

Method: Five-stage LLM integration framework; PMT-constrained co-occurrence knowledge graph; seven LLM configurations (zero-shot, RAG, theory-informed); comparison with IPW/MI, MICE+PMM, missForest baselines

Result: A-TLM outperforms all three classical baselines under block-wise MNAR conditions (RMSE 1.439 vs 1.496); achieves near-zero signed bias (-0.121); PMT causal structure retrieval outperforms unstructured RAG (MAE 0.993 vs 1.097); near-zero aggregate bias can mask opposing subgroup errors

Conclusion: A-TLM achieves best performance with near-zero signed bias and manageable hallucination; subgroup-stratified bias auditing proposed as standard; PMT causal structure integration outperforms unstructured retrieval

Abstract: Survey research faces mounting structural challenges: declining response rates, sample bias, block-wise missingness among at-risk respondents, and AI-assisted fraudulent completions in online panels. Large language models (LLMs) have been proposed as a remedy, yet rigorous evaluations across the full survey workflow remain scarce, particularly in disaster contexts where data quality matters most. We present and evaluate a five-stage framework for LLM integration covering questionnaire design, sample selection, pilot testing, missing-data imputation, and post-collection analysis, using the 2024 Hurricane Milton preparedness survey of Florida residents (n=946) as a shared empirical testbed. We introduce a Protection Motivation Theory (PMT)-constrained co-occurrence knowledge graph and develop seven LLM configurations spanning zero-shot inference, retrieval-augmented baselines, and novel theory-informed variants. Our proposed Anchored Marginal Theory-Informed LLM (A-TLM) outperforms all three classical imputation baselines (IPW/MI, MICE+PMM, missForest) on RMSE under disaster-relevant block-wise MNAR conditions (S4 RMSE 1.439 vs. 1.496 for the next-best), while achieving near-zero signed bias (-0.121) where the random-forest imputer produces the largest absolute bias (-0.631). Organizing retrieval around PMT causal structure and integrating all evidence in a single model call outperforms unstructured retrieval and staged sequential inference (MAE 0.993 vs. 1.097 for standard RAG). We document that near-zero aggregate bias can mask opposing subgroup errors and propose subgroup-stratified bias auditing as a reporting standard. A retrieval-constrained knowledge-graph chatbot demonstrates that hallucination is architecturally manageable through grounded refusal.

</details>


### [89] [Causal Evidence for Attention Head Imbalance in Modality Conflict Hallucination](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.19250)
*Jinrui Jiang,Zhangtai Wu,Zhen Wu,Xinyu Dai*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文研究多模态大语言模型(MLLMs)在视觉与文本信息冲突时产生幻觉的机制，通过因果分析发现了"驱动-抵抗"注意力头的不对称结构，并提出MACI干预方法有效缓解冲突幻觉。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 多模态大语言模型在视觉与文本信息冲突时，往往优先考虑错误的文本前提而非正确的视觉证据，导致幻觉问题。理解这一现象的内部机制对于提升模型可靠性至关重要。

Method: 使用path patching对5个开源MLLMs进行头部级别的因果分析，识别出两类因果角色相反的注意力头：幻觉驱动头和幻觉抵抗头。并通过消融实验验证其作用机制。

Result: 发现因果效应存在系统性不对称：驱动效应分布广泛且总体权重较大，而抵抗效应集中于少数高重要性头部。这种不均衡的路由结构使生成偏向错误前提。

Conclusion: 提出的MACI方法通过在检测到冲突时抑制幻觉驱动头，在5个MLLMs上于MMMC基准测试中实现最大幻觉降低，且可零样本迁移到SCI-SemanticConflict测试，兼顾幻觉减少与准确率权衡。

Abstract: Modality-conflict hallucination occurs when multimodal large language models (MLLMs) prioritize erroneous textual premises over contradictory visual evidence. To understand why visual evidence fails to prevail during generation, we take a mechanistic perspective and examine which internal components drive or resist this failure. We perform head-level causal analysis using path patching across five open-source MLLMs and identify two groups of attention heads with opposing causal roles: hallucination-driving heads and hallucination-resisting heads. We find a consistent asymmetry: driving effects are more broadly distributed and carry greater aggregate weight, whereas resisting effects concentrate in a small number of high-importance heads. Ablation experiments further confirm that these groups exert opposing effects during generation: distributed driving influence and localized resistance together form an imbalanced routing structure that biases generation toward the erroneous premise. Motivated by this finding, we propose MACI (Modality-conflict-Aware Causal Intervention), a conditional intervention that suppresses causally identified hallucination-driving heads only when conflict is detected. Across five MLLMs, MACI achieves the largest hallucination reduction among compared inference-time baselines on the MMMC benchmark with a favorable hallucination-accuracy trade-off, and transfers zero-shot to the SCI-SemanticConflict test.

</details>


### [90] [Towards Multi-Model LLM Schedulers: Empirical Insights into Offloading and Preemption](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.19593)
*Mert Yildiz,Pietro Spadaccino,Alexey Rolich,Francesca Cuomo,Andrea Baiocchi*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: This paper empirically studies multi-model LLM serving under GPU memory constraints, revealing that offloading leads to non-linear throughput degradation (smaller models more sensitive), preemption overhead is dominated by model state reload, and sequence length/interconnect bandwidth significantly impact performance. The findings guide the design of next-generation schedulers for heterogeneous multi-model workloads.


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: Existing LLM serving systems primarily optimize throughput for a single model, while multi-model scheduling under GPU memory constraints (requiring CPU-GPU offloading and preemption) remains underexplored.

Method: Empirical study of different LLMs across hardware platforms, focusing on performance implications of layer offloading and preemption.
