For speculative decoding, KVBuffer verifies draft tokens in parallel and avoids storing temporary states. For short contexts, KVBuffer computes attention outputs directly from buffered keys and values, without creating or updating the linear attention state. We implement KVBuffer in SGLang for Qwen3-Next. Our evaluations show that KVBuffer can reduce linear attention decoding latency by up to 45.17% and increase the maximum number of serving requests by 5x for speculative decoding when verifying four draft tokens.

</details>


### [191] [OScaR: The Occam's Razor for Extreme KV Cache Quantization in LLMs and Beyond](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.19660)
*Zunhai Su,Rui Yang,Chao Zhang,Yaxiu Liu,Yifan Zhang,Wei Wu,Jing Xiong,Dayou Du,Xialie Zhuang,Yulei Qian,Yuchen Xie,Yik-Chung Wu,Hongxia Yang,Ngai Wong*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: OScaR是一种用于X-LLMs（文本、多模态、全模态大模型）的轻量级KV缓存压缩框架，通过通道化旋转和全局token缩放策略解决Token Norm Imbalance问题，在INT2量化下实现近无损性能，显著提升解码速度和吞吐量。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 随着长上下文推理和多模态智能的发展，KV缓存的内存占用成为高效部署的主要瓶颈。现有per-channel量化方法在极端压缩下效果下降，核心问题在于Token Norm Imbalance (TNI)——当共享量化参数需跨越norm差异显著的token组时，会系统性放大误差。

Method: OScaR采用Canalized Rotation（通道化旋转）结合Omni-Token Scaling（全局token缩放）的策略，在per-channel范式基础上，通过缓解TNI引起的序列维度方差来提升量化精度。辅以优化的系统设计和CUDA内核实现。

Result: OScaR在X-LLMs上一致性地超越现有方法，INT2量化下达到近无损性能。相较于BF16 FlashDecoding-v2基线，解码速度提升3.0x，内存占用降低5.3x，吞吐量提升4.1x。

Conclusion: OScaR作为一个鲁棒、低复杂度、通用的KV缓存压缩框架，通过轻量级设计在INT2量化下实现近无损性能，并定义了新的帕累托前沿。

Abstract: The rapid advancement toward long-context reasoning and multi-modal intelligence has made the memory footprint of the Key-Value (KV) cache a dominant memory bottleneck for efficient deployment. While the established per-channel quantization effectively accommodates intrinsic channel-wise outliers in Key tensors, its efficacy diminishes under extreme compression. In this work, we revisit the inherent limitations of the per-channel quantization paradigm from both empirical and theoretical perspectives. Our analysis identifies Token Norm Imbalance (TNI) as the primary bottleneck to quantization fidelity. We demonstrate that TNI systematically amplifies errors when shared quantization parameters are required to span token groups exhibiting substantial norm disparities. Instead of relying on intricate quantization pipelines (e.g., TurboQuant), we propose OScaR (Omni-Scaled Canalized Rotation), an accurate and lightweight KV cache compression framework for X-LLMs (i.e., text-only, multi-modal, and omni-modal LLMs). Advancing the per-channel paradigm, OScaR employs Canalized Rotation followed by Omni-Token Scaling to mitigate TNI-induced sequence-dimensional variance both effectively and efficiently, further supported by our optimized system design and CUDA kernels. Extensive evaluations across X-LLMs show that OScaR consistently outperforms existing methods and achieves near-lossless performance under INT2 quantization, establishing it as a robust, low-complexity, and universal framework that defines a new Pareto front. Compared with the BF16 FlashDecoding-v2 baseline, our OScaR implementation achieves a notable up to 3.0x speedup in decoding, reduces memory footprint by 5.3x, and increases throughput by 4.1x. The code for OScaR is publicly available at https://github.com/ZunhaiSu/OScaR-KV-Quant.

</details>


### [192] [Mapping Uncharted Symmetries: Machine Discovery in Combinatorics](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.19063)
*Eugenio Cainelli,Lorenzo Luccioli,Alessandro Iraci,Michele D'Adderio,Giovanni Paolini*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文展示了机器学习可助力可验证的数学发现。提出 SLURP 框架及两种方法 MapSeek-Functional 和 MapSeek-Symbolic，成功应用于代数组合学，发现了 q,t-Narayana 多项式基于非交叉划分的新组合解释，并利用发现的统计量证明了此前未解决的对称性情况。所有发现已在 Lean 4 中形式化。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 代数组合学中长期存在悬而未决的数学问题，研究者希望探索现代机器学习是否能对可验证的数学发现做出贡献。具体而言，需要解决在严格比例分布约束下构造简单数学函数的问题。

Method: 提出了两种方法：(1) MapSeek-Functional，通过交替进行伪标签建模和监督训练的步骤来建模目标函数；(2) MapSeek-Symbolic，设计用于直接生成符号公式。将这两种方法应用于代数组合学中的研究问题。

Result: 发现了 q,t-Narayana 多项式的一个新组合解释，这是首个基于非交叉划分的解释；利用发现的一个统计量，成功给出了多项式对称性的组合证明。

Conclusion: 成功发现了 q,t-Narayana 多项式的新组合解释，这是首个基于非交叉划分的解释，并利用发现的统计量给出了此前未解决情况下多项式对称性的组合证明。所有数学发现均在 Lean 4 中形式化，以确保可验证性和可复现性。

Abstract: Inspired by long-standing open problems in algebraic combinatorics, we show that modern machine learning can meaningfully contribute to verifiable mathematical discoveries. In particular, we focus on the construction of simple mathematical functions under exact distributional constraints, a setting we formalize as Simple Learning Under Rigid Proportions (SLURP). We tackle this problem by introducing two methods: MapSeek-Functional, which models the desired function alternating pseudo-labeling and supervised training steps; and MapSeek-Symbolic, designed to directly produce symbolic formulas. We successfully apply both methods to a research problem in algebraic combinatorics, discovering a new combinatorial interpretation of the $q,t$-Narayana polynomials arising from representation theory. To our knowledge, this is the first such interpretation based on noncrossing partitions. Using one discovered statistic, we find a combinatorial proof of the symmetry of these polynomials in a previously unsolved case. To streamline verification and reproducibility, we release all code, including a formalization of all the mathematical discoveries of this paper in Lean 4.

</details>


### [193] [The impact of observation density on Bayesian inversion of latent dynamics in shock-dominated flows](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.19076)
*Bipin Tiwari,Muhammad Abid,Omer San*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: Summary generation failed


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: Motivation analysis unavailable

Method: Method extraction failed

Result: Result analysis unavailable

Conclusion: Conclusion extraction failed

Abstract: Inferring unknown initial states in shock-dominated compressible flows from sparse and noisy measurements is a challenging ill-posed inverse problem due to nonlinear wave interactions and limited sensing. In this work, we develop a non-intrusive reduced-order modeling framework for efficient Bayesian initial-state inversion with uncertainty quantification. The framework combines a convolutional autoencoder with a learned latent-space forward operator. The autoencoder compresses high-dimensional flow fields into a compact nonlinear latent representation, while the forward operator predicts final-time latent states from encoded initial conditions. This AE-ROM surrogate enables rapid forward evaluations and is embedded within a No-U-Turn Sampler (NUTS) for posterior exploration. The framework is demonstrated using 500 high-fidelity Sod shock tube simulations generated through Latin hypercube sampling and solved using a fifth-order WENO scheme. The inverse problem seeks to recover unknown left and right density and pressure states from sparse noisy observations of final-time density and pressure fields. Results show that the AE-ROM accurately reconstructs key shock-tube structures, including the rarefaction wave, contact discontinuity, and shock front. A latent dimension of 32 provides an effective balance between reconstruction accuracy and reduced-space compactness, while 250 training simulations are sufficient for accurate reconstruction. Increasing observation density significantly contracts posterior uncertainty, reducing the mean posterior standard deviation by approximately 78% for density and 76% for pressure. Overall, the proposed framework provides a computationally efficient and uncertainty-aware approach for inverse analysis of shock-dominated flows, with potential extensions to multidimensional compressible-flow and digital-twin applications.

</details>


### [194] [A Measure-Theoretic Analysis of Reasoning: Structural Generalization and Approximation Limits](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.19944)
*Yuyang Zhang,Yifu Zhang,Xuehai Zhou,Xiaoyin Chen*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 该论文从最优传输理论出发，建立了Transformer架构O零样本泛化的理论框架，证明了旋转变embedding相比绝对位置编码在泛化上的理论优势，并揭示了电路深度下界与Barron空间逼近限制对模型扩展性的根本性约束。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: LLM的经验缩放定律已被广泛记录，但其O零样本泛化的理论机制尚不明确，现有方法缺乏对分布外性能的系统性理论解释。

Method: 基于最优传输理论形式化推理过程，将离散轨迹投影到连续度量空间，利用Wasserstein-1距离量化分布偏移，通过Kantorovich对偶性建立O零样本泛化界。核心工具包括：架构Lipschitz连续性分析、Barron空间中的函数逼近理论、将序列回溯映射为Dyck-k语言以证明电路下界。

Result: 证明位置依赖型注意力（如绝对位置编码）无法保持平移不变性，Lipschitz常数为Ω(1)；而旋转变 embedding则保持等变性并可约束误差。通过Dyck-k语言映射建立了TC^0 Transformers的严格电路深度下界，表明Barron空间中不可约的逼近界限使宽度缩放无法替代深度缩放。54个Transformer配置的组合搜索实验验证了风险随Wasserstein距离单调递增的结论。

Conclusion: Rotary Embeddings配合适当的物理层深度扩展是实现O零样本泛化的必要条件，Width Scaling无法替代深度缩放。

Abstract: While empirical scaling laws for LLM reasoning are well-documented, the theoretical mechanisms governing out-of-distribution (OOD) generalization remain elusive. We formalize reasoning via optimal transport, projecting discrete trajectories into a continuous metric space to quantify domain shifts using the Wasserstein-1 distance. Invoking Kantorovich duality, we bound OOD generalization via architectural Lipschitz continuity and functional approximation limits. This exposes two primary constraints. First, position-dependent attention (e.g., Absolute Positional Encoding) fails to preserve shift invariance, yielding an $Ω(1)$ Lipschitz constant and expected risk, whereas shift-invariant mechanisms (e.g., Rotary Embeddings) preserve equivariance and bound the error. Second, by mapping sequential backtracking to a Dyck-$k$ language, we establish a strict circuit depth lower bound for $\text{TC}^0$ Transformers. Scaling physical layer depth is necessary to avert representation collapse -- a constraint that scaling representation width cannot bypass due to irreducible approximation bounds in Barron spaces. Evaluations across 54 Transformer configurations on combinatorial search corroborate these bounds, demonstrating that generalization risk degrades monotonically with the Wasserstein domain shift.

</details>


### [195] [MANGO: Meta-Adaptive Network Gradient Optimization for Online Continual Learning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.19080)
*Ankita Awasthi,Marco Apolinario,Kaushik Roy*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: MANGO是一种在线持续学习框架，通过梯度门控和元学习正则化平衡稳定性和可塑性，利用回放样本作为训练信号和遗忘评估器，在多个基准数据集上达到最优性能。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 在线持续学习(OCL)面临核心挑战：克服对过去任务的灾难性遗忘(稳定性)的同时高效学习新任务(可塑性)。现有方法存在局限：回放引入样本偏差、蒸馏无法调节参数更新、固定正则化忽视参数敏感性、流数据元学习缺乏反馈控制的参数更新。

Method: MANGO采用两大核心机制：1) 梯度门控(Gradient-gating)：基于参数敏感性对更新进行缩放，防止破坏性更新；2) 元学习正则化(Meta-learned regularization)：自适应调整稳定性系数，评估参数更新对回放样本的影响。在该框架中，回放不仅作为训练信号，还充当遗忘评估器，弥补了现有方法缺乏反馈控制的参数更新机制的缺陷。

Result: 在三个标准OCL基准数据集上，MANGO优于强基线方法，获得最先进结果，在不同回放大小下性能一致。在CLEAR-10领域增量学习和CIFAR-100、Tiny-ImageNet类别增量学习中均取得最高准确率，并在CLEAR-10上实现正向反向迁移，有效克服遗忘。

Conclusion: MANGO通过梯度门控和元学习正则化的创新结合，实现了最先进的性能。在CLEAR-10、CIFAR-100和Tiny-ImageNet三个基准数据集上，MANGO均超越所有基线方法，且在不同回放大小下保持一致的性能表现，成功解决了灾难性遗忘问题。

Abstract: In Online Continual Learning (OCL), a neural network sequentially learns from a non-stationary data stream in a single-pass with access only to a limited memory replay buffer. This contrasts sharply with off-line continual learning where training is multiple epoch dependent on large datasets. The main challenge faced by OCL is to overcome catastrophic forgetting of past tasks (stability) while learning new ones efficiently (plasticity). Existing methods counter forgetting via replay-based rehearsal, output level distillation, fixed regularization, or meta-learning on the current data. However, these methods have limitations: rehearsal introduces a stored sample bias; distillation operates on output-distributions without modulating parameter updates; fixed-regularization penalizes parameters irrespective of sensitivity; stream-only meta-learning lacks a feedback controlled parameter update. We propose Meta-Adaptive Network Gradient Optimization (MANGO), an OCL framework that balances stability-plasticity via gradient-gating and meta-learned regularization. Gradient-gating scales parameter updates based on sensitivity, preventing destructive updates. Meta-learned regularization adapts stability coefficients, evaluating the effect of parameter update on replay. In MANGO, replay acts as both a training signal and a forgetting evaluator. We evaluated our method on three standard OCL benchmark datasets. MANGO outperforms strong baselines, achieving state-of-the-art results with consistent performance across replay sizes. In domain incremental learning on CLEAR-10 and class incremental learning on CIFAR-100 and Tiny-ImageNet, it achieves highest accuracy among all baselines and achieves positive Backward Transfer, overcoming forgetting on CLEAR-10.

</details>


### [196] [Chessformer: A Unified Architecture for Chess Modeling](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.19091)
*Daniel Monroe,George Eilender,Philip Chalmers,Zhenwei Tang,Ashton Anderson*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: Chessformer proposes a unified encoder-only transformer architecture that simultaneously advances playing strength, human move prediction, and interpretability in chess by aligning model design (square tokens, geometric attention, policy head) with the domain's inherent structure.


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: Chess modeling tasks (playing strength, human play prediction, interpretability) have been addressed with disparate architectures that are often misaligned with the domain's geometry, raising the question of whether a unified architecture could support all three objectives simultaneously.

Method: Chessformer: an encoder-only transformer with three key design choices: (1) representing board squares as tokens, (2) augmenting self-attention with Geometric Attention Bias (GAB) for dynamic positional encoding, and (3) using an attention-based source-destination policy head for action prediction.

Result: Three major achievements: (1) \maiathree achieves 57.1% move-matching accuracy, surpassing prior SOTA with fewer than a quarter of the parameters; (2) integration into Leela Chess Zero adds over 100 Elo and achieves tournament victories over Stockfish; (3) square-token design enables granular interpretability through attributable attention patterns and activations.

Conclusion: Aligning a model's tokenization, positional encoding, and output design with the underlying structure of a domain can yield simultaneous gains in performance, human compatibility, and interpretability.

Abstract: Chess has long served as a canonical testbed for artificial intelligence, but modeling approaches for its central tasks have diverged. Maximizing playing strength, predicting human play, and enabling interpretability are typically solved with disparate architectures, and these designs are often misaligned with the geometry of the domain. This raises the natural question of whether these objectives require separate modeling paradigms, or if there exists a single architecture that supports them simultaneously. We introduce Chessformer, a unified architecture that advances the state of the art on all three central goals in chess modeling. Chessformer is an encoder-only transformer that represents board squares as tokens, augments self-attention with a novel dynamic positional encoding called Geometric Attention Bias (GAB) that adapts to domain-specific geometry, and predicts actions with an attention-based source-destination policy head. We evaluate Chessformer on each front. First, we develop \maiathree, a family of models for human move prediction that reaches 57.1\% move-matching accuracy, significantly surpassing the previous state of the art with fewer than a quarter of the parameters. Second, we integrate Chessformer into Leela Chess Zero, a leading open-source engine, adding over 100 Elo of playing strength and resulting in tournament victories over Stockfish in major computer chess competitions. Third, we show that Chessformer's square-token design makes attention patterns and activations directly attributable to board squares, enabling granular interpretability analyses that prior architectures do not naturally support. More broadly, our results demonstrate that aligning a model's tokenization, positional encoding, and output design with the underlying structure of a domain can yield simultaneous gains in performance, human compatibility, and interpretability.

</details>


### [197] [ScheduleFree+: Scaling Learning-Rate-Free & Schedule-Free Learning to Large Language Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.19095)
*Aaron Defazio*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出ScheduleFree+方法，解决Schedule-Free Learning在大型语言模型训练中的规模化问题，在1000 tokens per parameter训练规模下超越SOTA调度器31%，并为预训练中的模型平均和检查点合并提供了理论基础。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: Schedule-Free Learning在小规模LLM训练中已证明有效，但在大批量和大模型场景下性能不足；现有WSD调度器在长时训练中表现不佳；实际训练中需要learning-rate-free和schedule-free的简化方案。

Method: 识别大规模场景下的核心问题，通过一系列修复使Schedule-Free Learning能够规模化；方法结合模型平均机制，移除显式学习率调度。

Result: ScheduleFree+在1000 tokens per parameter训练规模下显著优于WSD调度器，性能提升31%。

Conclusion: Schedule-Free Learning特别适合长时训练场景，其理论框架为预训练过程中的模型平均和检查点合并提供了合理性支撑，具有重要的实践价值。

Abstract: Schedule-Free Learning has shown promise as a practical anytime training method for machine learning, showing success across dozens of standard benchmark problems. However, strong performance for LLM training has only been demonstrated at small scales. We identify a number of fixes necessary to scale up Schedule-Free Learning to larger batch sizes and model sizes, and present a learning-rate-free and schedule-free method (ScheduleFree+) for training large language models which greatly outperforms Warmup-Stable-Decay (WSD) schedules. We also demonstrate that Schedule-Free Learning is most effective for long duration training, and at 1000 tokens per parameter, it outperforms SOTA schedules by 31%. Schedule-Free Learning provides a theoretical foundation for the use of model averaging and checkpoint merging during pretraining.

</details>


### [198] [Performance Monitoring of Proton Exchange Membrane Water Electrolyzer by Transformers-Based Machine Learning Model](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.19107)
*Bingqing Chen,Ivan Batalov,Qiu Chen,Weiqi Ji,Lei Cheng*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 提出一种基于Transformer编码器-解码器架构的机器学习框架，在PEM电解槽正常运行期间虚拟重建极化曲线，实现无需停机的实时健康状态监测，在478小时测试中MSE降低10倍。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: PEM电解槽规模化部署需要实时健康状态评估，但现有电化学测试协议需中断正常运行；全尺寸堆栈部署中缺乏实用的在线状态监测手段，限制了对系统退化进行实时评估的能力。

Method: 采用基于补丁的序列token化方法将操作数据分割并编码为有意义的token，训练编码器-解码器Transformer模型，以运行数据为条件虚拟重建极化曲线等表征输出。

Result: 在四个纵向测试运行（不同测试电池和加载循环，最长达478小时）中，模型准确重建极化曲线，与普通Transformer相比均方误差降低10倍。

Conclusion: ML模型可实现PEM电解槽的持续性能监测，编码器捕获了有意义的健康状态潜在表示，为未来开发可解释性指标奠定基础。

Abstract: Green hydrogen plays an essential role in decarbonization, with capacity projected to scale to 560 GW by 2030 (vs. 1.39 GW in 2023) in net-zero settings. Proton exchange membrane (PEM) electrolysis is one of the most promising technology routes to green hydrogen production, and real-time system health monitoring of PEM electrolyzers is essential for their scalable deployment. In lab settings, performance degradation can be characterized through electrochemical testing protocols by periodic pauses of normal operation. Such interruption is not practical for full-scale stack deployments, limiting system operators' ability to make real-time assessments of state-of-health (SoH). We present a machine learning (ML) framework that performs virtual electrochemical characterization during normal operation. The method uses an encoder-decoder transformer, conditioned on operational data, to reconstruct characterization outputs, focusing here on polarization curves. Inspired by patch-based sequence tokenization, we segment the inputs into patches and encode them to form meaningful tokens, which substantially improves learning efficiency. Across four longitudinal runs, lasting up to 478 hours on different test cells and loading cycles, the model accurately reconstructed polarization curves and achieved 10x reduction in mean squared error (MSE) compared to a vanilla transformer. This proof-of-concept demonstrates that ML models can enable continuous performance monitoring for PEM electrolyzers and that the encoder captures meaningful latent representations of SoH, opening up opportunities to derive interpretable indicators in future work.

</details>


### [199] [CLIC: Contextual Language-Informed Cardiac Pathology Classification](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.19132)
*Giovani D. Lucafo,Rafael da Costa Silva,João Lucas Luz Lima Sarcinelli,Andre Guarnier De Mitri,Diego Furtado Silva*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: This paper presents CLIC, a multimodal framework that enhances ECG-based cardiac pathology classification by encoding patient demographics and technical metadata into natural language. The approach shows that template-based contextual text outperforms LLM-generated descriptions, demonstrating the clinical value of integrating non-signal patient data into automated diagnosis.


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: Current automated ECG classifiers rely solely on signal analysis, but cardiologists in clinical practice also leverage patient characteristics and data-acquisition context. Existing algorithms fail to integrate technical metadata and demographic variables.

Method: Proposed CLIC (Contextual Language-Informed Cardiac pathology classification) framework that encodes patient contextual data through natural language descriptions. Investigated both LLM-synthesized clinical text and template-based contextual text generation approaches.

Result: Multimodal framework significantly enhances diagnostic precision. Template-based contextual clinical text leads to consistent improvements in downstream classification performance, outperforming LLM-generated descriptions.

Conclusion: Controlled template-based contextual clinical text outperforms LLM-generated descriptions for downstream classification. The proposed CLIC framework demonstrates the clinical value of integrating non-signal contextual data into cardiac pathology classification.

Abstract: The electrocardiogram (ECG) is the gold standard for non-invasive diagnosis of cardiac pathologies and is a fundamental pillar of cardiovascular medicine. Recent progress in deep learning has led to the development of robust automated classifiers that achieve high performance by processing raw physiological signals. However, in clinical practice, diagnosis is rarely based solely on the signal. Cardiologists commonly support their interpretation with the patient's characteristics and the specific data-acquisition context. Despite this, most current algorithms remain restricted to signal-only analysis, failing to integrate technical metadata and demographic variables. This paper proposes Contextual Language-Informed Cardiac pathology classification (CLIC), a multimodal framework that significantly enhances diagnostic precision by encoding these variables through natural language. We demonstrate that translating patient-level contextual data into descriptive text provides an informative anchor that helps the model disambiguate complex physiological patterns. We further investigate the use of Large Language Models to synthesize richer clinical descriptions and observe that, while these generated texts remain competitive, controlled template-based contextual clinical text leads to consistent improvements in downstream classification performance.

</details>


### [200] [Identifiable Multimodal Causal Representation Learning under Partial Latent Sharing](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.19135)
*Manal Benhamza,Marianne Clausel,Myriam Tami*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 论文研究多模态场景下的因果表征学习identifiability问题。每个模态由非线性混合函数从特定的潜在因果变量子集生成。在灵活的非参数假设下，证明了成分级别的可辨识性，并提出基于Wasserstein距离的方法进行实现。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 因果表征学习（CRL） identifiability（可辨识性）是关键属性，确保能从数据生成过程中恢复机制，实现表征的可解释性和鲁棒性。在多模态场景下证明identifiability尤其困难。

Method: 引入基于Wasserstein距离的模块来恢复部分共享的潜在结构。该模块具有可微性，可轻松集成到各类架构中，只需极少的改动。

Result: 在不给定潜在变量参数分布的灵活假设下，建立了成分级别的causal latent representation可辨识性保证，且该结果适用于欠完备场景（每种模态的观测变量数多于潜在变量数）。

Conclusion: 所提方法在合成和真实数据集上超越了当前最优方法，验证了理论分析的有效性。

Abstract: Causal representation learning (CRL) seeks to uncover meaningful latent variables and their corresponding causal structure from high-dimensional observational data. Although its significance, CRL identifiability remains a crucial property, as it ensures the recovery of the mechanisms behind the data generation process, and hence the interpretability and robustness of the representation. Proving identifiability in CRL is intrinsically difficult, and we address in this work an even more challenging setting: multimodality. We consider multimodal observed data with a latent partially shared structure. Each modality is generated, through non linear mixing functions, from a specific subset of causal latent variables. Under flexible assumptions and without imposing any parametric distribution on the latent variables, we establish component-wise identifiability guarantees for the causal latent representation. Our identifiability results, furthermore, apply to the undercomplete scenario where we have, for each modality, more observed than latent variables. To instantiate our theoretical analysis, we introduce a Wasserstein-based module to recover the partially shared latent structure. Due to its differentiability, the latter can be easily integrated into all types of architecture, only requiring minimal changes. Extensive experiments on synthetic and realistic datasets validate the superiority of our approach over SOTA methods.

</details>


### [201] [PMF-CL: Pareto-Minimal-Forgetting Continual Learner for Conflicting Tasks](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.19145)
*Srijith Nair,Atilla Eryilmaz,Jia,Liu*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出PMF-CL框架，通过多任务学习视角寻找Pareto最优解来解决持续学习中的灾难性遗忘问题。对于二次问题，算法具有O(d²)的存储复杂度。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有持续学习算法虽能缓解灾难性遗忘，但缺乏对"需要存储何种信息"这一根本问题的系统性理解。现有方法假设存在任务间的全局最优解，但实际中任务很少共享全局最优解，导致遗忘不可避免。因此需要一种原则性的方法来系统性地处理冲突任务间的遗忘问题。

Method: 提出基于Pareto优化的PMF-CL框架。核心思想是在Pareto意义上寻找"最小遗忘"的解。具体方法包括：(1) 为线性回归和基函数回归推导出Pareto最小遗忘算法；(2) 为具有二次上界的广义损失函数（如逻辑回归）推导出相应算法；(3) 采用静态内存的迭代更新策略，内存占用为O(d²)，其中d为模型参数数量。

Result: 建立了持续学习问题从Pareto优化角度的形式化框架，为处理任务冲突提供了原则性和系统性的方法论基础。

Conclusion: 该框架为理解和解决灾难性遗忘提供了新的理论视角，通过Pareto最优解的概念，在无法完全避免遗忘的现实条件下，找到了"最小遗忘"的帕累托最优解，是一种具有坚实理论基础的持续学习方法。

Abstract: In the literature, many continual learning (CL) algorithms have been proposed to address the issue of catastrophic forgetting in ML models (i.e., learning new tasks leads to the loss of performance on previously learned tasks). Although all CL approaches use some form of memory to retain information about past tasks, a grounded understanding of what information needs to be stored to minimize catastrophic forgetting remains elusive. Recently, it has been recognized that under the strong assumption of the existence of a common global minimizer over all tasks, catastrophic forgetting can be completely avoided. However, in practice, tasks rarely have a common global minimizer, and a certain amount of forgetting is inevitable. In this paper, we propose a foundational framework for principled and systematic CL of conflicting tasks using a multi-task learning (MTL) perspective. The approach is based on finding Pareto-optimal solutions, i.e., the solutions which, by definition, minimally forget the previous tasks in the Pareto sense. We derive Pareto-minimal-forgetting CL algorithms for linear and basis-function regression, and general loss functions which have a quadratic upper bound, e.g., logistic regression. For quadratic problems, PMF-CL uses memory-efficient iterative updates with a static memory footage of $\mathcal{O}(d^2)$ for models with $d$ parameters.

</details>


### [202] [Lost and Found in Translation: Variational Diagnostics for Neural Codebook Channels](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.18846)
*Yusuke Hayashi*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出神经码本信道（neural codebook channel）$K_{e\to d}$，用于诊断变分自编码器（VAE）中编码器与解码器是否对潜在空间采用一致的解释。通过架构无关的Bernoulli-KL证书bounded耦合，揭示了传统诊断指标（ELBO、互信息、直方图等）无法检测"不匹配解码"这一经典通信理论中的失效模式。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 变分自编码器虽通过联合训练编码器$q_φ$和解码器$p_θ$获得隐空间，但现有诊断工具（ELBO、激活单元、互信息、码字直方图）仅能验证码字是否被使用，无法判断解码器是否按照编码器的码本解读每个潜在变量——即编码器-解码器不一致问题长期被忽视。

Method: 提出神经码本信道$K_{e\to d}(j\mid i)$作为编码器-解码器耦合诊断工具，核心依赖：1) Bernoulli-KL证书：$d_{\mathrm{bin}}(1-\mathcal{A} \,\|\, \barη_p) \le \barΔ$，由变分间隙控制；2) 边际不可能性结果：证明任何边际统计量（直方图、熵、激活码数、互信息）组合均无法确定$K_{e\to d}$。

Result: 在多场景验证：(1) sklearn数据集5/5 seeds、20/20 encoder-decoder pairs均满足边界；(2) 2D模型在$2.71\times$观察到的不一致处仍保持非平凡边界，四项恒等式收敛至$10^{-4}$；(3) MNIST importance sampling控制验证；(4) VQ-VAE达到预测极限$\hat{\mathcal{A}}=1.000$。

Conclusion: 神经码本信道$K_{e\to d}$及配套报告单元$(K_{e\to d}, \mathcal{A}, R_{\mathrm{eff}}, R, \mathrm{AU})$为VAE提供了可审计的编码器-解码器一致性诊断，使经典通信理论中的"不匹配解码"失效模式在单个深度生成模型中变得可见。

Abstract: Classical communication systems fail not only through random noise but also when transmitter and receiver use incompatible operational codebooks. Variational autoencoders (VAEs) train an encoder $q_φ$ and decoder $p_θ$ jointly, and practitioners treat the resulting latent space as a discrete code -- for clustering, conditional generation, and mechanistic interpretability. Yet standard VAE diagnostics -- ELBO, active units, mutual information, and code histograms -- certify only whether this code is used, never whether the decoder reads each latent under the encoder's code.
  We close this gap with the neural codebook channel $K_{e\to d}(j\mid i)$, a coupled encoder-decoder diagnostic whose off-diagonal mass is bounded by an architecture-free Bernoulli-KL certificate $d_{\mathrm{bin}}(1-\mathcal{A} \,\|\, \barη_p) \le \barΔ$ controlled by the variational gap. The certificate is the operational specialization of the classical KL chain rule under disintegration to the encoder-decoder disagreement event, complemented by a constructive marginal-impossibility result: no combination of marginal histograms, entropies, active-code counts, or mutual information determines $K_{e\to d}$.
  We audit the certificate on four sklearn datasets (finite-grid exact, 5/5 seeds, 20/20 pairs satisfy the bound), a 2D model where the bound is non-vacuous at $2.71\times$ the observed disagreement and the four-term identity closes within $10^{-4}$, MNIST under importance-sampling control, and a VQ-VAE attaining the predicted limit $\hat{\mathcal{A}}=1.000$. The package $(K_{e\to d}, \mathcal{A}, R_{\mathrm{eff}}, R, \mathrm{AU})$ is an audit-ready reporting unit. More broadly, the framework makes mismatched decoding -- a failure mode classical communication theory named decades ago -- visible inside a single deep generative model.

</details>


### [203] [On-Device Continual Learning with Dual-Stage Buffer and Dynamic Loss for Point-of-Care Pneumonia Diagnosis](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.19201)
*Danu Kim*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: PneumoNet是一种针对资源受限环境中即时医疗肺炎诊断的领域增量学习方案，通过轻量级CNN、双阶段平衡缓冲区和动态类加权损失解决领域偏移问题，在PneumoniaMNIST数据集上实现86.6%准确率和1.4%遗忘率。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 深度学习模型在肺炎检测中性能下降，原因在于设备、患者或机构差异导致的领域偏移，这在资源受限环境中尤为挑战。

Method: PneumoNet采用领域增量学习方法，结合轻量级CNN（用于设备端预测）、双阶段平衡缓冲区（实现类平衡重放）和动态类加权损失（纠正训练批次不平衡）。在模拟五个现实领域变化场景的PneumoniaMNIST数据集上进行评估。

Result: PneumoNet在领域偏移的PneumoniaMNIST数据集上达到86.6%准确率，遗忘率仅1.4%，且模型更小、推理速度更快。

Conclusion: PneumoNet展现出在资源受限环境中实现自适应、隐私保护的医疗AI诊断的潜力，适用于真实世界场景和大流行准备就绪的医疗环境。

Abstract: Deep learning models detect pneumonia from chest X-rays with high accuracy, but the performance declines under domain shifts caused by differences in devices, patients, or institutions. We present PneumoNet, a domain-incremental learning method for point-of-care pneumonia diagnosis in resource-limited settings. PneumoNet combines a lightweight CNN for on-device prediction, a dual-stage balanced buffer for class-balanced replay, and a dynamic class-weighted loss to correct training-batch imbalances. Evaluated on a domain-shifted PneumoniaMNIST dataset simulating five realistic domain change scenarios, PneumoNet achieves 86.6% accuracy with 1.4% forgetting while being smaller and faster than existing baselines. These results highlight PneumoNet's potential to enable adaptive, privacy-preserving diagnostic AI directly on point-of-care medical devices in real-world and pandemic-ready healthcare.

</details>


### [204] [Worst-Group Equalized Odds Regularization for Multi-Attribute Fair Medical Image Classification](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.19214)
*Nikhil Cherian Kurian,Victor Caquilpan Parra,Abin Shoby,Luke Whitbread,Lauren Oakden-Rayner,Robert Vandersluis,Jessica Schrouff,Lyle J. Palmer,Mark Jenkinson*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 论文提出了一种最差组等概率边际正则化器（WGE-ORM），用于在医学影像AI中同时处理多个人口统计学属性（如年龄、性别、种族）的公平性问题。该方法在推理时识别边际偏差最严重的子组并进行惩罚，在两个医学影像数据集的多标签场景中有效减少了等概率偏差，同时保持了诊断性能。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有子组AUC指标可能掩盖临床重要差异：当某些组表现出过度诊断（高TPR和高FPR）而另一些组表现出不足诊断（低TPR和低FPR）时，聚合AUC可能相互抵消，导致临床决策中的不平等被忽视。需要一种能在固定推理运行点和多个人口统计学属性上同时评估和缓解不公平性的方法。

Method: 提出最差组等概率边际正则化器（WGE-ORM）：在每次更新时识别由显式人口统计学属性（如年龄、性别、种族）定义的边际偏差最严重的子组，应用统一惩罚，无需显式交约束即可实现多人口统计学轴的公平优化。

Result: 在两个医学影像数据集的多标签设置中，该方法持续降低等概率偏差（Equalized Odds）和等机会偏差（Equalized Opportunity），对AUC影响极小，在保持诊断性能的同时提升了公平性。

Conclusion: WGE-ORM能够在实际多标签医学影像场景中有效实现跨多个人口统计学属性的公平性优化，无需穷举所有交子组，为医学AI的公平部署提供了实用的推理时解决方案。

Abstract: Diagnostic performance in medical AI varies systematically across demographic groups, yet subgroup AUC can mask clinically important disparities. At a fixed inference-time operating point, some groups may exhibit over-diagnostic behaviour, characterized by elevated true and false positive rates, while others show under-diagnostic patterns with reduced true and false positive rates. These opposing tendencies can cancel in aggregate AUCs while producing meaningful inequities in clinical decision-making. Motivated by the need to assess and mitigate such disparities at the operating point and across multiple demographic attributes simultaneously, we propose a worst-group equalized-odds margin regularizer. The proposed regularizer explicitly targets subgroup-level deviations on both the true positive and false positive sides at inference. At each update, the method identifies subgroups defined by explicit demographic attributes (e.g., age, sex, and race) that exhibit the most extreme margin deviations and applies a unified penalty, enabling fairness optimization across multiple demographic axes without requiring explicit intersectional constraints. Across two medical imaging datasets in realistic multi-label settings, our method consistently reduces disparities in Equalized Odds and Equalized Opportunity with minimal impact on AUC, preserving diagnostic performance while improving fairness.

</details>


### [205] [DeRegiME: Deep Regime Mixtures for Probabilistic Forecasting under Distribution Shift](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.19231)
*Kieran Wood,Stefan Zohren,Stephen J. Roberts*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: DeRegiME是一种直接多步概率预测器，通过稀疏变分GP将潜在不确定性regime与底层信号分离，利用非平稳regime-mixing核和Student-t似然实现可解释的mean-residual-noise分解，在10个基准上显著优于现有方法。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 解决神经预测器的关键局限：点预测丢弃残差不确定性；现有概率预测头（单边际、不可解释的混合、量子集、扩散样本）很少暴露残差的regime结构。实际中分布偏移（ abrupt、gradual、horizon-dependent）常出现于残差不确定性而非条件均值。

Method: 使用稀疏变分高斯过程（GP）实现，核心包括：非平稳 regime-mixing 核 + Student-t 似然，通过共享门控组合 per-regime 子核与噪声过程，形成单一稀疏GP后验（非GP专家混合）。采用直接和特征空间表示，将regime锚定为残差相似性聚类，隐式变点通过regime转换呈现。

Result: 在10个基准数据集、3种编码器网格上：NLPD相对最强基线提升20.3%，CRPS提升3.0%，MSE提升4.7%。提升在所有数据集（包含abrupt、gradual、seasonal shifts）上保持一致。

Conclusion: 通过stick-breaking门机制实现有效 regime 数量的自动剪枝；在10个基准数据集和3种编码器网格上，NLPD提升20.3%，CRPS提升3.0%，MSE提升4.7%，性能提升在各类型分布偏移（ abrupt、gradual、seasonal shifts）中保持一致。

Abstract: We introduce DeRegiME -- Deep Regime Mixture of Experts -- a direct multi-horizon probabilistic forecaster that separates latent uncertainty regimes from the underlying signal and softly assigns each forecast location to learned recurring regimes using a sparse variational Gaussian process (GP) whose nonstationary regime-mixing kernel and Student-t likelihood combine per-regime sub-kernels and noise processes via a shared gate. This yields a single sparse-GP posterior, not a mixture of GP experts. DeRegiME addresses a key limitation of neural forecasters: point forecasts discard residual uncertainty, and probabilistic heads -- whether single marginals, uninterpreted mixtures, quantile sets, or diffusion samples -- rarely expose the regime structure of the residual. Yet distribution shift in noisy heteroskedastic time series may be abrupt, gradual, or horizon-dependent and often appears in residual uncertainty rather than the conditional mean. DeRegiME yields an interpretable mean-residual-noise decomposition with a direct-sum feature-space representation that anchors regimes as clusters of residual similarity whose transitions surface as implicit changepoints. The effective number of regimes is pruned by the stick-breaking gate. We prove kernel validity and predictive-density propriety, and across ten benchmarks and three encoder grids DeRegiME improves negative log predictive density (NLPD) by 20.3% over the strongest encoder-matched baseline, a DeepAR/GluonTS-style dynamic Student-t head, with parallel gains on CRPS (3.0%) and MSE (4.7%). Improvements are consistent across all datasets, which span abrupt, gradual, and seasonal shifts.

</details>


### [206] [GAE Falls Short in Imperfect-Information Self-Play Reinforcement Learning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.19235)
*Zhiyuan Fan,Gabriele Farina*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 针对不完全信息博弈中均衡自博弈的方差放大问题，提出基于Q-boosting的方差缩减优势估计和VRPO算法，通过Expected SARSA(λ)迹平均化动作采样噪声，在斗地主等大型博弈游戏中展现了强劲性能。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 竞争性多智能体强化学习在不完全信息博弈中面临部分可观测性和对抗性对手的挑战，需要随机策略。标准广义优势估计(GAE)在均衡自博弈中因采样随机未来动作产生额外方差，即使批评器精确该方差也会持续存在，构成性能瓶颈。

Method: 提出基于集中式动作价值批评器的Q-boosting方差缩减优势估计，用多步Expected SARSA(λ)迹替代采样多步备份以计算策略期望平均化动作采样噪声，同时保留PPO的裁剪目标和在线策略演员更新，形成VRPO算法。

Result: VRPO在斗地主和无限注德州扑克等中型到大型不完全信息博弈中始终取得强劲性能，验证了方法的有效性。

Conclusion: VRPO通过Q-boosting有效解决了均衡自博弈中方差放大的瓶颈，为不完全信息博弈中的多智能体强化学习提供了新的解决思路。

Abstract: Competitive multi-agent reinforcement learning in imperfect-information games requires agents to act under partial observability and against adversarial opponents, necessitating stochastic policies. While self-play reinforcement learning with Proximal Policy Optimization (PPO) has achieved strong empirical success, its standard advantage estimator, generalized advantage estimation, suffers from additional variance due to the sampling of stochastic future actions. This variance is amplified in equilibrium self-play because of the stochastic nature of the equilibrium policy and persists even when the critic is exact. We address this bottleneck by introducing $Q$-boosting, a variance-reduced advantage estimator based on a centralized action-value critic, and propose Variance-Reduced Policy Optimization (VRPO), incorporating this new estimator. The algorithm replaces sampled multi-step backups with a multi-step Expected SARSA$(λ)$ trace, computing policy expectations at each step to average out action-sampling noise, while retaining PPO's clipped objective and on-policy actor updates. Empirically, VRPO consistently achieves strong performance from mid-sized to large-scale games including Dou Dizhu and Heads-Up No-Limit Texas Hold'em.

</details>


### [207] [Euclidean Embedding of Data Using Local Distances](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.19243)
*Dimitris Arabadjis*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文研究从局部距离图恢复全局一致的欧几里得嵌入问题，提出一种仅依赖邻域图加权距离而无须先验向量表示的优化方法，将非线性变分问题转化为迭代稀疏线性问题求解。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有度量嵌入方法通常需要预计算的向量特征表示，且难以保证全局等距嵌入的一致性。本文旨在从纯局部距离图出发，无需特征向量即可获得全局一致的欧几里得嵌入。

Method: 通过变分方法将图上局部距离与嵌入函数的微分诱导的欧几里得度量相匹配；采用无坐标形式推导Euler-Lagrange方程，仅需从距离图计算算子；将隐式非线性方程转化为迭代稀疏线性问题求解。

Result: 实验表明该非参数算法在合成流形和真实数据集上能一致保持局部度量结构和邻域关系，同时近似实现全局等距嵌入。

Conclusion: 该方法实现了三大贡献：(a)给出了连续统中最优欧几里得嵌入的泛函方程；(b)提出仅需邻域距离图的无表示形式；(c)建立了完全基于局部图操作的估计程序。

Abstract: We study the problem of recovering a globally consistent Euclidean embedding of data, given only a local distance graph and propose a method that optimally represents these distances. The method operates solely on a neighborhood graph weighted by pairwise distances, without requiring any prior vector representation of the data. The embedding is obtained by solving a variational problem that matches local, on-graph distances to the Euclidean metric, induced by the differentials of the embedding functions. The resulting Euler-Lagrange equations are derived in a coordinate-free form, enabling direct evaluation of all operators from the distance graph alone. Though non-linear and missing an explicit expression for their non-linearity, these equations are shown to be resolved as an iteratively updated sparse linear problem. The main contributions of the proposed approach are (a) the derivation of the functional equations governing the optimal Euclidean embedding in the continuum, (b) a representation-free formulation that requires only a neighborhood distance graph and no feature vectors and (c) an estimation procedure based exclusively on local graph operations. We experimentally evaluate the resulting non-parametric algorithm on synthetic manifolds and real datasets, demonstrating consistent preservation of local metric structure and neighboring relations, while approximating the global isometric embedding.

</details>


### [208] [Beyond Extrapolation: Knowledge Utilization Paradigm with Bidirectional Inspiration for Time Series Forecasting](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.19249)
*Liu Chong,Yingjie Zhou,Hao Li,Pengyang Wang,Qingsong Wen,Ce Zhu*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: This paper proposes KUP-BI, a novel time-series forecasting paradigm that leverages "continuation-style knowledge" from historical training data as an approximate proxy for unavailable post-target continuation information. By distilling this knowledge into standard forecasting backbones via a feature-level gating module, the method provides structured inductive bias that helps models exploit typical continuation patterns rather than relying solely on parametric extrapolation.


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: Existing time-series forecasters rely on one-way inference (history → target) and ignore the structural information from the natural chain "history -- target -- post-target continuation". The post-target continuation (how trajectories evolve after the target) can stabilize forecasting but is unobservable at inference time.

Method: KUP-BI distills continuation-style knowledge from a train-only historical library (as proxy for post-target continuation) and integrates it into standard forecasting backbones via a lightweight feature-level gating module. This provides structured inductive bias without introducing new information.

Result: KUP-BI achieves consistent improvements over state-of-the-art baselines on six public datasets, validating the effectiveness of incorporating continuation-style knowledge as a structured inductive bias for bidirectional forecasting.

Conclusion: KUP-BI consistently improves forecasting performance of state-of-the-art models across six public datasets with small additional overhead.

Abstract: Time-series forecasting is critical in various scenarios, such as energy, transportation, and public health. However, most existing forecasters rely primarily on one-way inference, \textit{i.e.}, mapping \textbf{history} to \textbf{target}, and overlook the structural information provided by a revised natural chain (``\textbf{history} (model input) -- \textbf{target} (ground-truth output) -- \textbf{post-target continuation}''). The post-target continuation records how trajectories evolve after the target, which can help stabilize forecasting, but it is not observable at inference time. In this work, we aim to obtain an approximate proxy of the post-target continuation for the current input, providing structural knowledge for bidirectional forecasting. This idea is instantiated as KUP-BI (Knowledge Utilization Paradigm with Bidirectional Inspiration), a new time-series modeling paradigm that distills continuation-style knowledge (as an approximate post-target continuation proxy) from a \emph{train-only} historical library and integrates it into standard forecasting backbones. The input stream and the continuation-proxy stream are fused via a lightweight feature-level gating module. This design does not introduce information beyond what is already contained in the training trajectories; instead, it provides a structured inductive bias that helps backbones exploit typical continuation patterns rather than relying solely on parametric extrapolation. Experimental results on six public datasets show that KUP-BI consistently improves the forecasting performance of state-of-the-art models, with small additional overhead.

</details>


### [209] [ExECG: An Explainable AI Framework for ECG models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.19258)
*Jong-Hwan Jang,Yong-yeon Jo*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: ExECG是一个用于心电图可解释人工智能的三阶段Python框架，通过Wrapper-Explainer-Visualizer架构统一异构ECG格式和多种XAI方法，实现可重复和可互操作的ECG可解释性分析。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: Deep learning ECG models lack transparency despite strong performance. Existing ECG XAI methods have inconsistent pipelines and reporting conventions, hindering reuse, reproducibility, and clinical adoption.

Method: Proposes ExECG, a three-stage Python pipeline: (1) Wrapper standardizes heterogeneous ECG formats, (2) Explainer unifies diverse XAI methods under shared execution protocol, (3) Visualizer enables consistent cross-method comparison.

Result: Demonstrated end-to-end functionality through concise examples and two case studies, confirming framework's interoperability and reproducibility for ECG explainability.

Conclusion: ExECG provides a standardized, reproducible, and interoperable approach to ECG explainability, enabling easier adoption and comparison of XAI methods in clinical ECG analysis workflows.

Abstract: Deep learning has enabled ECG diagnostic models with strong performance in tasks such as arrhythmia classification and abnormality detection. However, accuracy alone is insufficient for clinical deployment because it does not explain why a specific output was produced, limiting justification, error analysis, and trust. Although ECG XAI has been extensively investigated and steadily improved, practical pipelines and reporting conventions vary across studies, hindering reuse and reproducibility. To address these issues, we present Explainable AI framework for ECG models (ExECG), a Python framework that provides a three-stage pipeline: Wrapper standardizes access across heterogeneous ECG formats and intermediate representations, Explainer unifies diverse XAI methods under a shared execution protocol, and Visualizer supports consistent cross-method comparison within a unified interface. We demonstrate end-to-end usage with concise examples and two case studies, highlighting interoperable and reproducible ECG explainability.

</details>


### [210] [Backdooring Masked Diffusion Language Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.19262)
*Daniel Yiming Cao,Chengzhong Wang,Sheng-Yen Chou,Chengyu Huang,Pin-Yu Chen,Shengwei An*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: This paper presents the first systematic study of training-time backdoor attacks on Masked Diffusion Language Models (MDLMs), proposing SHADOWMASK which modifies the forward corruption process to create a hidden denoising pathway for triggers. The method provides principled mathematical formulation and achieves near-100% attack success while preserving clean utility and resisting defenses.


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: MDLMs are emerging as a compelling new paradigm for text generation, but their training-time security remains largely unexplored. Existing backdoor attacks on Gaussian diffusion models or autoregressive language models do not directly apply to MDLMs because MDLMs rely on discrete state corruption and iterative denoising rather than continuous noising or left-to-right prediction.

Method: SHADOWMASK modifies the MDLM forward corruption process by replacing the standard all-mask terminal distribution with a trigger-mask mixture prior. This creates a dedicated denoising pathway from trigger-corrupted states to attacker-specified targets while preserving clean denoising behavior. The method includes: (1) defining the backdoored forward process, (2) deriving the reverse-time posterior, and (3) obtaining the continuous-time training objective.

Result: Evaluations on DiT-based MDLM and LLaDA-8B-Instruct across WikiText-103, OpenWebText, and Alpaca show effectiveness of SHADOWMASK.

Conclusion: SHADOWMASK achieves near-100% attack success, substantially outperforms standard data poisoning, largely preserves clean utility, remains effective under full-model and parameter-efficient fine-tuning, and is robust against representative defenses.

Abstract: Masked diffusion language models (MDLMs) are emerging as a compelling new paradigm for text generation, but their training-time security remains largely unexplored. Existing backdoor attacks on Gaussian diffusion models or autoregressive language models do not directly apply to MDLMs because MDLMs rely on discrete state corruption and iterative denoising rather than continuous noising or left-to-right prediction. In this work, we present the first systematic study of training-time backdoor attacks on MDLMs. We propose SHADOWMASK, a backdoor attack that modifies the MDLM forward corruption process by replacing the standard all-mask terminal distribution with a trigger-mask mixture prior. This creates a dedicated denoising pathway from trigger-corrupted states to attacker-specified targets while preserving clean denoising behavior. We further provide a principled mathematical formulation by defining the backdoored forward process, deriving the reverse-time posterior, and obtaining the continuous-time training objective. Evaluations on DiT-based MDLM and LLaDA-8B-Instruct across WikiText-103, OpenWebText, and Alpaca show that SHADOWMASK achieves near-100% attack success, substantially outperforms standard data poisoning, largely preserves clean utility, remains effective under full-model and parameter-efficient fine-tuning, and is robust against representative defenses.

</details>


### [211] [TwinRouterBench: Fast Static and Live Dynamic Evaluation for Realistic Agentic LLM Routing](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.18859)
*Pei Yang,Wanyi Chen,Tongyun Yang,Pengbin Feng,Jiarong Xing,Wentao Guo,Yuhang Yao,Yuhang Han,Hanchen Li,Xu Wang,Zeyu Wang,Jie Xiao,Anjie Yang,Liang Tian,Lynn Ai,Eric Yang,Tianyu Shi*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: TwinRouterBench是一个针对多步Agent场景的LLM路由基准测试，通过静态和动态双轨设计评估路由器的成本-质量权衡能力，解决了现有基准测试在长时域应用中的评估缺陷。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有路由基准测试仅在单次提示场景下评估路由器，从未在中间Agent步骤暴露路由可见前缀，也未测试廉价替代方案是否能保持下游任务成功，且常依赖在线LLM评判。在长时域应用（编码Agent、深度研究系统、计算机使用Agent）中，单个用户请求会触发大量模型调用，因此准确的路由决策至关重要。

Method: 提出了TwinRouterBench，一个步骤级路由基准测试，包含两个轨道：1) 静态轨道：来自SWE-bench、BFCL、mtRAG、QMSum和PinchBench的970个路由可见前缀，配合基于downgrade-and-cascade协议的执行验证目标层；2) 动态轨道：在完整的500-case SWE-bench Verified套件上运行路由器，并报告100个独立案例的评估结果。评分基于层标签、轨迹成员资格和令牌成本的确定性算术运算，无需在线LLM评判。

Result: 该基准测试支持快速离线迭代，随后在实时Agent执行下进行端到端验证。两个轨道设计使研究者能够在受控环境中快速迭代，同时通过动态轨道在实际Agent执行中验证路由策略的有效性。

Conclusion: TwinRouterBench通过静态和动态双轨设计，为LLM路由研究提供了全面的评估框架，有效解决了现有基准测试在多步Agent场景下的评估局限性。代码和数据已开源。

Abstract: LLM routing matters most in long-horizon applications such as coding agents, deep research systems, and computer-use agents, where a single user request triggers many model calls. Routing each call to the cheapest sufficient model can cut costs without sacrificing quality, yet existing router benchmarks evaluate routers only on one-shot prompts. They never expose the router-visible prefix at an intermediate agent step, never test whether a cheaper replacement preserves downstream task success, and often rely on online LLM judges at evaluation time. We introduce TwinRouterBench, a step-level routing benchmark with two tracks. The static track provides 970 router-visible prefixes from 520 instances across SWE-bench, BFCL, mtRAG, QMSum, and PinchBench, each paired with an execution-verified target tier estimated under a released downgrade-and-cascade protocol; scoring is deterministic arithmetic over tier labels, trajectory membership, and token costs, with no online evaluator-side LLM judge. The dynamic track supplies a harness that runs routers on the full 500-case SWE-bench Verified suite; in this paper we report a 100-case held-out evaluation disjoint from the static SWE supervision split. At each LLM call the router selects a concrete model from a locked pool, and success is measured by official task resolution and realized API spend. The two tracks support fast offline iteration followed by end-to-end validation under live agent execution. Code and data are available at https://github.com/CommonstackAI/TwinRouterBench.

</details>


### [212] [CODA: Rewriting Transformer Blocks as GEMM-Epilogue Programs](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.19269)
*Han Guo,Jack Zhang,Arjun Menon,Driss Guessous,Vijay Thakkar,Yoon Kim,Tri Dao*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: CODA是一个GPU内核抽象，将Transformer中的内存受限算子（归一化、激活、残差更新、归约等）重新参数化为GEMM+尾声程序，使这些操作在GEMM输出tile驻留在芯片上时执行，避免频繁的全局内存访问。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 当前Transformer训练系统中，除注意力机制外的算子（如归一化、激活、残差连接、归约等）虽计算量小但需频繁移动大量中间张量通过全局内存，导致数据移动成为性能瓶颈。

Method: CODA基于核心观察：许多可分离的框架内核可以代数重参数化为GEMM输出tile仍驻留在芯片上时执行的计算。方法固定GEMM主循环，暴露一组可组合的尾声原语用于缩放、归约、成对变换和累加。

Result: 在代表性Transformer工作负载上，人工编写和LLM生成的CODA内核均达到高性能，证明GEMM+尾声编程能在保持专家级GEMM性能结构的同时覆盖几乎所有非注意力计算。

Conclusion: GEMM-plus-epilogue编程为结合框架级生产力和硬件级效率提供了实用路径。

Abstract: Transformer training systems are built around dense linear algebra, yet a nontrivial fraction of end-to-end time is spent on surrounding memory-bound operators. Normalization, activations, residual updates, reductions, and related computations repeatedly move large intermediate tensors through global memory while performing little arithmetic, making data movement an increasingly important bottleneck in otherwise highly optimized training stacks. We introduce CODA, a GPU kernel abstraction that expresses these computations as GEMM-plus-epilogue programs. CODA is based on the observation that many Transformer operators exposed as separate framework kernels can be algebraically reparameterized to execute while a GEMM output tile remains on chip, before it is written to memory. The abstraction fixes the GEMM mainloop and exposes a small set of composable epilogue primitives for scaling, reductions, pairwise transformations, and accumulation. This constrained interface preserves the performance structure of expert-written GEMMs while remaining expressive enough to cover nearly all non-attention computation in the forward and backward pass of a standard Transformer block. Across representative Transformer workloads, both human- and LLM-authored CODA kernels achieve high performance, suggesting that GEMM-plus-epilogue programming offers a practical path toward combining framework-level productivity with hardware-level efficiency.

</details>


### [213] [EviTrack: Selection over Sampling for Delayed Disambiguation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.19283)
*Omer Haq*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: Summary generation failed


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: Motivation analysis unavailable

Method: Method extraction failed

Result: Result analysis unavailable

Conclusion: Conclusion extraction failed

Abstract: Sequential prediction is challenging in regimes of delayed disambiguation, where early observations are ambiguous and multiple latent explanations remain plausible until sufficient evidence accumulates. Standard approaches based on marginal inference struggle in this setting, either collapsing uncertainty prematurely or failing to recover once informative evidence arrives.
  We introduce EviTrack, a test-time inference framework that operates over latent trajectories rather than marginal states. EviTrack maintains a set of competing trajectory hypotheses and applies evidence- and likelihood-ratio-based selection to delay commitment until supported by data, drawing inspiration from hypothesis management in multiple hypothesis tracking and track-before-detect.
  To evaluate this setting, we construct a controlled synthetic benchmark with known latent ground truth that explicitly exhibits delayed disambiguation. At matched inference budget, EviTrack substantially outperforms sampling-based baselines, achieving faster post-disambiguation recovery.
  These results show that, in delayed disambiguation regimes, moderate trajectory-level selection is more effective than increasing sampling coverage, highlighting selection over sampling as a key principle for reliable sequential inference.

</details>


### [214] [An Objective Performance Evaluation of the LSTM Networks in Time Series Classification](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.19311)
*Sooraj Sunil,Balakumar Balasingam*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文对比了LSTM深度学习分类器与基于模型的EM分类器在二元时间序列分类任务中的性能差异。研究表明，当数据符合假设的模型类别时，利用已知模型结构的EM分类器表现优异；而LSTM分类器需要更大的噪声统计差异才能实现可靠分类，且在仅测量噪声不同时，其性能始终低于参考分类器。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 深度学习模型在时间序列分析中的应用日益广泛，但在已知物理定律的领域，数据驱动模型相对于基于模型的方法的性能优势很少被客观评估。

Method: 采用Monte Carlo仿真方法，在两个标量线性高斯状态空间模型上进行评估。实验从三个维度进行：任务难度（由过程噪声或测量噪声的分离程度控制）、序列长度、训练数据集规模。Kalman滤波器似然比检验作为理论最优参考。

Result: EM分类器在数据符合模型假设时表现强劲；LSTM分类器需要更大的噪声统计差异才能实现可靠分类；当两个模型仅在测量噪声上存在差异时，LSTM性能随序列长度或训练数据规模的增加仍无法达到参考分类器水平。

Conclusion: 在结构化环境中，当数据的生成过程符合假设的模型类别时，基于模型的分类方法优于数据驱动的深度学习方法。这表明模型先验知识的利用对于此类问题具有重要价值。

Abstract: The rapid adoption of deep learning has increasingly led to data-driven models replacing classical model-based algorithms, even in domains governed by well-understood physical laws. While data-driven models, such as long short-term memory (LSTM) networks, have become a popular choice for time-series analysis, their performance relative to model-based approaches in structured environments is rarely evaluated objectively. This paper presents a performance evaluation framework comparing an LSTM classifier against a model-based expectation maximization (EM) classifier for binary time-series classification. The evaluation is conducted on two scalar linear Gaussian state space models differing only in their noise statistics, where the Kalman filter likelihood ratio test with true parameters serves as a reference for the best achievable classification performance.Through Monte Carlo simulations, the classifiers are evaluated across three axes: task difficulty, controlled by the separation in process or measurement noise between the two models; sequence length; and training dataset size. The results show that the EM classifier, which exploits the known model structure, performs strongly when the data conform to the assumed model class. The LSTM classifier requires a larger separation in noise statistics to achieve reliable classification, and its performance saturates below the reference classifier when the models differ only in measurement noise, regardless of sequence length or training dataset size.

</details>


### [215] [Inference-Time Scaling in Diffusion Models through Iterative Partial Refinement](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.19317)
*Taegu Kang,Jaesik Yoon,Sungjin Ahn*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出Iterative Partial Refinement (IPR)，一种无需外部验证器的顺序扩散模型推理时扩展方法。IPR通过迭代式重噪声化并重新生成部分区域，在更丰富的上下文下修正早期决策，显著提升全局一致性。在MNIST Sudoku上有效解率从55.8%提升至75.0%。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有扩散模型的推理时扩展方法通常依赖外部验证器或奖励模型进行样本排序和选择，限制了其在缺乏可靠评估器场景下的可扩展性。此外，针对具有区域级混合噪声条件的顺序推理扩散模型的推理时扩展方法尚未得到充分探索。

Method: IPR首先从已生成的完整样本出发，选择部分区域进行重噪声化，然后在保留其余区域的条件下重新生成被选中的区域。这种迭代式部分细化使模型能够在比初始生成更丰富的上下文信息下修正早期的决策，从而产生全局一致性更好的样本，无需外部验证器的参与。

Result: 在需要全局约束满足的推理任务中，IPR持续提升性能：在MNIST Sudoku任务上，有效解率从55.8%提升至75.0%。

Conclusion: IPR通过迭代部分细化策略，在无需外部验证器的情况下实现了扩散模型推理时的有效扩展，在MNIST Sudoku上从未知解率55.8%提升至75.0%，证明了该方法在需要全局约束满足的推理任务中的有效性。

Abstract: Inference-time scaling has emerged as a major approach for improving reasoning capabilities, and has been increasingly applied to diffusion models. However, existing inference-time scaling methods for diffusion models typically rely on external verifiers or reward models to rank and select samples, limiting their scalability to settings where such evaluators are available and reliable. Moreover, while recent diffusion models perform sequential inference with region-wise, mixed-noise conditioning, inference-time scaling tailored to this setting remains relatively underexplored. We propose Iterative Partial Refinement (IPR), an inference-time scaling method for sequential diffusion that requires no external verifier. Starting from an already-generated sample, IPR re-noises a subset of regions and regenerates them conditioned on the remaining regions, enabling the model to revise earlier decisions under a richer context than was available during the initial generation. This iterative partial refinement produces more globally consistent samples without external verification. On reasoning tasks requiring global constraint satisfaction, IPR consistently improves performance: on MNIST Sudoku, the valid solution rate increases from 55.8% to 75.0%. These results show that iterative partial refinement alone can serve as an effective inference-time scaling strategy for diffusion models in sequential, mixed-noise settings. Code is available at: https://github.com/ahn-ml/IPR

</details>


### [216] [An Exterior Method for Nonnegative Matrix Factorization](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.19325)
*Qiujing Lu,Tonmoy Monsoor,Ehsan Ebrahimzadeh,Kartik Sharma,Vwani Roychowdhury*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出eNMF外部优化框架，通过分离低秩近似与非负性约束、旋转映射到非负象限边界的方式，解决了传统内点法NMF收敛慢、易陷入次优解的问题。实验证明其在重建误差和收敛速度上显著优于9种state-of-the-art算法，且99%的实验表明不同算法趋于收敛到等价解。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 传统NMF使用内点法（interior-point methods）强制约束不可行性，但非负约束可能阻碍非凸优化景观中的进展，导致收敛缓慢或收敛到次优的驻点。

Method: 提出外部框架eNMF，将低秩近似与非负性约束分离。具体包括：(1) 从最优无约束分解初始化；(2) 引入旋转程序将无约束因子映射到非负象限的最近边界点；(3) 采用简单迭代更新收敛至满足KKT条件的边界驻点。该方法还提供了NMF解在置换和正交变换下的等价类几何解释。

Result: 在400次NMF实验（真实与合成数据集）中，99%的情况显示不同算法趋于收敛到等价的因子矩阵。eNMF在81个竞争配置（9种算法×9种初始化×3个真实数据集+2个合成数据集）中始终表现最优：在相同时间设置下重建误差降低30%，在相同误差设置下加速150%。下游任务（音频处理、推荐系统）也验证了其实用价值。

Conclusion: eNMF通过将低秩近似与非负性约束分离，提供了一种新的NMF优化范式。该框架不仅具有坚实的理论基础（KKT条件满足的边界解），还提供了对NMF解的几何解释（置换和正交变换下的等价类）。实验结果表明其在重建误差和收敛速度上均显著优于现有方法，并在下游任务（音频处理、推荐系统）中展现出实际应用价值。

Abstract: Nonnegative matrix factorization (NMF) seeks a low-rank approximation $X \approx UV^T$ with nonnegative factors and is commonly solved using interior methods that enforce feasibility throughout optimization. We show that such constraint-driven approaches can impede progress in the nonconvex landscape, leading to slow convergence or convergence to suboptimal stationary points. We propose an exterior framework for NMF (eNMF) that separates low-rank approximation from nonnegativity enforcement. Our method initializes from the optimal unconstrained factorization and introduces a rotation procedure that maps unconstrained factors to an exterior point closest to the nonnegative orthant. This viewpoint yields an algorithmic framework in which simple iterative updates converge to KKT-satisfying stationary points on the boundary of the positive orthant. The exterior formulation also enables a geometric interpretation of NMF solutions, clarifying equivalence classes of factorizations under permutation and orthogonal transformations. An intriguing numerical result, involving 400 NMF experiments across both real and synthetic datasets, show that in 99% of the cases, different algorithms tend to converge towards equivalent factor matrices. We benchmark eNMF against 9 state-of-the-art NMF algorithms with 9 initialization schemes across 3 real-world and 2 synthetic datasets. eNMF consistently outperforms all 81 competitors, achieving up to 30% lower reconstruction error under equal-time settings and up to 150% speedup under equal-error settings. The downstream experiments further demonstrate substantial performance gains in audio processing and recommendation tasks, corroborating the practical benefits of the proposed exterior optimization framework. Code is available at https://github.com/roychowdhuryresearch/eNMF

</details>


### [217] [What Makes a Representation Good for Single-Cell Perturbation Prediction?](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.19343)
*Wenkang Jiang,Yuhang Liu,Yichao Cai,Erdun Gao,Jiayi Dong,Ehsan Abbasnejad,Lina Yao,Javen Qinfeng Shi*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: PerturbedVAE通过分离扰动特异性信息与不变结构来解决单细胞扰动建模中的信号不平衡问题，在基准数据集上取得最优性能，特别是在分布外组合预测任务上表现突出。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有方法（包括因果表示学习和基础模型）在单细胞扰动建模中面临一个被忽视的挑战：基因表达数据中扰动不变信息占主导地位，而扰动特异性信号本质上是稀疏的。这导致学习到的表示要么纠缠了不变信息和扰动特异性信息（产生虚假且不可泛化的预测器），要么完全压制了扰动特异性信号（使其对预测无效）。

Method: PerturbedVAE采用变分自编码器框架，通过显式分离扰动特异性信息和主导的不变结构来恢复因果表示。框架包含可辨识性分析，刻画了在何种条件下可以可靠地恢复稀疏扰动效应，从而明确框架在何种条件下可具体实现。

Result: PerturbedVAE在多个评估设置的广泛使用基准数据集上达到最先进的性能，在分布外组合预测任务中获得显著提升，并揭示了解释性的扰动响应程序。

Conclusion: 本文提出了PerturbedVAE框架，通过显式分离扰动不变信息和扰动特异性信息，并恢复因果表示来解决单细胞扰动建模中的信号不平衡问题。理论分析提供了可辨识性条件，实验验证了在基准数据集上达到最优性能，特别是在分布外组合预测任务中表现突出，并揭示了解释性扰动响应程序。

Abstract: Single-cell perturbation modeling is fundamental for understanding and predicting cellular responses to genetic perturbations. However, existing approaches, from causal representation learning to foundation models, often struggle with an overlooked challenge: gene expression is dominated by perturbation-invariant information, while perturbation-specific signals are intrinsically sparse. As a result, learned representations either entangle invariant and perturbation-specific information, leading to spurious and non-generalizable predictors, or suppress perturbation-specific signals altogether, rendering them ineffective for prediction. To address this, we propose PerturbedVAE, a general framework designed to resolve this signal imbalance. The framework explicitly separates perturbation-specific information from dominant invariant structure and recovers causal representations to effectively utilize such information for prediction. We further provide an identifiability analysis that characterizes the conditions under which sparse perturbation effects can be reliably recovered, thereby clarifying how the framework can be concretely specified under such conditions. Empirically, PerturbedVAE achieves state-of-the-art performance on a widely used benchmark across multiple evaluation settings, yielding significant gains on out-of-distribution combinatorial predictions and uncovering interpretable perturbation-response programs.

</details>


### [218] [Accurate, Efficient, and Explainable Deep Learning Approaches for Environmental Science Problems](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.19366)
*Jimeng Shi*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文是一篇关于环境智能的博士论文，通过开发三个AI驱动的方法解决环境科学中的洪水预测、天气预报和科学问答问题。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 环境科学面临大数据挑战，AI技术虽已兴起但在环境科学领域的应用仍需深入探索，尤其是物理模型计算成本高、天气预报数据规模大、LLM存在幻觉问题等具体痛点。

Method: 针对三个不同问题采用了针对性方法：①FIDLAr采用预测引导的深度学习模型进行洪水管理；②CoDiCast采用条件扩散模型进行概率天气预报；③Hypercube-RAG基于结构化文本立方体框架结合检索增强生成。

Result: FIDLAr在准确性和效率上优于基线模型并提供可解释输出；CoDiCast实现了准确高效的天气预报并可量化不确定性；Hypercube-RAG同时具备准确性、效率和可解释性三个特性。

Conclusion: 本文通过WaLeF、FIDLAr、CoDiCast和Hypercube-RAG四个具体方法，分别解决了环境监测中的洪水预测、天气预报和科学问答三个核心问题，较为全面地展示了AI技术在环境科学领域的应用潜力。

Abstract: Environmental science plays a pivotal role in safeguarding ecosystems, a domain driven by large-scale, heterogeneous data. In the big data era, artificial intelligence (AI) has emerged as a transformative tool for learning patterns and supporting decision-making. This dissertation develops AI-based approaches tailored to complex environmental science problems to achieve Environmental Intelligence, studying three specific challenges. First, we focus on flood prediction and management in coastal river systems. Conventional physics-based models are computationally intensive, limiting real-time application. To overcome this, we propose a deep learning (DL)-based model, WaLeF, for water level forecasting, and a forecast-informed DL model, FIDLAr, to manage water levels. Evaluated in a flood-prone coastal system in South Florida characterized by extreme rainfall and sea level fluctuations, FIDLAr outperforms baselines in accuracy and efficiency while providing interpretable outputs. Second, we target global weather prediction, which is challenged by massive data scale. Traditional physics methods are deterministic and computationally heavy. We propose CoDiCast, a conditional diffusion model tailored for probabilistic weather forecasting. Adapted from generative AI for predictive tasks, experiments show CoDiCast achieves accurate, efficient forecasts with explicit uncertainty quantification. Lastly, we address scientific question-answering in environmental science. When answering in-domain questions, large language models (LLMs) often suffer from hallucinations due to out-of-date or limited knowledge. While retrieval-augmented generation (RAG) retrieves domain-specific knowledge, existing methods trade off accuracy, efficiency, or explainability. We propose Hypercube-RAG, built on a structured text cube framework, which successfully exhibits all three properties simultaneously.

</details>


### [219] [The Evaluation Game: Beyond Static LLM Benchmarking](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.19377)
*Paul Wang,Jade Garcia-Bourrée,Anne-Marie Kermarrec,Vincent Corruble*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出一个基于博弈论框架来理解大语言模型微调防御jailbreak的理论基础，利用群作用数学结构形式化数据增强，并在Llama、Qwen、Mistral三个模型族上验证了微调仅诱导局部泛化的假设，揭示了对抗评估基准应被视为评估者群作用下的轨道而非静态数据集。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 当前LLM中jailbreak攻防实践主要依赖经验性微调策略，缺乏理论支撑。论文认为微调防御的理论基础尚未被充分探索，需要形式化框架来理解训练者与评估者之间的博弈动态。

Method: 1. 建立训练者与评估者（审计模型jailbreak）的两人博弈形式化模型；2. 利用群作用（group actions）数学结构形式化数据增强，将数据增强表示为对称性和变换；3. 最小非平凡实例采用圆与循环平移群进行分析；4. 在三个模型族（Llama、Qwen、Mistral）上进行拒绝率与训练提示距离相关性的实证研究。

Result: 1. 发现关键阈值现象：训练者泛化范围低于临界值时，评估者在线性多轮中保持恒定漏检率；其他设置下行为差异显著；2. 三个模型族均显示强证据支持微调的局域性假设：对抗提示微调后的拒绝率与到微调样本的距离高度相关。

Conclusion: 微调防御的效果取决于泛化范围，且呈局域性特征。对抗评估基准应被重新定义为评估者群作用下的轨道而非静态提示集。忽视训练者端适应的审计协议无法区分真实修复与记忆化补丁。

Abstract: As jailbreaks, adversarially crafted inputs that bypass safety constraints, continue to be discovered in Large Language Models, practitioners increasingly rely on fine-tuning as a defensive strategy. Yet the theoretical foundations underlying this robustness fine-tuning remain underexplored. We introduce a game-theoretic framework in which the interaction between an evaluator (auditing the model for jailbreaks) and a trainer is formalized as a two-player game.
  A key feature of our approach is the use of group actions, a mathematical structure that captures symmetries and transformations, to formally represent data augmentation. The simplest non-trivial instance is the circle with cyclic translation groups, where we exhibit various regimes depending on the trainer's generalization range. Below a critical threshold, the evaluator maintains a constant miss ratio for linearly many rounds, whereas other settings can yield very different behaviors. We further provide empirical evidence supporting locality-dependence of the model: for the three model families we tested (Llama, Qwen and Mistral), we have significant evidence that fine-tuning on adversarial prompts induces only local generalization, with refusal rates on test examples highly correlated with the distance to the fine-tuning prompts. Our framework recasts the central object of adversarial evaluation: a benchmark is not a static set of prompts but an orbit under the evaluator's group action, and audit protocols that ignore trainer-side adaptation cannot distinguish a genuine fix from a memorized patch.

</details>


### [220] [A Bitter Lesson for Data Filtering](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.19407)
*Christopher Mohri,John Duchi,Tatsunori Hashimoto*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 反对大模型预训练中常用的数据过滤策略，通过扩展性研究发现足够大的模型不仅能容忍低质量数据，还能从中受益，暗示在计算资源充足的情况下，最佳数据过滤策略是不过滤。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: Challenge the widely-held belief that data quality filtering is essential for training large models; investigate whether this assumption holds at scale.

Method: Scaling studies in the high compute, data-scarce regime, comparing filtered vs unfiltered training data across different model scales.

Result: Large parameter models not only tolerate low-quality data but actually benefit from it, contradicting the common practice of aggressive data filtering.

Conclusion: With sufficient compute and model capacity, data filtering may be counterproductive; the optimal filtering strategy is no filtering at all.

Abstract: We investigate data filtering for large model pretraining via new scaling studies that target the high compute, data-scarce regime. In spite of an apparently common belief that filtering data to include only high-quality information is essential, our experiments suggest that with enough compute, the best data filter is no data filter. We find that sufficiently trained large parameter models not only tolerate low-quality and distractor data, but in fact benefit from nominally ``poor'' data.

</details>


### [221] [Unlocking the Potential of Continual Model Merging: An ODE Perspective](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.19409)
*Lihong Lin,Haidong Kang*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 论文针对持续模型合并(CMM)中遗忘严重的问题，提出基于ODE的ODE-M方法，通过时间依赖速度场追踪低损失路径并施加屏障约束，实现对合并过程的显式控制，在基准测试中取得最优效果。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有合并规则缺乏对学习能力分配的显式可控性，导致任务顺序合并时遗忘严重且性能分配不一致。根本原因在于将各任务模型视为孤立参数点进行固定代数组合，未考虑独立训练模型在参数空间中的连通性。

Method: 提出ODE驱动的合并方法(ODE-M)，通过构建时间依赖的速度场追踪低损失连接路径，并施加屏障约束防止损失增加，实现任务模型沿最优轨迹逐步合并。

Result: 在主流CMM基准测试中达到最优性能，显著优于现有方法。

Conclusion: ODE-M通过ODE框架成功实现了对模型合并过程的显式可控引导，在主流CMM基准测试中达到最优性能，有效平衡了旧任务保留与新任务学习之间的关系。

Abstract: Continual Model Merging (CMM) enables rapid customization of foundation models across sequentially arriving tasks, offering a scalable alternative to repeated retraining. However, existing merging rules lack explicit controllability over the allocation of learning capacity between previously learned capabilities and newly merged models. Consequently, as tasks are merged sequentially, this deficiency accumulates into severe forgetting, particularly in scenarios with heterogeneous task importance, where performance allocation becomes highly inconsistent. The key reason can be attributed to the fact that previous methods treat each task model as an isolated parameter point and apply fixed algebraic combinations, rather than explicitly constructing a transition that respects how independently trained models can be connected in parameter space. Motivated by mode connectivity, we assume that desirable merged models lie on low loss connecting paths, and that continual merging should follow such paths without crossing loss barriers that induce forgetting. Grounded in these insights, we propose a novel ODE-driven Merging (ODE-M) tailored for CMM that traces such a path by integrating a time-dependent velocity field and enforcing barrier constraints to prevent loss-increasing steps. Extensive experiments demonstrate that ODE-M achieves state-of-the-art performance compared to its competitors across mainstream CMM benchmarks.

</details>


### [222] [When to Stop Reusing: Dynamic Gradient Gating for Sample-Efficient RLVR](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.19425)
*Yuchun Miao,Sen Zhang,Yuqi Zhang,Yaorui Shi,Qi Gu,Xunliang Cai,Lefei Zhang*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文发现RLVR中rollout复用导致的性能下降与lm_head层权重变化的急剧飙升同步(DWD现象)，理论证明lm_head梯度范数可作为策略偏移的实时信号，并提出DGG方法通过监控该信号实现高达2.93×样本效率提升。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: RLVR已成为LLM高级推理的主导范式，但rollout样本获取成本高昂，样本效率成为关键瓶颈。复用rollout批次进行多轮梯度更新虽为经典RL标准实践，但在RLVR中会放大策略偏移，导致严重性能下降，且难以早期检测。

Method: 识别了"权重不均衡发散(DWD)"现象：性能下降与lm_head权重变化的急剧飙升同步，而中间层保持稳定。理论证明了有害梯度集中在lm_head层。提出DGG方法，实时监控lm_head梯度范数并在有害梯度破坏优化器之前进行拦截。

Result: DGG在数学、ALFWorld、WebShop和搜索增强QA任务中一致匹配或超越单次使用基线，实现了最高2.93倍的样本效率提升和2.14倍的wall-clock加速。

Conclusion: DGG通过实时监测lm_head梯度范数成功解决了RLVR中因 rollout 复用导致的策略偏移问题，为样本效率优化提供了可解释且轻量级的解决方案。

Abstract: Reinforcement Learning with Verifiable Rewards (RLVR) has become the dominant paradigm for advanced reasoning in Large Language Models (LLMs), but rollout samples are expensive to obtain, making sample efficiency a critical bottleneck. A natural remedy is to reuse each rollout batch for multiple gradient updates, a standard practice in classical RL. Yet in RLVR, this amplifies policy shift, leading to severe performance degradation. Detecting the onset of degradation early enough to stop reuse remains an open and challenging problem. We close this gap by identifying the \textit{Disproportionate Weight Divergence (DWD)} phenomenon: performance degradation is synchronized with a sharp surge in the \texttt{lm\_head} weight change, while intermediate layers remain stable. Empirically, we verify that DWD emerges consistently across diverse LLMs and tasks. Theoretically, we prove that (i) harmful gradients concentrate at the \texttt{lm\_head} while intermediate layers are structurally attenuated, and (ii) the \texttt{lm\_head} gradient norm lower-bounds the policy divergence. These results establish the \texttt{lm\_head} gradient norm as a principled, real-time signal of catastrophic policy shift. Guided by this insight, we propose \textit{Dynamic Gradient Gating (DGG)}, a lightweight intervention that monitors the \texttt{lm\_head} gradient norm in real time and intercepts harmful gradients before they corrupt the optimizer. DGG consistently matches or exceeds the standard single-use baseline, achieving up to $2.93\times$ sample efficiency and $2.14\times$ wall-clock speedup across math, ALFWorld, WebShop, and search-augmented QA tasks.

</details>


### [223] [When the Majority Votes Wrong, the Intervention Timing for Test-Time Reinforcement Learning Hides in the Extinction Window](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.19444)
*Hongxiang Lin,Zhirui Kuai,Erpeng Xue,Lei Wang*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文揭示了测试时强化学习(TTRL)中多数投票机制的严重缺陷：大多数所谓"学习增益"实际是对已可解决问题的强化，而错误答案的污染更新导致净损害且不可逆。发现"正确回答消失窗口"现象，其先行指标为翻转率(FR)。提出TTRL-Guard框架，通过三种机制(FRS、MPS、RCSU)针对性保护消失窗口期间的正确信号，显著提升数学推理性能。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有TTRL方法使用多数投票作为伪标签信号存在系统性缺陷：多数增益来自已可解决问题而非真正学习；错误答案的污染更新超过真正学习；一旦多数投票锁定错误答案，损害不可逆。

Method: 提出TTRL-Guard框架，包含三种针对性机制：Flip-Rate-Aware Reward Scaling(FRS)根据FR下降动态降低风险更新权重；Minority-Preserving Sampling(MPS)保留少数正确回答的梯度信号；Risk-Conditioned Sparse Updatings(RCSU)暂停高极化问题的更新。

Result: 在三个模型和四个基准上验证，AIME 2025相对TTRL提升54%，Qwen2.5-7B-Instruct和Qwen3-4B达到最佳平均pass@1。

Conclusion: TTRL-Guard通过FRS、MPS和RCSU三种机制有效解决了正确回答消失问题，在多个模型和基准上取得了最先进性能，AIME 2025相对提升达54%。

Abstract: Test-time reinforcement learning (TTRL) reports substantial accuracy gains on mathematical reasoning benchmarks using majority vote as a pseudo-label signal. We argue these gains are systematically misinterpreted: most reflect sharpening of already-solvable problems rather than genuine learning, while problems corrupted from correct to incorrect outnumber truly learned ones, and this damage is irreversible once majority vote locks onto a wrong answer. Per-problem tracking reveals that correct-answer signals in low-ability problems are briefly active before being permanently suppressed, a phenomenon we term the \textit{Correct-Answer Extinction Window}, with Flip Rate (FR) as its leading indicator. We thus propose \textbf{TTRL-Guard}, a lightweight framework with three mechanisms targeting the extinction window: Flip-Rate-Aware Reward Scaling (FRS) down-weights at-risk updates as FR declines, Minority-Preserving Sampling (MPS) retains gradient signal from minority correct answers, and Risk-Conditioned Sparse Updatings (RCSU) suspends updates on polarized problems.
