Experiments across three models and four benchmarks show that TTRL-Guard achieves the best average pass@1 on Qwen2.5-7B-Instruct and Qwen3-4B, improves relatively over TTRL by +54\% on AIME 2025. \footnote{Our code and implementation details are available at https://github.com/linhxkkkk/TTRL-Guard.

</details>


### [224] [Quantifying the Pre-training Dividend: Generative versus Latent Self-Supervised Learning for Time Series Foundation Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.19462)
*Noam Major,Kathy Razmadze,Yoli Shavit*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文系统评估了自监督学习在时间序列领域的"预训练红利"价值，比较了生成式范式与潜在对齐架构，发现SSL在异常检测和分类任务上收益显著（高达375%），但在预测任务上效果有限。这一发现受精度-不变性权衡机制支配，且表征质量与数据来源无关、可在中等架构深度下饱和，为通过大规模合成数据实现扩展提供了路径。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 自监督学习(SSL)在视觉和NLP领域已获成功，但其快速应用于时序数据时，研究主要聚焦于生成式范式和预测任务，对学习表征的更广泛效用缺乏量化评估。

Method: 建立了一个受控框架来评估"预训练红利"；系统比较了生成式范式与潜在对齐架构；引入LeJEPA和DINO的时间序列适配版本；采用离散小波变换(DWT)增强来强制局部波动不变性。

Result: 预训练红利高度不对称：在异常检测和分类任务上提升可达375%，但在预测任务上提升甚微；表征效用非普适，受精度-不变性权衡支配；表征质量与数据来源基本无关，在中等架构深度下趋于饱和。

Conclusion: 表示质量与数据来源基本无关，且在中等架构深度下趋于饱和，表明可通过大规模合成数据生成实现扩展。为时序SSL的超参数配置、架构选择和数据集构建提供了理论依据。

Abstract: The success of self-supervised learning (SSL) in vision and NLP has motivated its rapid adoption for time series. However, research has focused primarily on Generative paradigms and forecasting tasks, leaving the broader utility of learned representations unquantified. We establish a controlled framework to evaluate the "pre-training dividend": the value added by SSL across diverse temporal tasks. We systematically compare Generative paradigms against Latent Alignment architectures, introducing adaptations of LeJEPA and DINO for time series. These adaptations utilize Discrete Wavelet Transform (DWT) augmentations to enforce invariance to local fluctuations. Our analysis reveals that the pre-training dividend is highly asymmetric: SSL yields gains of up to 375% for anomaly detection and classification, yet remains marginal for forecasting. We demonstrate that representational utility is non-universal, governed by a precision-invariance trade-off where the specific signal resolution required by the task must align with the objective. Finally, we show that representation quality is largely independent of data origin and saturates at moderate architectural depths, suggesting a path to scaling via massive synthetic generation. Our code is available at: https://github.com/noammajor/Models

</details>


### [225] [Sampling-Based Safe Reinforcement Learning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.19469)
*Luca Vignola,Bruce D. Lee,Manish Prajapat,Manuel Wendl,Melanie Zeilinger,Andreas Krause,Yarden As*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: SBSRL is a model-based safe RL algorithm that uses sampling-based constraint enforcement over dynamics uncertainty to guarantee safety during learning, with theoretical bounds and practical scalability to robotic control tasks.


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: Safe exploration is a fundamental challenge in reinforcement learning that limits real-world deployment of RL agents.

Method: SBSRL maintains safety by enforcing constraints jointly across a finite set of dynamics samples, approximating worst-case optimization over uncertain dynamics. Uses uncertainty-constrained exploration strategy to eliminate explicit exploration bonuses.

Result: High-probability safety guarantees throughout learning, finite-time sample complexity bound for near-optimal policy recovery, safe and efficient exploration in both simulation and real robotic hardware.

Conclusion: SBSRL provides a principled approach to safe RL with theoretical guarantees, successfully scaling to high-dimensional continuous control problems through deep ensemble implementations.

Abstract: Safe exploration remains a fundamental challenge in reinforcement learning (RL), limiting the deployment of RL agents in the real world. We propose Sampling-Based Safe Reinforcement Learning (SBSRL), a model-based RL algorithm that maintains safety throughout the learning process by enforcing constraints jointly across a finite set of dynamics samples. This formulation approximates an intractable worst-case optimization over uncertain dynamics and enables practical safety guarantees in continuous domains. We further introduce an exploration strategy based on constraining epistemic uncertainty, eliminating the need for explicit exploration bonuses. Under regularity conditions, we derive high-probability guarantees of safety throughout learning and a finite-time sample complexity bound for recovering a near-optimal policy. Empirically, SBSRL achieves safe and efficient exploration both in simulation and in real robotic hardware, and readily extends to practical deep-ensemble implementations that scale to high-dimensional continuous control problems.

</details>


### [226] [Adynamical systems view of training generativemodels and the memorization phenomenon](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.19483)
*Siva Athreya,Chiranjib Bhattacharya,Vivek S. Borkar*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: Explains memorization in generative models via two time scale dynamics in SGD, using a stylized loss function model with strong/weak variable dependence to connect memorization, collapse, and double descent phenomena through a dynamical systems framework.


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: Provide a system theoretic explanation for memorization phenomenon in generative models—where models yield identical/similar outputs for extended periods during training—through dynamic rather than static analysis.

Method: Uses stylized loss function model with strong/weak variable dependence (Austin [2016]), analyzes constant step size SGD via results from Azizian et al. [2024], and combines with mathematical collapse model from Borkar [2025a].

Result: Demonstrates how two distinct time scales in constant step size SGD naturally arise from the loss function structure, explaining memorization as a dynamic phenomenon and connecting it to collapse and double descent.

Conclusion: Presents a novel dynamical systems perspective on memorization in generative models, unifying it with other reported phenomena (double descent, collapse) through a system theoretic lens based on two time scale SGD dynamics.

Abstract: Using recent works of one of the authors (VSB) on collapse in generative models and two time scale dynamics in stochastic gradient descent in high dimensions, we give a system theoretic explanation of the memorization phenomenon in generative models. This relies purely on the dynamic aspects of the training phase. Specifically, we use a result of Austin [2016] to motivate a stylized model for the loss function for stochastic gradient descent (SGD) wherein the loss function has a strong dependence on some variables and weak dependence on the rest in a precise sense. This naturally leads to two distinct time scales in the constant step size SGD that is commonly used in machine learning. This fact has been used to explain the double descent phenomenon in SGD in Borkar [2026]. In conjunction with a mathematical model for collapse phenomenon in SGD developed in Borkar [2025a], we analyze the constant step size SGD using the recent results of Azizian et al. [2024] in order to explain the phenomenon of memorization wherein a generative model that is concurrently being tuned yields the same or similar outputs for significant stretches of time. This gives a novel perspective on the aforementioned phenomena reported in machine learning literature and their interrelationships, using a dynamical systems viewpoint.

</details>


### [227] [The Silent Hyperparameter: Quantifying the Impact of Inference Backends on LLM Reproducibility](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.19537)
*David Pape,Jonathan Evertz,Lea Schönherr*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 该研究首次系统性地揭示了LLM推理后端选择对基准测试结果的显著影响。研究发现不同的推理引擎（vLLM、SGLang、llama.cpp等）可能导致高达16.6个百分点的基准分数差异，这种差异源于前缀缓存、CUDA图等系统级优化。该研究强调推理后端是一个此前被忽视但至关重要的超参数，呼吁学界标准化报告推理技术栈以提升可重复性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 当前LLM的进步通过标准化基准测试来衡量，最先进改进往往只相差不到一个百分点。同时，现代LLM的评估计算成本推动了专门推理后端（用于高效执行训练模型推理的软件系统）的广泛采用。然而，系统级优化（如自定义CUDA内核和低精度算术）可能改变token概率并引入非确定性，可能导致生成结果发散。尽管业界存在约200种不同的推理引擎，但研究表明具体使用的推理技术栈很少被报告。

Method: 研究者首先对推理技术领域进行了全面调查，识别出200种不同的引擎，分析了35,000篇ML论文。然后在保持模型权重、解码参数和硬件不变的前提下，对5种广泛使用的推理引擎（包括vLLM、SGLang和llama.cpp等）进行了系统性实证研究，跨越多个开源模型和已建立的基准测试。通过隔离后端优化并追踪执行流水线来追溯差异来源。

Result: 研究发现推理后端的选择本身即可将基准测试分数偏移高达16.6个百分点，并导致高比例的输出分歧。这种差异由系统级优化驱动，包括：前缀缓存、CUDA图、自定义内核以及日志处理中的引擎特定默认设置。

Conclusion: 本研究揭示了推理后端作为LLM评估中一个此前未报告但至关重要的超参数。研究表明，推理引擎的选择可以导致基准测试分数高达16.6个百分点的差异，并引发高频率的输出分歧。基于此，研究者主张标准化报告推理技术栈，以提高基准测试比较的可重复性和可解释性。

Abstract: Progress in LLMs is increasingly measured through standardized benchmarks, where state-of-the-art improvements are often separated by fractions of a percentage point. At the same time, the computational cost of evaluating modern LLMs has driven widespread adoption of specialized inference backends, software systems that execute trained models efficiently at inference time. While critical for scalability, system-level optimizations, such as custom CUDA kernels and reduced-precision arithmetic, can alter token probabilities and introduce non-determinism, possibly cascading into divergent generation. In this work, we first survey the inference landscape, identifying 200 distinct engines, and analyze 35,000 ML publications, finding that the specific inference stack is rarely reported despite this widespread diversity. We then present a systematic empirical study of how inference backends affect LLM benchmark results. Holding model weights, decoding parameters, and hardware constant, we evaluate five widely used inference engines, including vLLM, SGLang, and llama.cpp, across multiple open-weight models and established benchmarks. We show that the choice of backend alone can shift benchmark scores by up to 16.6 percentage points and induce high rates of output disagreement. By isolating backend optimizations and tracing the execution pipeline, we find this divergence is driven by system-level optimizations like prefix caching and CUDA graphs, custom kernels, and engine-specific defaults in logit processing. Our findings identify the inference backend as a previously unreported but consequential hyperparameter in the evaluation of LLM and advocate standardized reporting of inference stacks to improve the reproducibility and interpretability of benchmark comparisons.

</details>


### [228] [TORQ: Two-Level Orthogonal Rotation for MXFP4 Quantization](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.19561)
*Zukang Xu,Xing Hu,Dawei Yang*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出TORQ框架，通过理论分析MXFP4激活量化的误差结构，识别出两个结构性失衡问题，并采用双层正交旋转（块间和块内）来解决，在主流LLM上实现了接近全精度推理的4位浮点量化性能。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 随着LLM向实际部署推进，MXFP4格式成为低比特推理的关键，但直接应用于激活量化会导致显著的精度下降。

Method: 提出TORQ框架，通过两层正交旋转重塑激活空间的几何属性：(1)宏观层面利用Schur-Horn定理进行块间正交旋转，重新分配激活能量，防止高方差块主导共享缩放因子；(2)微观层面采用最大熵引导的块内旋转缓解码本坍塌，最大化MXFP4码本的信息容量。

Result: 在LLaMA3和Qwen3等主流模型上，TORQ显著优于现有方法：Qwen3-32B在WikiText上的困惑度降至8.43（vs BF16的7.61），平均准确率从直接RTN的38.40%提升至73.63%（vs BF16的74.82%）。

Conclusion: TORQ通过双层正交旋转显著缩小了4位浮点量化的精度差距，在Qwen3-32B上达到接近全精度推理的性能。

Abstract: As Large Language Models (LLMs) advance toward practical deployment, the Microscaling FP4 (MXFP4) format has emerged as a cornerstone for next-generation low-bit inference, owing to its ability to balance high dynamic range with hardware efficiency. However, directly applying MXFP4 to LLM activation quantization inevitably leads to significant accuracy degradation. In this paper, we theoretically analyze the error structure of MXFP4 activation quantization, revealing that the root cause of this performance drop lies in two structural imbalances between activation distributions and the MXFP4 block floating-point format: (1) extreme inter-block variance imbalance and (2) intra-block codebook utilization imbalance. To address these challenges, we propose TORQ (Two-level Orthogonal Rotation for MXFP4 Quantization), a training-free Post-Training Quantization (PTQ) framework designed to reshape the geometric properties of the activation space through optimal coordinate transformations. At the macroscopic level, TORQ leverages the Schur-Horn theorem to redistribute activation energy via inter-block orthogonal rotation, preventing high-variance blocks from driving up shared scaling factors and thereby preserving the precision of small-magnitude elements. At the microscopic level, TORQ employs maximum-entropy-guided intra-block rotation to alleviate codebook collapse and maximize the MXFP4 codebook's information capacity. Experiments on mainstream LLMs such as LLaMA3 and Qwen3 show that TORQ significantly improves the accuracy of MXFP4 activation quantization compared to existing methods: on Qwen3-32B, the perplexity on WikiText is reduced to 8.43 (vs. 7.61 for BF16), and the average accuracy increases from 38.40% with direct RTN to 73.63% (vs. 74.82% for BF16), substantially narrowing the gap between 4-bit floating-point quantization and full-precision inference.

</details>


### [229] [Online Market Making and the Value of Observing the Order Book](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.19584)
*Davide Maran,Marcello Restelli*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文研究带动作依赖反馈的在线做市问题。当发生交易时交易者估值被隐藏（标准 bandit 反馈），未发生交易时则暴露供需信息。研究证明这种额外信息可根本性改善可学习性：随机环境下无需平滑假设即可达 O(√T) 最优后悔，均值回复价格过程和对抗环境分别达 O(√T) 和 O(T^{2/3}) 后悔界，贡献了新的浓度不等式。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 传统在线学习做市问题采用全 censored 反馈模型（仅当交易发生时才有反馈），忽略了未交易时订单簿所蕴含的供需信息。本文认为这种 action-dependent 反馈模型更贴近真实限价订单簿场景，可能从根本上改变问题的可学习性。

Method: 1. 随机设置：设计基于消除法的算法（elimination-based algorithm），无需对交易者估值分布做平滑假设
2. 均值回复价格过程：分别考虑局部自回归动态和基于累积偏差的全局漂移条件，利用新的浓度不等式建立高概率 O(√T) 后悔界
3. 对抗设置：采用"探索-扰动"（explore-then-perturb）算法框架

Result: 1. 随机环境（i.i.d. 市价）：高概率 O(√T) 后悔界
2. 广泛均值回复价格过程：局部自回归动态或弱全局漂移条件下均建立高概率 O(√T) 后悔界
3. 对抗环境（oblivious 价格）：期望 O(T^{2/3}) 后悔界

Conclusion: 本文证明了在市场报价问题中，观察订单簿（而非仅依赖交易反馈）可从根本上改善学习算法的后悔界。在随机环境下达到 O(√T) 最优后悔界，在对抗环境下达到 O(T^{2/3}) 后悔界，且不需要对交易者估值分布做平滑性假设，为实际高频做市场景提供了理论支撑。

Abstract: We study an online market-making problem in which a learner sequentially posts bid and ask prices for a single asset while interacting with traders holding private valuations. Unlike existing online learning formulations that assume fully censored feedback, we introduce an action-dependent feedback model inspired by real limit order books: when a trade occurs, the trader's valuation remains hidden, whereas when no trade occurs, informative feedback about supply and demand is revealed.
  We show that this additional information fundamentally changes the learnability of the problem. In the stochastic setting with i.i.d. market prices, we propose an elimination-based algorithm that achieves $O(\sqrt T)$ regret with high probability, without requiring any smoothness assumptions on the distribution of trader valuations. We then extend this result to a broad class of mean-reverting price processes by considering both local, autoregressive dynamics and a weaker global drift condition based on cumulative deviations from the mean. Under either assumption, we establish high-probability $O(\sqrt T)$ regret bounds, relying on a new concentration inequality of independent interest. Finally, in the adversarial setting with oblivious prices, we design an explore-then-perturb algorithm that guarantees $O(T^{2/3})$ regret in expectation.
  Our results quantify the value of observing the order book in online market making and demonstrate that even limited, action-dependent feedback can substantially improve regret guarantees compared to standard bandit feedback models.

</details>


### [230] [MiMuon: Mixed Muon Optimizer with Improved Generalization for Large Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.19619)
*Feihu Huang,Yuning Luo,Songcan Chen*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 该论文研究了Muon优化器的泛化误差理论，证明其泛化界为O(1/(Nκ^T))，并提出混合优化器MiMuon将泛化误差改善至O(1/N)，同时保持O(1/T^(1/4))的收敛速度，实验验证了在Qwen3-0.6B和YOLO26m等大模型上的有效性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: Muon优化器在矩阵参数模型上展现出显著的收敛速度优势，但其泛化性能尚未被理论刻画，存在研究空白。

Method: 基于算法稳定性(algorithmic stability)和数学归纳法(mathematical induction)分析Muon的泛化误差；通过谨慎使用梯度正交化将Muon与动量SGD混合，提出MiMuon优化器。

Result: 证明Muon的泛化误差为O(1/(Nκ^T))，其中κ为梯度估计奇异值最小差值；MiMuon的泛化误差改善为O(1/N)（因κ通常很小）；MiMuon保持与Muon相同的O(1/T^(1/4))收敛速率。

Conclusion: MiMuon优化器结合了Muon的梯度正交化技术和SGD的动量机制，在保持O(1/T^(1/4))收敛速度的同时，将泛化误差从O(1/(Nκ^T))降低到O(1/N)。实验验证了MiMuon在Qwen3-0.6B和YOLO26m等大模型训练中的有效性。

Abstract: Matrix-structured parameters frequently appear in many artificial intelligence models such as large language models. More recently, an efficient Muon optimizer is designed for matrix parameters of large-scale models, and shows markedly faster convergence than the vector-wise algorithms. Although some works have begun to study convergence properties (i.e., optimization error) of the Muon optimizer, its generalization properties (i.e., generalization error) is still not established. Thus, in this paper, we study generalization error of the Muon optimizer based on algorithmic stability and mathematical induction, and prove that the Muon has a generalization error of $O\big(\frac{1}{Nκ^{T}}\big)$, where $N$ is training sample size, and $T$ denotes iteration number, and $κ>0$ denotes minimum difference between singular values of gradient estimate. To enhance generalization of the Muon, we propose an effective mixed Muon (MiMuon) optimizer by cautiously using orthogonalization of gradient, which is a hybrid of Muon and momentum-based SGD optimizers. Then we prove that our MiMuon optimizer has a lower generalization error of $O\big(\frac{1}{N}\big)$ than $O\big(\frac{1}{Nκ^{T}}\big)$ of Muon optimizer, since $κ$ generally is very small. Meanwhile, we also studied the convergence properties of our MiMuon algorithm, and prove that our MiMuon algorithm has the same convergence rate of $O(\frac{1}{T^{1/4}})$ as the Muon algorithm. Some numerical experimental results on training large models including Qwen3-0.6B and YOLO26m demonstrate efficiency of the MiMuon optimizer.

</details>


### [231] [Optimal Reconstruction from Linear Queries](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.19625)
*Yuval Filmus,Shay Moran,Elizaveta Nesterova*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 该论文研究高维空间中从近似线性查询重构未知点的问题。当查询数量趋于无穷时,最优误差收敛到√(2d/(d+1))δ这一显式极限;固定维度下误差以双指数速度衰减,而维度增长时需要指数级(约exp(d))查询才能实现误差趋零。核心技术贡献是将1901年的经典Jung定理推广为鲁棒变体。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 该研究源于多个实际应用场景的需求,包括低维遥感与信号恢复、高维数据分析以及隐私敏感推理。这些场景共同面临的核心挑战是:如何在有限近似线性查询下最优重构高维空间中的未知点,并明确刻画重构误差与资源消耗之间的权衡关系。

Method: 采用几何与动力系统方法,利用对称性和李群作用进行分析。具体技术贡献是将Jung定理(1901)从经典的"直径为1的集合最大可能半径"问题推广到"近似极值体的鲁棒特征刻画",建立了估计误差与查询数量之间的精确关系。

Result: (1)极限误差:当查询数T→∞时,最优重构误差收敛至√(2d/(d+1))δ;(2)收敛速度:固定维度下超额误差以双指数速度衰减,快于典型学习曲线;(3)维度依赖:维度增长时,需要约exp(d)量级查询才能实现误差趋零;(4)理论工具:建立了Jung定理的鲁棒推广版本。

Conclusion: The paper establishes that the optimal reconstruction error converges to √(2d/(d+1))δ as query数量无限增长。当维度固定时,超额误差以双指数速度衰减;当维度增长时,需要约exp(d)数量级的查询才能实现误差趋近于零。该研究将经典的Jung定理(1901)推广为鲁棒变体,建立了近似线性查询下点重构的完整误差分析框架。

Abstract: We study the problem of reconstructing an unknown point in $\mathbb{R}^d$ from approximate linear queries. This setting arises naturally in applications ranging from low-dimensional remote sensing and signal recovery to high-dimensional data analysis and privacy-sensitive inference. Our main goal is to characterize the optimal reconstruction error as a function of the number of queries $T$, the ambient dimension $d$, and the noise parameter $δ$.
  We first analyze the limit $T \to \infty$ and show that the optimal reconstruction error converges to the explicit value $\sqrt{2d/(d+1)} δ$, which plays a role analogous to the Bayes optimal error in supervised learning. When the dimension is fixed, we show that the excess error above this limit decays doubly exponentially fast as $T \to \infty$, a rate that is significantly faster than those typically encountered in learning curves. When the dimension grows, we show that a number of queries on the order of $\exp(d)$ is necessary and sufficient to achieve vanishing excess error. Finally, we introduce and analyze an improper variant of the reconstruction problem.
  From a technical perspective, our main contribution is a generalization of Jung's theorem (1901). The classical theorem bounds the maximum possible radius of a set of diameter 1 and characterizes extremal bodies. Our generalization provides a robust variant that characterizes near-extremal bodies and is proved via geometric and dynamical arguments exploiting symmetry and Lie group actions.

</details>


### [232] [MSAlign: Aligning Molecule and Mass Spectra Foundation Models for Metabolite Identification](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.19752)
*Paul Krzakala,Gabriel Melo,Camille Lançon,Charlotte Laclau,Rémi Flamary,Etienne Thévenot,Florence d'Alché-Buc*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: Summary generation failed


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: Motivation analysis unavailable

Method: Method extraction failed

Result: Result analysis unavailable

Conclusion: Conclusion extraction failed

Abstract: Accurately identifying metabolites i.e. small molecules from mass spectrometry data remains a core challenge in metabolomics, with broad applications in drug discovery, environmental analysis, and clinical research. We address the Molecule Retrieval task, which consists in recovering the chemical structure of a metabolite from its MS/MS spectrum given a set of candidate molecules. While the recent release of benchmark datasets such as MassSpecGym and Spectraverse has considerably accelerated the development of novel machine learning approaches, the complexity of data preprocessing pipelines and the lack of unified implementations make methods and results difficult to reproduce and compare. We make three contributions. First, we propose a unified framework encompassing recent approaches based on representation alignment and contrastive learning. Second, we introduce MSAlign, inspired by multimodal alignment in vision-language models, which learns a shared representation space by aligning two frozen foundation models (DreaMS for mass spectra and ChemBERTa for molecules) through lightweight MLP projections trained with a candidate-based contrastive objective. MSAlign is simple to implement, fast to train and consistently outperforms existing approaches across all benchmarks. Third, we investigate a long-standing evaluation problem: data splitting strategies in molecule retrieval implicitly trade off data leakage against domain shift. We formalize this tension by introducing a quantitative measure of distribution shift, and use it to evaluate splitting strategies in existing benchmarks. All datasets, splits, candidate sets, and a unified implementation of MSAlign and baselines are publicly released to support reproducible research.

</details>


### [233] [AR1-ZO: Topology-Aware Rank-1 Zeroth-Order Queries for High-Rank LoRA Fine-Tuning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.19767)
*Ziye Chen,Hongbin Lin,Chenyu Zhang,Xiangda Yan,Yongjie Yang,Yao Shu*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文解决零阶（ZO）优化与LoRA结合时的"秩悖论"问题：当LoRA秩增加时适配器容量提升，但标准两点ZO要么扰动秩依赖数量的坐标，要么在原子级更新下使有限差分信号不可观测。作者提出AR1-ZO方法，通过交替秩-1原子查询与拓扑感知缩放γ=αr，在无需辅助基、激活钩子、曲率估计或额外前向查询的情况下恢复秩不变的有效信号。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 零阶优化允许在不需要存储反向传播激活的情况下微调大语言模型，LoRA提供紧凑的可训练适配器。然而将两者结合时存在"秩悖论"：增加LoRA秩虽然提升适配器容量，但标准两点ZO方法要么扰动秩依赖数量的坐标，要么在原子级更新下导致有限差分信号无法观测。这是一个测量-拓扑问题，而非需要外部子空间的问题。

Method: 核心洞察：LoRA本身已分解为匹配的秩-1原子，每个原子是维度为d_out+d_in的完整因子坐标块。AR1-ZO方法：1) 每步仅查询一个原子，保持存储适配器秩r；2) 从单次查询扰动维度中移除r；3) 采用拓扑感知缩放γ=αr替代标准LoRA缩放α/r，恢复秩不变的有效有限差分信噪比（FD-SNR）。

Result: 理论上证明了：原子极小性、秩无关的有效查询维度、方向坍缩及其恢复、以及剩余的秩依赖作为摊销覆盖成本。实验在OPT和Qwen3模型上验证了信号机制，表明在标准两次前向传播查询预算下，AR1-ZO使高秩LoRA在匹配预算的ZO方法中变得有效。

Conclusion: AR1-ZO通过交替秩-1原子查询与拓扑感知缩放，成功化解了ZO优化与高秩LoRA结合的秩悖论，在保持秩不变信号的同时无需任何辅助机制，为在激活存储受限场景下高效微调大语言模型提供了新思路。

Abstract: Zeroth-order (ZO) optimization enables large-language-model fine-tuning without storing backpropagation activations, while LoRA supplies compact trainable adapters. Combining them creates a rank paradox: increasing LoRA rank improves adapter capacity, but standard two-point ZO either perturbs a rank-dependent number of coordinates or, under atomwise updates, can make the finite-difference signal unobservable. This paper shows that the bottleneck is a measurement-topology problem rather than a need for an external subspace. LoRA already decomposes into matched rank-$1$ atoms, each a complete factor-coordinate block of dimension $d_\text{out}+d_\text{in}$. Querying one atom per step keeps the stored adapter rank $r$ while removing $r$ from the single-query perturbation dimension. The naive atomwise query is still miscalibrated: if it inherits canonical LoRA scaling $α/r$, the active finite-difference signal shrinks as $1/r$ and the active finite-difference signal-to-noise ratio (FD-SNR) as $1/r^2$, producing directional collapse under a fixed residual evaluation-noise floor. AR1-ZO pairs alternating rank-$1$ atom queries with topology-aware scaling $γ=αr$, restoring rank-invariant active signal without auxiliary bases, activation hooks, curvature estimates, or extra forward queries. Theory proves atom minimality, rank-independent active query dimension, directional collapse and restoration, and the remaining rank dependence as an amortized coverage cost. Experiments on OPT and Qwen3 models validate the signal mechanism and show that AR1-ZO makes high-rank LoRA effective among matched-budget ZO methods under the standard two-forward-pass query budget.

</details>


### [234] [Latent Laplace Diffusion for Irregular Multivariate Time Series](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.19805)
*Zinuo You,Jin Zheng,John Cartlidge*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: LLapDiff proposes a novel latent diffusion framework that overcomes the discrete-continuous trade-off in irregular multivariate time series forecasting by modeling latent trajectories with Laplace-domain parameterization, eliminating the need for step-by-step temporal integration.


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: Existing methods for irregular multivariate time series forecasting face a fundamental trade-off: discrete-time methods distort temporal structure through re-gridding, while continuous-time models require sequential solvers that are prone to drift during long-horizon forecasting.

Method: LLapDiff models the target as a low-dimensional latent trajectory in a generative framework. The reverse diffusion process is guided by a stable modal parameterization derived from stochastic port-Hamiltonian dynamics, with mean evolution parameterized in the Laplace domain via learnable complex-conjugate poles. A renewal-averaging analysis links continuous dynamics to irregular observations by mapping sampling gaps to effective event-domain poles, motivating a gap-aware history summarizer.

Result: Experiments demonstrate that LLapDiff improves over baseline methods in long-horizon forecasting tasks. Additionally, its continuous-time generative nature naturally supports missing-value imputation by querying the same model at historical timestamps.

Conclusion: LLapDiff advances irregular multivariate time series forecasting by combining latent trajectory modeling with Laplace-domain parameterization, enabling horizon-wide generation without sequential integration. The gap-aware history summarizer and missing-value imputation capability further extend its practical utility.

Abstract: Irregular multivariate time series impose a trade-off for long-horizon forecasting: discrete methods can distort temporal structure via re-gridding, while continuous-time models often require sequential solvers prone to drift. To bridge this gap, we present Latent Laplace Diffusion (LLapDiff), a generative framework that models the target as a low-dimensional latent trajectory, enabling horizon-wide generation without step-by-step integration over physical time. We guide the reverse process utilizing a stable modal parameterization motivated by stochastic port-Hamiltonian dynamics, and parameterize its mean evolution in the Laplace domain via learnable complex-conjugate poles, enabling direct evaluation over irregular timestamps. We also link continuous dynamics to irregular observations through renewal-averaging analysis, which maps sampling gaps to effective event-domain poles and motivates a gap-aware history summarizer. Extensive experiments show that LLapDiff improves over baselines in long-horizon forecasting, and its continuous-time generative nature supports missing-value imputation by querying the same model at historical timestamps. Code is available at https://github.com/pixelhero98/LLapDiffusion.

</details>


### [235] [LionMuon: Alternating Spectral and Sign Descent for Efficient Training](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.19811)
*Arman Bolatov,Artem Riabinin,Nikita Kornilov,Andrey Veprikov,Samuel Horváth,Martin Takáč,Aleksandr Beznosikov*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: LionMuon combines Lion and Muon optimizers via periodic alternation (period P), achieving Muon's effectiveness at sign-based methods' computational cost. Matches Lion's memory footprint (half AdamW). At P=2, Pareto-dominates all baselines. Theoretically supported by heavy-tailed noise complexity bounds.


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: Sign-based optimizers (Lion, Signum) have cheap per-step updates but weaker directions; Muon has stronger directions but higher per-step cost. A method combining Muon's effectiveness with sign-based methods' computational efficiency is desired.

Method: LionMuon alternates Lion and Muon updates with period P, sharing a single dual-EMA momentum buffer. SignMuon (simpler single-EMA variant) also outperforms pure Muon. State memory matches Lion (half of AdamW).

Result: At P=2, LionMuon achieves lower validation loss at lower compute than all competitors across 124M-720M model scales. Sharp complexity bounds established under heavy-tailed noise, governed by period-averaged smoothness and noise constants.

Conclusion: LionMuon achieves optimal performance-memory trade-off with P=2, Pareto-dominating Muon, Lion, Signum, and AdamW across all tested scales. Theoretically grounded with sharp complexity bounds under heavy-tailed noise.

Abstract: In large-scale optimization, the cheapness and effectiveness of update steps are the most crucial factors for a successful optimizer. Sign-based optimizers like Lion or Signum produce cheap per-step updates, whereas Muon's spectral matrix-sign update gives a much stronger direction at a substantially higher per-step cost. In this work, we propose LionMuon, which retains the effectiveness of Muon steps while considerably cutting the averaged iteration cost, similar to sign-based methods. It alternates between Lion's and Muon's updates on a fixed period P, sharing a single dual-EMA momentum buffer between them. The optimizer state memory therefore matches Lion and is exactly half of AdamW's. A simpler single-EMA variant, SignMuon, by itself already outperforms pure Muon. At P = 2, LionMuon Pareto-dominates Muon, Lion, Signum, and AdamW on every dataset and architecture we tested at 124M model size, reaching lower validation loss at lower compute, and the same advantage persists at 355M and 720M scale. On the theory side, we prove sharp complexity bounds under heavy-tailed noise which are governed by period-averaged smoothness and noise that interpolate between Muon's and Lion's constants. These bounds predict the compute-optimal period and the conditions under which LionMuon outruns Muon and Lion. Code: https://github.com/brain-lab-research/lion-muon

</details>


### [236] [FLUXtrapolation: A benchmark on extrapolating ecosystem fluxes](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.19812)
*Anya Fries,Jacob A Nelson,Martin Jung,Markus Reichstein,Jonas Peters*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: FLUXtrapolation是一个用于评估生态系统通量外推能力的基准测试，针对跨气候、生态系统类型和环境条件的协变量分布偏移（P_X）以及重要驱动因素在全球尺度上不可观测的条件偏移（P_{Y|X}）进行建模。该基准定义了时间、空间和温度三种外推场景，并从尾部误差和多尺度角度评估模型性能。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 生态系统通量（碳、水、能量的通量）对于理解全球碳循环、水循环和能量循环至关重要，但只能在稀疏分布的观测塔进行直接测量。通量上upscaling是一个具有挑战性的领域泛化问题，模型需要在观测站点训练而在未观测区域预测，面临协变量分布偏移和条件偏移的双重挑战。

Method: FLUXtrapolation基于通量上upscaling领域专业知识设计，定义了三种外推场景：时间外推、空间外推和基于温度的外推。评估维度包括：跨留出域的性能、跨时间聚合的性能以及尾部误差。采用Hourly RMSE中位数和尾部误差的多尺度评估指标。

Result: 初步实验表明，基线模型在中位数小时RMSE指标上表现相近，但在本文提出的尾部聚焦评估和多尺度评估下表现出明显差异。

Conclusion: FLUXtrapolation为机器学习在分布偏移下的方法提供了一个现实且相关的挑战，其进展可直接支持改善通量上upscaling这一科学目标。该基准测试揭示了现有方法在处理尾部预测和跨域泛化方面的不足。

Abstract: We introduce FLUXtrapolation, a benchmark for extrapolating ecosystem fluxes under progressively harder distribution shifts. Ecosystem fluxes are central to understanding the carbon, water, and energy cycles, yet they can only be measured directly at sparsely located measurement towers. Producing global flux estimates therefore requires training models on observed sites using globally available covariates and predicting in unobserved regions, that is, upscaling. Flux upscaling is a challenging domain generalization problem that is affected by a shift in covariate distribution across climates, ecosystem types, and environmental conditions, as well as by conditional shift: important drivers remain unobserved at global scale. We provide a quantitative analysis of both these shifts in $P_X$ and $P_{Y\mid X}$. FLUXtrapolation is designed based on domain expertise on flux upscaling: it defines temporal, spatial, and temperature-based extrapolation scenarios and evaluates performance across held-out domains, temporal aggregations, and tail errors. In a pilot study, we find that baselines perform similarly under median hourly RMSE, but separate under the proposed tail-focused and multi-scale evaluation. FLUXtrapolation therefore poses a realistic and thus relevant challenge for machine learning methods under distribution shift; at the same time, progress on this benchmark would directly support the scientific goal of improving flux upscaling.

</details>


### [237] [General Lower Bounds for Differentially Private Federated Learning with Arbitrary Public-Transcript Interactions](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.19813)
*Yicheng Li*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: The paper proves a general lower bound (federated van Trees bound) on the statistical accuracy achievable in differentially private federated learning protocols, covering arbitrary adaptive multi-round settings with sample reuse, with applications to mean estimation, linear regression, and nonparametric regression.


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: Understanding the fundamental statistical limits of differentially private federated learning is critical, especially given the widespread adoption of FL systems and the tension between privacy guarantees and statistical utility. Prior work on lower bounds in this setting has been limited, leaving a gap in our understanding of optimal trade-offs.

Method: The proof hinges on a privacy-information contraction inequality for complete public transcripts. This inequality characterizes how zCDP constraints translate into information-theoretic restrictions on what estimators can learn, yielding a federated analogue of the classical van Trees bound.

Result: The result establishes a federated van Trees lower bound applicable to any estimator under total clientwise sample-level zCDP constraints. This bound is further specialized and illustrated through concrete applications in mean estimation, linear regression, and nonparametric regression, demonstrating its broad applicability.

Conclusion: The work provides a unified lower bound framework for private federated learning, demonstrating fundamental limits that apply broadly across estimation tasks and protocol designs.

Abstract: We prove a general lower bound for differentially private federated learning protocols with arbitrary public-transcript interactions. The protocol may use any number of adaptive rounds, and each client's local samples may be reused across rounds. For parameter estimation under squared \(\ell_2\) loss, we establish a federated van Trees lower bound for every estimator satisfying a total clientwise sample-level zero-concentrated differential privacy (zCDP) constraint. The main technical ingredient is a privacy-information contraction inequality for complete public transcripts. We illustrate the bound through applications to mean estimation, linear regression, and nonparametric regression.

</details>


### [238] [Set-Valued Policy Learning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.19830)
*Laura Fuentes-Vicente,Mathieu Even,Gaëlle Dormion,Antoine Chambaz,Uri Shalit,Julie Josse*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出集合值策略学习范式，通过保角推断和随机性注入方法解决多治疗场景下传统单点推荐策略的敏感性问题和不确定性量化难题，在合成数据及IVF应用中验证了方法的稳健性和可操作性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 传统治疗策略仅输出单一推荐，对估计不确定性、模型规格和有限样本变异性高度敏感，且缺乏关于推荐行动置信度的指导。

Method: 提出多治疗场景下的集合值策略学习范式，扩展学习延迟框架，通过最大下界（greatest Lower Bound）方法和保角策略学习（conformal policy learning）连接未观测到的真实最优治疗与估计的最优治疗规则，并开发随机性注入方法以保证边际覆盖。

Result: 集合值策略使不确定性量化成为内在特性，预测集合大小反映决策模糊度，无需对底层黑箱最优治疗规则做假设即可保证边际覆盖。

Conclusion: 该方法在合成数据和体外受精（IVF）真实应用中验证有效，能够产生稳健且可操作的策略，自然地融入临床考虑，同时有效平衡性能与可靠性。

Abstract: Conventional treatment policies map patient covariates to a single recommended intervention in order to maximize expected clinical outcomes. Although a rich body of causal inference methods has been developed to estimate such policies, point-valued recommendations can be highly sensitive to estimation uncertainty, model specification, and finite-sample variability, while typically providing little guidance about how confident one should be in the recommended action. In this work, we propose a set-valued policy learning paradigm for the multiple-treatment setting, in which policies output a set of plausible treatments rather than a single recommendation. This formulation enables intrinsic uncertainty quantification, with the size of the predicted set reflecting the degree of decision ambiguity. We extend the learning-to-defer framework to multiple treatments via a novel \textit{greatest Lower Bound} method, and introduce \textit{conformal policy learning}, which bridges the gap between unobserved ground-truth optimal treatments and estimated optimal treatment rules. Drawing on insights from the noisy-label literature, we develop a randomness-injection approach that guarantees marginal coverage without requiring assumptions on underlying black-box optimal treatment rules. Through experiments on synthetic data and a real-world application to In-Vitro Fertilization (IVF), we demonstrate that our methods produce robust and actionable policies that naturally incorporate clinical considerations while effectively balancing performance and reliability.

</details>


### [239] [A Closed-loop, State-centric, Multi-agent Framework for Passenger Load Estimation from Heterogeneous Data Streams](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.19834)
*Yiyao Xu,Hao Zhou,Yuhang Wang,Jingran Sun*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: Summary generation failed


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: Motivation analysis unavailable

Method: Method extraction failed

Result: Result analysis unavailable

Conclusion: Conclusion extraction failed

Abstract: To support operations and passenger-facing services, transit agencies need reliable passenger load trajectories. Currently, load estimates are typically inferred from imperfect sensing systems rather than fully observed, and the accuracy of modern automatic passenger counting (APC) systems still varies with station layout, flow intensity, and operating conditions. To address the challenges of robust passenger load estimation from heterogeneous data streams, including incremental count errors, evidence conflicts, and context-dependent sensor reliability, we propose a closed-loop, state-centric, multi-agent framework. This method enforces physical feasibility at every step, allocates trust dynamically among evidence sources, and feeds physics-derived violation residuals back into training for robustness improvement. The architecture consists of a unified stop-event backbone, a coupled Perception--Physical--Fusion loop for stop-by-stop inference, and optional trip-level macro-correction and closed-loop calibration modules.

</details>


### [240] [StableGrad: Backward Scale Control without Batch Normalization](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.19856)
*Jose I. Mestre,Alberto Fernández-Hernández,Cristian Pérez-Corral,Manuel F. Dolz,Enrique S. Quintana-Ortí*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: Summary generation failed


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: Motivation analysis unavailable

Method: Method extraction failed

Result: Result analysis unavailable

Conclusion: Conclusion extraction failed

Abstract: Training very deep neural networks requires controlling the propagation of magnitudes across depth. Without such control, activations and gradients may vanish, explode, or enter unstable regimes that make optimization fail. Modern architectures often mitigate this problem through Batch Normalization, residual connections, or other normalization layers, which repeatedly re-scale or bypass intermediate representations. However, these mechanisms are not always appropriate. In Physics-Informed Neural Networks (PINNs), the network represents a continuous physical field and its input derivatives define the training objective, making batch-dependent normalization problematic because it can introduce non-local dependencies into the predicted field and its derivatives. We propose StableGrad, an optimizer-level scale-control mechanism that corrects layer-wise weight-gradient imbalances without modifying the forward model. Because the normalization is applied only after backpropagation and before the optimizer update, the network output, its derivatives, and the physical residual remain unchanged. We analyze the effective training dynamics induced by this rescaling and evaluate StableGrad on deep PINNs as the target application, with BatchNorm-free convolutional networks serving as a diagnostic stress test. On PINN benchmarks, StableGrad improves matched-depth solution accuracy and makes deeper models more reliable under standard optimization. On ResNet and EfficientNet architectures, where removing Batch Normalization normally leads to training collapse, StableGrad stabilizes optimization without introducing any other architectural change. These results show that optimizer-level control of weight-gradient scale can provide a practical alternative when forward normalization is unavailable or undesirable.

</details>


### [241] [JAXenstein: Accelerated Benchmarking for First-Person Environments](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.19926)
*Ruo Yu Tao,George Konidaris*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: JAXenstein是一个基于JAX的开源强化学习基准测试，通过移植Wolfenstein 3D渲染引擎填补了JAX生态系统在视觉第一人称任务基准方面的空白。该基准在速度上优于现有同类方案数倍，且具有良好的可扩展性，可用于测试智能体的探索能力和部分可观测环境处理能力。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 当前JAX强化学习生态系统中缺乏针对视觉第一人称任务的基准测试，这类任务对于测试智能体的探索能力和处理部分可观测性的能力至关重要。同时，算法开发过程中实验环境供给不足已成为制约研究进展的瓶颈。

Method: 基于Wolfenstein 3D渲染引擎，在JAX框架下实现了快速且可扩展的视觉第一人称任务环境。该实现利用了现代机器学习工具的优势，通过代码优化实现了几倍于现有同类视觉基准的运行速度。

Result: JAXenstein在运行速度上显著优于同类视觉基准测试数倍，同时保持了良好的可扩展性，易于扩展至更复杂的第一人称领域。

Conclusion: JAXenstein作为首个JAX生态下的视觉第一人称RL基准测试，为研究社区提供了一个开源、高效且可扩展的实验平台，填补了JAX RL生态系统在视觉第一人称任务基准测试方面的空白，有望推动该领域探索算法和部分可观测环境研究的进展。

Abstract: The progression of reinforcement learning algorithms have been driven by challenging benchmarks. The rate in which a researcher can iterate on a problem setting directly impacts the speed of algorithm development. Modern machine learning has produced tools that allow for fast and scalable algorithm development like the JAX library. With the availability of these tools, a serious bottleneck in algorithm development is the availability of large and complex domains for experimentation. Most notably, the JAX reinforcement learning ecosystem does not have any benchmarks that test visual first-person tasks; these domains are crucial for testing both exploration and an agent's ability to overcome partial observability. We introduce JAXenstein: an open-source JAX-based benchmark that implements the Wolfenstein 3D rendering engine for fast and scalable experimentation in visual first-person tasks. JAXenstein is several times faster than comparable vision-based benchmarks, and is easily extensible to more complex first-person domains.

</details>


### [242] [Exploiting Non-Negativity in DAG Structure Learning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.19947)
*Samuel Rey,Madeline navarro,Gonzalo Mateos*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文针对线性结构方程模型下的DAG学习问题，通过引入非负边权重约束简化了圈性约束条件，提出了基于增广拉格朗日方法的优化算法，并在理论上证明了该方法具有良性全局景观（真DAG为唯一全局最优解且无虚假驻点），数值实验验证了其优越性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: DAG学习在信号处理、机器学习和因果推断中具有核心地位，但圈性作为全局组合性质带来计算挑战。现有的连续约束方法仍面临非凸优化景观和退化的KKT条件问题。

Method: 利用非负边权重约束简化圈约束表征，通过增广拉格朗日方法（ADMM）求解正则化非负DAG学习问题。

Result: 理论证明：在总体情况下，真DAG是所提增广拉格朗日形式的唯一全局最小化子；景观中不存在虚假的内部驻点；真DAG是唯一的无环KKT点。数值实验表明该方法优于现有最先进的连续DAG学习方法。

Conclusion: 非负约束产生良性优化景观，理论保证真DAG是唯一满足KKT条件的无环解，数值实验验证了方法优于现有先进方法。

Abstract: This work addresses the problem of learning directed acyclic graphs (DAGs) from nodal observations generated by a linear structural equation model. DAG learning is a central task in signal processing, machine learning, and causal inference, but it remains challenging because acyclicity is a global combinatorial property. Continuous acyclicity constraints have led to important algorithmic advances by replacing the discrete DAG constraint with smooth equality constraints. However, existing formulations still involve difficult non-convex optimization landscapes and may suffer from degenerate first-order optimality conditions. Here, we restrict attention to DAGs with non-negative edge weights and exploit this additional structure to obtain a simpler characterization of acyclicity. Building on this characterization, we formulate a regularized non-negative DAG learning problem and develop an algorithm based on the method of multipliers. We further analyze the benign optimization landscape induced by non-negativity. In the population regime, we show that the true DAG is the unique global minimizer of the proposed augmented-Lagrangian formulation; moreover, the landscape contains no spurious interior stationary points, and the true DAG is the only acyclic KKT point. Numerical experiments on synthetic and real-world data show that the proposed method improves over state-of-the-art continuous DAG-learning alternatives.

</details>


### [243] [Detecting Fluent Optimization-Based Adversarial Prompts via Sequential Entropy Changes](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.19966)
*Mohammed Alshaalan,Miguel R. D. Rodrigues*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出CPD Online，一种基于在线变化点检测的对抗后缀检测方法，通过分析token级next-token entropy流并应用CUSUM统计量，无需训练即可在多种开源LLM上实现超越窗口困惑度基线的检测性能，且能精确定位攻击触发位置。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 基于优化的对抗后缀攻击可以在保持流利性的同时绑过静态和窗口困惑度检测器，因此需要一种新的检测范式。

Method: 1) 利用LLM系统提示估计鲁棒基线；2) 对用户token entropy做标准化处理；3) 应用单边CUSUM统计量进行在线变化点检测；4) 定位对抗后缀的起始位置。

Result: CPD在1012个优化后缀攻击和1012个困惑度受控的良性提示的基准测试中，在6个开源聊天模型上均提升F1；在LLaMA-2-7B的默认CUSUM设置下，AUROC达0.88，F1达0.82；79.6%的触发点集中在对抗后缀内；集成到LLaMA Guard后可减少17-22%的门控调用。

Conclusion: CPD方法在6个开源聊天模型上均超越最强基线，F1提升显著；可定位攻击触发位置（79.6%集中在对抗后缀内）；作为LLaMA Guard的门控策略可降低17-22%调用量同时保持检测质量。

Abstract: Optimization-based adversarial suffixes can jailbreak aligned large language models (LLMs) while remaining fluent, weakening static and windowed perplexity-based detectors. We cast adversarial suffix detection as an online change-point detection problem over the token-level next-token entropy stream. Using the LLM system prompt to estimate a robust baseline, we standardize user-token entropies and apply a one-sided CUSUM statistic. The resulting detector, CPD Online (CPD), is model-agnostic, training-free, runs online, and localizes the adversarial suffix onset. On a benchmark of 1,012 optimization-based suffix attacks (GCG, AutoDAN, AdvPrompter, BEAST, AutoDAN-HGA) and 1,012 perplexity-controlled benign prompts, CPD improves F1 over the strongest windowed-perplexity baseline on all six open-weight chat models (LLaMA-2-7B/13B, Vicuna-7B/13B, Qwen2.5-7B/14B). On LLaMA-2-7B at the canonical CUSUM setting ($k=0$), CPD reaches AUROC $0.88$ and F1 $0.82$. Beyond prompt-level detection, CPD concentrates 79.6% of its triggers inside the adversarial suffix, versus 17-46% for windowed perplexity. Finally, when used as a lightweight gate for LLaMA Guard, CPD reduces guard calls by 17-22% on a high-volume, benign-dominated deployment while preserving guard-level detection quality

</details>


### [244] [Your Neighbors Know: Leveraging Local Neighborhoods for Backdoor Detection in Decentralized Learning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.19969)
*Sayan Biswas,Antoine Boutet,Davide Frey,Romaric Gaudel,Rachid Guerraoui,Maxime Jacovella,Anne-Marie Kermarrec,Dimitri Lerévérend,François Taïani,Martijn de Vos*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出Argus框架,首个针对去中心化学习(DL)的原生后门攻击检测方案。该框架无需中央协调器或先验触发器知识,利用邻居节点间的结构相似性度量来区分真实后门与数据异质性导致的误报,并提供理论收敛性保证。实验表明,Argus可将攻击成功率降低90个百分点,同时保持模型效用。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 去中心化学习(DL)无需中央服务器即可协作训练模型,但其协作特性使其易受后门攻击。后门攻击指模型在标准输入下正常运作,但在遇到特定触发器的数据时执行隐藏的恶意行为。现有DL后门攻击防御研究不足,且现有防御方案常忽略DL的约束条件。

Method: Argus框架的核心机制:(1)诚实节点本地分析接收到的模型更新,识别潜在后门触发器;(2)节点与邻居共享触发器,使用结构相似性度量区分真实后门与数据异质性引起的误报;(3)关键洞察:假阳性触发器在参与者间表现不一致,而真阳性则呈现一致模式;(4)未通过协作测试的模型更新被拒绝,持续恶意发送者最终被驱逐。

Result: 实验在三个标准数据集和三种先进基线方法上评估:(1)攻击成功率降低最多90个百分点;(2)模型效用保持在全知神谕(omniscient oracle)的5个百分点范围内;(3)随数据异质性增加,Argus相对基线的优势进一步扩大。

Conclusion: 本文首次为DL特定的后门检测机制提供理论收敛保证,证明以高概率过滤可疑模型更新可保持与标准DL相当的收敛速率。Argus作为DL原生框架,无需中央协调器或触发器先验知识,为去中心化学习环境下的后门攻击防御提供了有效解决方案。

Abstract: Decentralized learning (DL) is an emerging machine learning paradigm where nodes collaboratively train models without a central server. However, the collaborative nature of DL makes it vulnerable to backdoor attacks, where a model is taught to behave normally on standard inputs while executing hidden, malicious actions when encountering data with specific triggers. Backdoor attacks in DL remain understudied and existing defenses often overlook DL constraints. We introduce Argus, a novel backdoor detection framework native to DL that requires neither a central coordinator nor prior knowledge of the trigger. In Argus, honest nodes locally analyze received model updates to identify potential backdoor triggers. Nodes then collectively share their triggers with their neighbors and use a structural similarity metric to separate true backdoors from false alarms induced by data heterogeneity. A key insight is that false positive triggers exhibit inconsistencies across participants while true positive ones show consistent patterns. Model updates that fail this collaborative test are rejected, and persistently malicious senders are eventually evicted. We provide the first theoretical convergence guarantees for a DL-specific backdoor detection mechanism, showing that filtering out suspicious model updates with high probability preserves a convergence rate comparable to standard DL. We implement and evaluate Argus on three standard datasets and against three state-of-the-art baselines. Across settings, Argus reduces attack success rates by up to 90 points compared to no defense, while preserving model utility within 5 percentage points of an omniscient oracle. Furthermore, the effectiveness of Argus compared to baselines improves as data heterogeneity increases.

</details>


### [245] [Block-Sphere Vector Quantization](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.19972)
*Heesang Ann,Joongkyu Lee,Min-hwan Oh*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文系统比较了三种旋转量化方法(EDEN、TurboQuant、RabitQ)的理论性能差异，并提出新的BlockQuant算法，通过利用随机旋转向量的球面几何特性，在理论上证明了其对期望失真和内积失真的优越性，实验验证了实际效果。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有旋转量化器(EDEN、RabitQ、TurboQuant)的比较难以解释，因为它们依赖不同的失真准则、概率机制和实现假设，导致相对优势取决于具体评价准则而非绝对优势。

Method: 本文采用两种方法：(1) 统一理论框架比较EDEN、TurboQuant和RabitQ；(2) 提出Block-Sphere Quantization (BlockQuant)，一种基于随机旋转向量球面几何设计的分块量化算法，在球面上对分块进行量化以更好地保留旋转后嵌入的几何性质。

Result: 理论分析表明：EDEN和TurboQuant在MSE失真上表现更好；EDEN在期望内积失真上有效；RabitQ提供强的高概率控制；EDEN对期望失真度量提供特别强的保证。BlockQuant在重建MSE和期望内积失真两方面理论上均优于基线方法。

Conclusion: BlockQuant通过块球面设计在理论上优于基线方法，并在真实嵌入数据集和长上下文LLM推理任务中展现出与理论改进一致的实际收益。

Abstract: Vector quantization is a fundamental primitive for scalable machine learning systems, enabling memory-efficient storage, fast retrieval, and compressed inference. Recent rotation-based quantizers such as EDEN, RabitQ, and TurboQuant have introduced strong guarantees and empirical performance, but the surrounding comparisons have been difficult to interpret because they rely on different distortion criteria, probability regimes, and implementation assumptions. As our first contribution, we provide a unified theoretical comparison of these methods and show that their relative advantages are criterion-dependent rather than absolute: EDEN and TurboQuant are favorable for MSE distortion, EDEN is also effective for expected inner-product distortion, and RabitQ provides strong high-probability control. This comparison further clarifies that EDEN provides particularly strong guarantees for expected distortion measures. As our second contribution, we introduce Block-Sphere Quantization (BlockQuant), a new rotation-based block quantization algorithm designed around the spherical geometry of randomly rotated vectors. Unlike coordinate-wise quantizers, BlockQuant quantizes blocks on the sphere, preserving the geometry of rotated embeddings more faithfully. We prove that this block-spherical design theoretically improves over the baselines considered in this paper for both reconstruction MSE and expected inner-product distortion. Our experiments on real embedding datasets and long-context LLM inference tasks show practical gains that are consistent with our theoretical improvements.

</details>


### [246] [Learning with Foresight: Enhancing Neural Routing Policy via Multi-Node Lookahead Prediction](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.19975)
*Xia Jiang,Yaoxin Wu,Yew-Soon Ong,Yingqian Zhang*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: MnLP是一种新型训练策略，通过在训练阶段预测多个未来节点来增强神经策略的长期规划能力，同时保持推理效率不变。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有神经策略的训练范式存在根本性局限：主要关注下一节点预测进行解构建，导致短视决策，削弱了神经策略的长期规划能力。

Method: 该方法设计了因果和可丢弃的MnLP模块，仅在训练阶段运行以保持推理效率；通过将多深度辅助监督纳入损失函数，实现长期上下文理解能力。

Result: 在各种问题规模、分布和真实基准测试中，MnLP均优于现有训练方法，显著提升了神经策略的泛化能力。

Conclusion: MnLP通过无缝集成到多种神经架构中，在不增加推理开销的情况下显著提升了神经策略的泛化能力。

Abstract: Neural policies have shown promise in solving vehicle routing problems due to their reduced reliance on handcrafted heuristics. However, current training paradigms suffer from a fundamental limitation: they primarily focus on next-node prediction for solution construction, resulting in myopic decision-making that undermines long-horizon planning capacity. To this end, we introduce Multi-node Lookahead Prediction (MnLP), a novel training strategy that extends the supervised learning paradigm to predict multiple future nodes simultaneously. We incorporate causal and discardable MnLP modules that operate exclusively during training, facilitating models to anticipate multi-step decisions while preserving inference-time efficiency. By incorporating multi-depth auxiliary supervision into the loss function, MnLP equips neural policies with the ability of long-range contextual understanding. Experimentally, MnLP outperforms existing training methods, improving the generalization capability of neural policies across various problem sizes, distributions, and real-world benchmarks. Moreover, MnLP can be seamlessly integrated into diverse neural architectures without introducing additional inference overhead.

</details>


### [247] [LLM Benchmark Datasets Should Be Contamination-Resistant](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.19999)
*Ali Al-Lawati,Jason Lucas,Dongwon Lee,Suhang Wang*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 论文针对LLM预训练污染基准数据集的问题，提出"抗污染基准"（contamination-resistant/unlearnable but inference-supportive）的概念，通过利用Transformer架构的推理-训练非对称性设计抗污染数据集，并呼吁社区推进相关方法与平台开发。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 当前基准数据集普遍存在污染问题（即被包含在预训练语料中），导致其作为模型泛化能力评估工具的价值被削弱。

Method: 利用Transformer架构中推理与训练 pipeline 之间的非对称性来实现污染抵抗性，并通过数学改进使这些数据集可跨不同LLM架构互操作。

Result: 提出了污染抵抗性（不可学习但支持推理）数据集的概念与设计原则，包括广泛适用性和架构互操作性等数学特性。

Conclusion: 作者呼吁社区采取行动：(i) 推进新型抗污染方法，(ii) 开发支持方法和平台，(iii) 将抗污染基准纳入现有评估流程。

Abstract: Benchmark datasets are critical for reproducible, reliable, and discriminative evaluation of LLMs. However, recent studies reveal that many benchmark datasets are included in pretraining corpora, i.e., $\textit{contaminated}$, which diminishes their value as reliable measures of model generalization. In this paper, we argue that benchmark datasets should be $\textit{contamination-resistant}$, i.e., $\textit{unlearnable}$, but support $\textit{inference}$. To accomplish this, we first highlight the wide prevalence of benchmark dataset contamination and outline the properties of contamination-resistant datasets. Second, we highlight how the asymmetry between the inference and training pipelines in the Transformer architecture can be leveraged to support contamination-resistance. Third, we outline mathematical advancements to make these datasets interoperable across various LLM architectures. Based on the above, we call on the community to ensure the reliability of LLM benchmarking by: (i) advancing novel contamination-resistant methodologies, (ii) developing supporting methods and platforms, and (iii) adopting contamination-resistant benchmarks into existing evaluation pipelines.

</details>


### [248] [Fine-Tuning Without Forgetting via Loss-Adaptive Learning Rates](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.20005)
*Parjanya Prajakta Prashant,Jiongli Zhu,Aldan Creo,Babak Salimi*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文针对LLM微调中的灾难性遗忘问题，提出基于"遗忘受学习率×√(当前损失)约束"这一发现，设计了FINCH损失自适应学习率调度器。该方法在高损失批次降低学习率以抑制遗忘，同时保持微调目标不变。在知识获取、科学和低资源语言适应等基准上，实现了平均93%的遗忘减少且任务性能不下降。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有方法通过抑制高损失 token 来缓解遗忘，但这些 token 对学习新任务至关重要，尤其是预训练覆盖不足的领域。需要在不抑制高损失 token 的情况下控制遗忘。

Method: 提出FINCH方法，采用损失自适应学习率调度策略：针对高损失批次降低学习率，随模型收敛逐步提升学习率，同时保持微调目标不变。

Result: 平均减少93%的遗忘，保持任务性能。在Qwen3-4B上，TruthfulQA降解降低5倍，HaluEval降解逆转，保持置信度校准。

Conclusion: FINCH在保持任务性能的同时，显著减少了灾难性遗忘，为LLM微调提供了一种简单而有效的解决方案。

Abstract: Fine-tuning large language models on new data improves task performance but degrades capabilities learned during pretraining, a phenomenon known as catastrophic forgetting. Existing methods mitigate this by modifying the fine-tuning objective to suppress high-loss tokens or sequences, but these tokens are essential for learning new tasks, especially those with poor pretraining coverage. In such settings, hard tokens should still contribute to learning, so forgetting must be controlled without suppressing them. We identify a simple mechanism for doing so: per-step forgetting is bounded by the product of the learning rate and the square root of the current training loss. This suggests that high-loss batches are especially prone to inducing forgetting. Motivated by this observation, we introduce FINCH, a loss-adaptive learning-rate schedule that reduces the learning rate on high-loss batches and increases it as the model converges, while leaving the fine-tuning objective unchanged. Across knowledge acquisition, science, and low-resource language adaptation benchmarks, FINCH reduces forgetting by 93% on average while matching the task performance of standard fine-tuning. On Qwen3-4B knowledge acquisition, FINCH cuts TruthfulQA degradation by 5x and reverses HaluEval degradation, while better preserving confidence calibration. Overall, our results show that learning-rate schedules are an effective tool to shape model behavior during fine-tuning, beyond just target-task optimization.

</details>


### [249] [Training-Free Bayesian Filtering with Generative Emulators](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.20028)
*Thomas Savary,François Rozet,Gilles Louppe*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出利用扩散模型作为动态系统的模拟器，在无需额外训练的情况下实现最优粒子滤波器，解决传统粒子滤波器在高维场景下的可扩展性难题，并在混沌系统和大气动力学实验中验证了其有效性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 粒子滤波器虽然对非线性动态系统和观测模型具有理论精确性，但在高维场景下面临严重的可扩展性挑战。最优粒子滤波器变体虽已理论上提出，但因经典数值求解器的实现困难而长期未被有效探索。

Method: 利用扩散模型（diffusion-based emulators）作为动态系统的替代模拟器，无需额外训练即可实现最优粒子滤波器变体。

Result: 所提方法成功将粒子滤波扩展至高维设置，在非线性混沌系统（包括大气动力学）上验证了有效性。

Conclusion: 扩散模型可作为实现最优粒子滤波器的有效工具，为高维贝叶斯滤波问题提供了一种新的解决思路。

Abstract: Bayesian filtering is a well-known problem that aims to estimate plausible states of a dynamical system from observations. Among existing approaches to solve this problem, particle filters are theoretically exact for non-linear dynamics and observations, but suffer from poor scalability in high dimensions. In this work, we show that diffusion-based emulators of dynamical systems can be used to implement, without additional training, an optimal variant of particle filters that has remained largely unexplored due to implementation challenges with classical numerical solvers. Experiments on nonlinear chaotic systems, including atmospheric dynamics, demonstrate that the proposed approach successfully scales particle filtering to high-dimensional settings.

</details>


### [250] [Take It or Leave It: Intent-Controlled Partial Optimal Transport](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.20030)
*Salil Parth Tripathi,Bertrand Chapron,Fabrice Collard,Nicolas Courty,Ronan Fablet*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: IC-POT extends partial optimal transport by replacing global rejection with pointwise rejection costs, enabling structured mass rejection informed by side-specific information. The problem admits a dual interpretation and can be solved via balanced Kantorovich OT on augmented support. Demonstrated in PU learning, domain adaptation, and geophysical applications.


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: Existing partial optimal transport methods use global rejection mechanisms (budget, rebate, or uniform rule), but many applications require structured pointwise rejection decisions based on side-specific reliability, support geometry, or external information about which components should participate in matching.

Method: The method recasts the IC-POT problem as a balanced Kantorovich optimal transport problem on an augmented support, enabling efficient computation via standard OT solvers while providing a dual interpretation in terms of local acceptance thresholds.

Result: Theoretically, IC-POT admits a dual formulation with local acceptance thresholds. Empirically, incorporating pointwise rejection rules that encode statistical structure improves baseline pipelines in positive-unlabeled learning and open-partial domain adaptation, and successfully handles multi-modal satellite ocean measurements with physical and sensor priors.

Conclusion: IC-POT provides a principled framework that replaces rigid global rejection with flexible pointwise rejection costs, enabling applications such as PU learning, open-partial domain adaptation, and multi-modal satellite ocean measurements where physical/sensor priors naturally inform the rejection mechanism.

Abstract: While optimal transport (OT) enforces a rigid constraint by requiring two measures to be matched exactly, partial optimal transport relaxes this requirement by allowing mass to remain unmatched through a global budget, scalar rebate, or uniform rejection rule. However, many applications call for more structured, pointwise rejection mechanisms, where the decision to leave mass unmatched depends on side-specific reliability, support geometry, or external information about which components should participate in the comparison. We introduce \emph{intent-controlled partial optimal transport} (IC-POT), a targeted generalization of partial transport that replaces the global rejection paradigm with pointwise rejection costs over both measures. We show that the resulting optimization problem admits a dual interpretation in terms of local acceptance thresholds and can be solved by recasting it as a balanced Kantorovich OT problem on an augmented support. Beyond theoretical analysis, we demonstrate the practical relevance of IC-POT in settings where rejection is driven by side information. In positive-unlabeled learning and open-partial domain adaptation, incorporating pointwise rejection rules that encode statistical structure improves fixed baseline pipelines. Finally, we motivate the use of IC-POT with a geophysical practical case: multi-modal satellite ocean measurements, for which physical and sensors priors naturally inform the rejection mechanism and define the retrieved comparable signal information.

</details>


### [251] [CAMERA: Adapting to Semantic Camouflage in Unsupervised Text-Attributed Graph Fraud Detection](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.20032)
*Junjun Pan,Yixin Liu,Yu Zheng,Lianhua Chi,Alan Wee-Chung Liew,Shirui Pan*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: CAMERA是一个用于无监督文本属性图欺诈检测的框架，通过多专家协同和上下文感知门控实现对语义伪装欺诈者的有效检测。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 欺诈者通过故意模仿良性用户的文本回复来演化伪装策略（语义伪装），从根本上破坏了利用结构和属性线索识别欺诈者的常用假设，使无监督TAGFD难以发现欺诈者。

Method: 采用自我解耦的专家混合架构，每个专家专门建模不同类型的欺诈指示线索；引入上下文感知门控模型，联合考虑自我节点表示及其局部邻域上下文以自适应整合不同专家学习的线索；利用欺诈者的内在稀缺性支持无监督单类学习。

Result: 在4个挑战性数据集上的实验表明，CAMERA持续优于对比方法，证明了其对抗语义伪装欺诈者的有效性。

Conclusion: CAMERA框架通过专家级目标鼓励建模主流良性模式，实现了对伪装欺诈者的可靠无监督检测，在4个数据集上持续优于竞争对手。

Abstract: Text-attributed graph fraud detection (TAGFD) plays a critical role in preventing fraudulent activities on online social and e-commerce platforms. However, to evade detection, fraudsters continuously evolve their camouflaging strategies by deliberately mimicking textual responses of benign users, thereby concealing their malicious purposes. This phenomenon, referred to as semantic camouflage, fundamentally undermines commonly relied assumptions on how structural and attribute cues can be exploited to identify fraudsters, and makes it difficult to spot fraudsters with unsupervised TAGFD. To bridge the gaps, we propose a Case-Adaptive Multi-cue Expert fRAmework (CAMERA) for unsupervised TAGFD. CAMERA employs an ego-decoupled mixture-of-experts architecture, where each expert specializes in modeling a distinct type of fraud-indicative cue. A context-informed gating model is introduced to jointly consider the ego node representation and its local neighborhood context for adaptive integration of cues learned by different experts. Furthermore, CAMERA leverages the inherent rarity of fraudsters to support unsupervised one-class learning with expert-level objectives that encourage modeling dominant benign patterns, thereby enabling reliable unsupervised detection of camouflaged fraudsters. Experiments on 4 challenging datasets show that CAMERA consistently outperforms competitors, showing its effectiveness against semantically camouflaged fraudsters. Code available at https://github.com/CampanulaBells/CAMERA

</details>


### [252] [D$^3$-Subsidy: Online and Sequential Driver Subsidy Decision-Making for Large-Scale Ride-Hailing Market](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.20036)
*Taijie Chen,Rui Su,Siyuan Feng,Laoming Zhang,Hongyang Zhang,Haijiao Wang,Zhaofeng Ma,Jintao Ke*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: D³-Subsidy是滴滴出行开发的层级扩散式司机端补贴优化框架，通过前缀条件扩散模型生成城市级控制信号，结合拉格朗日对偶映射实现大规模实时决策，并支持多城市预训练与参数高效微调，兼顾响应性、约束合规性与低延迟部署需求。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 网约车平台需平衡司机供给与乘客需求的动态匹配，司机端补贴是关键调控杠杆。生产环境面临三重约束：①对随机冲击的快速响应；②严格的补贴率上限；③城市级规模的低延迟执行。现有方案无法同时满足这些要求。

Method: 提出D³-Subsidy框架，包含三个核心模块：(1) 前缀条件扩散模型——从固定历史数据采样未来轨迹，确保训练与推理一致；(2) 上下文条件逆模块——将生成的计划解码为低维城市级控制信号；(3) 拉格朗日对偶映射——将城市级规划与细粒度订单-司机匹配解耦，直接将补贴率约束嵌入激励而不需迭代优化。此外采用多城市预训练+参数高效微调策略实现跨城市迁移。

Result: 离线评估显示D³-Subsidy在提升Rides和GMV的同时改善了补贴上限合规性。真实A/B测试验证了显著业务提升，且违规指标维持在运营阈值内。

Conclusion: 该工作首次将扩散模型应用于大规模实时补贴控制，有效弥合了离线训练与在线部署的鸿沟，为网约车平台的城市级供给调控提供了可扩展、可解释且约束感知的解决方案。

Abstract: Ride-hailing platforms like DiDi Chuxing operate in highly dynamic environments where balancing driver supply and passenger demand is critical. Although driver-side subsidies serve as a primary lever to align these forces and improve key KPIs like completed rides (\texttt{Rides}) and gross merchandise value (\texttt{GMV}), optimizing them in production requires simultaneously meeting three constraints: (i) responsiveness to stochastic shocks, (ii) strict subsidy-rate caps, and (iii) low-latency execution at city scale. These requirements rule out expensive per-order optimization, calling for a forward-looking, constraint-aware city-level controller for online sequential decision making. To meet these requirements, we introduce D$^3$-Subsidy (Dynamic Driver-side Diffusion-based Subsidy), a hierarchical diffusion-based framework for deployable city-wide subsidy control. To bridge the train-inference gap, D$^3$-Subsidy employs a prefix-conditioned diffusion model that samples plausible future trajectories from immutable historical observations, ensuring the training protocol aligns with the fixed-history nature of online deployment. These generated plans are then decoded by a context-conditioned inverse module into low-dimensional city-level control signals. For scalable execution, we bridge the gap between city-level planning and fine-grained dispatch via a Lagrangian-dual-derived mapping, which embeds subsidy-rate caps directly into order-driver incentives without iterative optimization. Additionally, a multi-city pretraining strategy with parameter-efficient fine-tuning enables robust transfer across heterogeneous cities. Extensive offline evaluations demonstrate that D$^3$-Subsidy improves \texttt{Rides} and \texttt{GMV} while enhancing cap compliance, and a real-world A/B test confirms significant uplift while keeping budget-related violation metrics within operational thresholds.

</details>


### [253] [Active Context Selection Improves Simple Regret in Contextual Bandits](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.20040)
*Mohammad Shahverdikondori,Jalal Etesami,Negar Kiyavash*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文研究上下文多臂老虎机中上下文分布p对regret的影响：已知p时主动采样优于被动采样，最大改进达$\Theta(k^{1/4})$；未知p时EETC算法仍可渐近达到最优。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 在上下文多臂老虎机中，探索"上下文分布p"如何影响 regret 上界，特别是主动选择采样上下文能否带来可量化的改进。

Method: 已知p：推导被动和主动采样的下界与上界，揭示主动采样通过分配$q_j \propto p_j^{2/3}$可获得更优速率。未知p：提出EETC算法，在估计p和主动采样之间取得最优平衡。

Result: 被动采样速率$\sqrt{n/T} \cdot \|p\|_{1/2}$，主动采样速率$\sqrt{n/T} \cdot \|p\|_{2/3}$，改进幅度可达$\Theta(k^{1/4})$；EETC算法在未知p时仍可渐近达到主动采样最优速率。

Conclusion: 当p已知时，主动采样可达到最优速率$\sqrt{n/T} \cdot \|p\|_{2/3}$；当p未知时，EETC算法可渐近达到相同性能，仅差常数因子。

Abstract: We study the contextual multi-armed bandit problem with a finite context space (a.k.a. subpopulations), where the learner recommends a best action for each context and is evaluated by context-weighted simple regret. Our guarantees are worst-case over the reward distributions, while remaining instance-dependent with respect to the context distribution vector $p$. Akin to experimental design problems where the population of interest is fixed but the sampled subpopulation can be controlled, we allow the learner to actively choose which context to sample from. For a known $p$, we characterize tight regret rates: passive sampling where contexts are randomly revealed achieves regret of order $\sqrt{n/T \, \lVert p \rVert_{1/2}}$, whereas active sampling with allocation $q_j \propto p_j^{2/3}$ achieves the tight rate $\sqrt{n/T} \, \lVert p \rVert_{2/3}$. The resulting improvement can be as large as $Θ(k^{1/4})$, where $k$ is the number of contexts. We further extend the analysis to budgeted active sampling, characterize the corresponding tight rate, and identify when a limited active budget suffices to recover the fully active rate. When $p$ is unknown, we propose the Explore-Explore-Then-Commit (EETC) algorithm, which optimally balances estimating the context distribution and the time to switch to active allocation, such that for large horizons, it matches the known-$p$ active rate up to constants. Experiments on synthetic and real-world data support our theoretical findings.

</details>


### [254] [Smooth Partial Lotteries for Stable Randomized Selection](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.20069)
*Alexander Goldberg,Giulia Fanti,Nihar B. Shah*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 论文提出"平滑性"作为部分彩票机制的设计原则，通过Lipschitz条件约束评分到选中概率的映射关系。提出Clipped Linear Lottery机制，在上下阈值之间线性缩放选中概率，理论上证明其最坏情况下的遗憾值在(1-k/n)因子内达到下界，实验验证其比现有机制具有更好的平滑性-效用权衡。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: Current partial lottery designs are inherently unstable - small perturbations to a single candidate's score cause large shifts in selection probabilities. This undermines lotteries' core goal of reducing sensitivity to fine-grained score differences near the decision boundary, which is essential in high-stakes selection processes like peer review and admissions.

Method: Formalizes smoothness as Lipschitz continuity on score-to-selection-probability mapping. Proposes Clipped Linear Lottery with linear scaling between upper acceptance threshold and lower rejection threshold. Proves theoretical lower bound for any smooth selection rule and shows Clipped Linear Lottery achieves it up to a (1-k/n) factor. Compares with Individual Fairness and Differential Privacy based selection mechanisms.

Result: The Clipped Linear Lottery achieves near-optimal regret bounds and better smoothness-utility tradeoff than alternatives both theoretically and empirically. Real-world experiments on ICLR 2025, NeurIPS 2024, and Swiss NSF data demonstrate existing lotteries are highly unstable under single-score perturbations.

Conclusion: Clipped Linear Lottery achieves near-optimal worst-case regret (matching lower bound within factor (1-k/n)) and better smoothness-regret tradeoff than alternatives like Individual Fairness and Differential Privacy approaches. Empirical validation on three real peer review datasets confirms theoretical findings.

Abstract: Competitive selection processes, from scientific funding to admissions and hiring, use evaluations to score candidates, and eventually choose a subset of them based on those scores. Recently, many organizations have adopted partial lotteries, which randomize selection based on evaluation scores. However, existing lottery designs are inherently unstable, as a small change to a single candidate's score can cause large shifts in their selection probabilities. This instability undermines a key goal of lotteries: reducing the influence of fine-grained score distinctions near the decision boundary. We propose smoothness as a design principle for partial lotteries, formalizing it as a Lipschitz condition on the mapping from review scores over candidates to selection probabilities. We introduce the Clipped Linear Lottery, a simple mechanism in which selection probabilities scale linearly with estimated quality between an upper threshold, above which we always accept, and a lower threshold, below which we always reject. We prove that the Clipped Linear Lottery's worst-case regret matches a lower bound for any smooth selection rule up to a factor of $(1 - k/n)$, where $k/n$ is the acceptance rate. We compare smooth selection to other stability notions like Individual Fairness and Differential Privacy, showing that the Clipped Linear Lottery achieves a better smoothness-regret tradeoff than alternatives. Experiments on real peer review data from ICLR 2025, NeurIPS 2024, and the Swiss National Science Foundation demonstrate that existing lottery designs are highly unstable in practice even under perturbations to a single score. Our experiments also confirm the tightness of our theoretical analysis and show that our proposed Clipped Linear Lottery achieves a better smoothness-utility tradeoff than alternatives in practice.

</details>


### [255] [Towards Distillation Guarantees under Algorithmic Alignment for Combinatorial Optimization](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.20074)
*Thien Le,Melanie Weber*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: Summary generation failed


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: Motivation analysis unavailable

Method: Method extraction failed

Result: Result analysis unavailable

Conclusion: Conclusion extraction failed

Abstract: Distillation transfers knowledge from a large model trained on broad data to a smaller, more efficient model suitable for deployment. In structured prediction settings, prior knowledge about the task can guide the choice of a target architecture that is algorithmically aligned with the underlying problem. Building on recent learning-theoretic analyses of decision-tree (DT) distillation (Boix-Adsera, 2024), we study when distillation succeeds for combinatorial optimization tasks. We focus on the case where the target model is a graph neural network whose architecture is aligned with a dynamic programming (DP) algorithm for the task. Assuming that the source model is sufficiently rich, formalized through the linear representation hypothesis (LRH) (Elhage et al., 2022; Park et al., 2024), we show that the distillation problem can be solved efficiently in the complexity parameters of the DP transition function, represented as a DT. Our results provide a rigorous sufficient condition for successful distillation in the flavour of algorithmic alignment.

</details>


### [256] [INSHAPE: Instance-Level Shapelets for Interpretable Time-Series Classification](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.20088)
*Seongjun Lee,Seokhyun Lee,Changhee Lee*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: INSHAPE是一种可解释的时间序列分类框架，通过为每个时间序列发现特定的可变长度判别模式（非重叠片段）并建模其时间依赖性，解决了种群级shapelet方法的局限性，同时通过自底向上方法桥接局部和全局可解释性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有方法主要关注在整个数据集上优化的种群级shapelet，存在两个根本局限：(i)种群级模式往往与实例特定特征不一致，导致性能次优和潜在误导性解释；(ii)大多数方法将shapelet视为独立实体，忽视了多个模式之间的时间依赖和交互。

Method: INSHAPE将时间序列分解为非重叠片段，识别特定于每个时间序列的变长判别模式，并通过自底向上方法将实例级shapelet聚合为原型（种群级）shapelet，从而建模时间依赖性。

Result: 在128个UCR和30个UEA基准数据集上的广泛实验表明，INSHAPE始终优于最先进的基于shapelet的方法，同时提供更直观和可解释的洞察。

Conclusion: INSHAPE通过将实例级shapelet聚合为原型模式，有效桥接了局部和全局可解释性，在128个UCR和30个UEA数据集上实现了最先进的性能，同时提供了更直观和可解释的见解。

Abstract: Discovering shapelets -- i.e., discriminative temporal patterns within time series -- has been widely studied to address the inherent complexity of time-series classification (TSC) and to make model decision-making processes more transparent. However, existing methods primarily focus on population-level shapelets optimized across the entire dataset, which leads to two fundamental limitations: (i) population-level patterns often misalign with instance-specific features, resulting in suboptimal performance and potentially misleading interpretations, and (ii) most methods treat shapelets as independent entities, overlooking important temporal dependencies and interactions among multiple patterns. To address these limitations, we propose INSHAPE, an interpretable TSC framework that discovers variable-length, discriminative temporal patterns specific to each time series. INSHAPE identifies these patterns as non-overlapping segments and models their temporal dependencies, thereby providing clear instance-level interpretations while achieving strong predictive performance. Furthermore, INSHAPE bridges local and global interpretability through a bottom-up approach, aggregating instance-level shapelets into prototypical (population-level) shapelets. Extensive experiments on 128 UCR and 30 UEA benchmark datasets show that INSHAPE consistently outperforms state-of-the-art shapelet-based methods while providing more intuitive and interpretable insights.

</details>


### [257] [Draft Less, Retrieve More: Hybrid Tree Construction for Speculative Decoding](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.20104)
*Yuhao Shen,Tianyu Liu,Xinyi Hu,Quan Kong,Baolin Zhang,Jun Dai,Jun Zhang,Shuang Ge,Lei Chen,Yue Li,Mingcheng Wan,Cong Wang*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: Graft是一个训练-free、无损的推测解码补偿框架，通过剪枝与检索的协同机制，在保持高接受率的同时显著降低VRAM和计算开销，在多种场景下实现最高5.41倍加速。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 推测解码中构建大型draft树虽能最大化接受率，但导致严重的VRAM带宽和计算开销，成为端到端加速的瓶颈；动态深度剪枝虽能减少延迟，但会丢弃潜在有效候选，使接受率无法达到密集树的上界。

Method: 提出Graft补偿框架，将剪枝和检索作为相互增强的操作。采用顺序"先剪枝后嫁接"机制——剪枝释放计算预算，检索补偿剪枝导致的覆盖损失并恢复接受长度，将高预测性检索到的token附加到剪枝打开的位置，以接近零的开销填补拓扑空白。
