Abstract: Artificial Intelligence (AI) systems are increasingly dependent on complex, multi-layered software supply chains that introduce challenges for reproducibility, transparency, and security assurance. This study presents an Artificial Intelligence Bill of Materials (AIBOM) schema extending the CycloneDX standard to capture AI-specific provenance, model lineage, and disclosure metadata. The framework provides a formalised approach to verifiable software provenance through structured schema engineering, cryptographic validation, and agent-driven automation. An autonomous AI pipeline is developed to perform continuous environment inspection, vulnerability enrichment, and reproducibility auditing using machine-verifiable provenance chains. Empirical evaluation demonstrates 98.7% reproducibility fidelity, 96.2% vulnerability match precision, and a 63% reduction in manual oversight across containerised analytic workflows. These results confirm the feasibility of automated provenance assurance and reproducible AI lifecycle validation. The AIBOM framework advances the scientific foundations of software supply chain transparency and AI reproducibility engineering, offering a generalisable methodology for securing AI systems, strengthening provenance integrity, and supporting compliance with international information security standards.

</details>


### [278] [Towards LLM-Assisted Architecture Recovery for Real-World ROS~2 Systems: An Agent-Based Multi-Level Approach to Hierarchical Structural Architecture Reconstruction](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.20055)
*Dominique Briechle,Raj Chanchad,Tobias Geger,Ruidi He,Dhruv Jajadiya,Dhruv Kapadiya,Andreas Rausch,Meng Zhang*

Main category: cs.SE

TL;DR: 本文提出一种基于蓝图引导的LLM辅助ROS~2架构恢复管道的改进方案，通过精细化提示策略和多层中间表示实现跨抽象层次的层级结构恢复，并在协作机械臂产品拆解系统上验证了架构恢复的一致性、可扩展性和鲁棒性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: ROS~2系统中，结构（解）组合和集成语义通常隐式编码在源代码、启动文件等分布式工件中，导致层级架构恢复困难。现有方法主要关注节点级实体和通信连接，缺乏跨多抽象层次的结构恢复支持。

Method: 两大核心改进：(1) 精细化提示策略，提升架构合成的一致性和可控性；(2) 基于多层次中间架构表示的分阶段恢复策略，融合原子ROS节点列表和启动文件依赖，支持跨多抽象层次的结构约束重建。

Result: 在协作机械臂自动产品拆解系统上验证，相比前序工作，该案例具有更高的集成复杂度和更丰富的功能。结果表明架构恢复的结构一致性、可扩展性和鲁棒性均有所提升。

Conclusion: 所提方法有效改善了ROS~2系统的架构恢复能力，但在大规模ROS~2系统中，动态集成语义相关的挑战仍有待解决。

Abstract: Explicit software architecture models are essential artifacts for communicating, analyzing, and evolving complex software-intensive systems. In ROS~2-based robotic systems, however, structural (de-)composition and integration semantics are often only implicitly encoded across distributed artifacts such as source code and launch files, making recovery of hierarchical architecture particularly difficult. Existing approaches mainly focus on node-level entities and communication wiring, while providing limited support for recovering hierarchical structural (de-)composition across multiple abstraction levels.
  In this paper, we extend our previously proposed blueprint-guided LLM-assisted architecture recovery pipeline for ROS~2 systems through two major enhancements: (1) refined prompting to improve the consistency and controllability of architecture synthesis, and (2) a staged recovery strategy based on multi-level intermediate architectural representations that incorporate the atomic ROS node list and launch file dependencies, thereby enabling structurally constrained reconstruction across multiple abstraction levels.
  The approach is evaluated on a real-world automated product disassembly system based on cooperative robotic arms and heterogeneous ROS~2 artifacts. Compared to our previous work, the considered case study exhibits substantially higher integration complexity and richer functionality. The results demonstrate improved structural consistency, scalability, and robustness of architecture recovery, while also revealing remaining challenges related to dynamic integration semantics in large-scale ROS~2 systems.

</details>


### [279] [Does Code Cleanliness Affect Coding Agents? A Controlled Minimal-Pair Study](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.20049)
*Priyansh Trivedi,Olivier Schmitt*

Main category: cs.SE

TL;DR: 论文研究了代码整洁度（cleanliness）如何影响自主编程智能体（autonomous coding agents）的性能。通过构建最小配对（minimal pairs）评估框架，对33个任务进行660次实验后发现：代码整洁度不影响智能体的任务完成率，但使用更整洁代码的智能体消耗的token减少7-8%，文件重访次数减少34%。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 随着自主编程智能体的快速普及，现有评估主要关注任务完成率，忽略了底层代码质量对智能体导航和修改能力的影响。一个关键问题尚未得到解答：代码的结构和样式质量（"整洁度"）是否会影响智能体操控代码的效果？

Method: 构建最小配对（minimal pairs）评估协议：匹配的仓库在架构、依赖和外部行为上相同，但在静态分析规则违反和认知复杂度上不同。通过两个方向的智能体管道构建配对：降级干净仓库或清理混乱仓库。在6个配对上设计33个任务，通过应用程序公共表面的隐藏测试进行评估。使用Claude Code进行660次试验。

Result: 代码整洁度不改变智能体的通过率。然而，它显著改变了智能体的操作足迹：在更整洁代码上工作的智能体使用的token减少7-8%，文件重访次数减少34%。

Conclusion: 传统的可维护性原则在AI驱动开发时代仍然高度相关，影响着编码智能体的计算成本和导航效率。代码整洁度与模型选择、测试框架和提示工程一样，是影响智能体行为的重要因素。

Abstract: As autonomous coding agents see rapid adoption, their evaluation has primarily focused on task completion rates holding the target codebase fixed. This leaves a critical question unanswered: does the structural and stylistic quality, or ``cleanliness'' of the underlying code affect an agent's ability to navigate and modify it? To isolate the effect of code cleanliness from agent capability, we introduce an evaluation protocol built around minimal pairs: repositories that match on architecture, dependencies, and external behaviour, but differ on static-analysis rule violations and cognitive complexity. The pairs are constructed in both directions, by agent pipelines that either degrade a clean repository or clean a messy one. We author 33 tasks across six such pairs, evaluated through hidden tests at the application's public surface. Across 660 trials with Claude Code, code cleanliness does not change the agent's pass rate. However, it substantially alters the agent's operational footprint: agents working on cleaner code use 7 to 8% fewer tokens and reduce file revisitations by 34%. Our findings suggest that traditional maintainability principles remain highly relevant in the era of AI-driven development, shaping the computational cost and navigational efficiency of coding agents. Code cleanliness joins model choice, harness, and prompting as a factor that materially affects agent behaviours.

</details>


<div id='cs.IR'></div>

# cs.IR [[Back]](#toc)

### [280] [DOTRAG: Retrieval-Time Reasoning Along Paths](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.18760)
*Larnell Moore,Naihao Deng,Rada Mihalcea,Farnaz Jahanbakhsh*

Main category: cs.IR

TL;DR: DotRAG提出将检索过程重新定义为沿路径的推理过程，通过查询条件约束引导图探索，在无需显式逐步推理链的情况下迭代发现关系路径，在MetaQA和UltraDomain上达到SOTA性能。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 当前GraphRAG主要采用"先检索后推理"的范式，依赖启发式方法获取上下文。这类方法难以适应复杂多跳任务所需的查询特定逻辑，常出现无关上下文累积或正确关系路径遗漏的问题。

Method: 提出DotRAG框架，核心包括：(1) 生成查询条件约束指导图探索；(2) 剪枝无关区域；(3) 迭代发现关系路径，无需显式逐步推理链。引入"思维分解(DOT)"抽象，将检索分解为局部化搜索空间，并根据每个查询自适应调整搜索策略。

Result: 在MetaQA和UltraDomain数据集上达到SOTA性能，在多跳任务上表现一致提升。

Conclusion: 推理引导的检索方法有效，DotRAG通过将推理过程融入检索环节，克服了传统retrieve-then-reason范式的局限性。

Abstract: Graph Retrieval-Augmented Generation (GraphRAG) is dominated by a retrieve-then-reason paradigm, where context is retrieved using heuristics and then reasoned over. Such methods struggle to adapt to the query-specific logic required for complex multi-hop tasks, often accumulating irrelevant context or missing correct relational paths. We propose DotRAG, a training-free GraphRAG framework that reformulates retrieval as a reasoning process over paths. Our approach generates query-conditioned constraints that guide graph exploration, prune irrelevant regions, and iteratively discover relational paths without relying on explicit step-by-step reasoning chains. We introduce Division of Thought (DOT), an abstraction that decomposes retrieval into localized search spaces and adapts the search strategy to each query. DotRAG achieves SOTA performance on MetaQA and UltraDomain, with consistent gains on multi-hop tasks, demonstrating the effectiveness of reasoning-guided retrieval.

</details>


### [281] [ALDEN: Boosting Private Data Extraction from Retrieval-Augmented Generation Systems via Active Learning and Distribution Estimation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.18762)
*Xingyu Lyu,Jianfeng He,Ning Wang,Yidan Hu,Tao Li,Danjue Chen,Shixiong Li,Yimin Chen*

Main category: cs.IR

TL;DR: ALDEN是一种针对检索增强生成(RAG)系统的新型数据提取攻击方法，通过主动学习多样化恶意查询，并利用基于衰减的动态算法分析知识库主题分布，显著提升了私有数据提取的效率和效果。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: RAG系统虽被广泛用于增强大语言模型的可靠性，但现有研究表明其仍易受数据提取攻击。攻击者可将恶意命令嵌入用户查询以提取私有数据。然而，现有攻击方法存在数据提取率低、实际效果有限的问题，需要更有效的攻击方法来揭示系统脆弱性。

Method: ALDEN采用两大核心策略：1）利用主动学习技术多样化恶意查询，提升数据提取效率；2）观察到底层知识库的数据分布可为查询生成提供有价值指导，引入基于衰减的动态算法估算相应主题分布。通过结合这两种技术，实现对RAG系统更有效的数据提取攻击。

Result: 综合评估表明，ALDEN在数据提取效果上显著优于现有最先进的攻击方法，验证了该方法的有效性和高效率。

Conclusion: 该研究揭示了RAG系统安全漏洞的严重性，提出的ALDEN攻击框架不仅展示了更强的攻击能力，也为后续RAG系统安全防护研究提供了重要参考价值。

Abstract: Retrieval-Augmented Generation (RAG) is widely used to augment large language models with external knowledge retrieval to improve reliability and generalization. However, recent studies have shown that RAG systems remain vulnerable to data extraction attacks, where adversaries can extract private data by embedding malicious commands into user queries. Despite their feasibility, existing attacks typically suffer from low data extraction rates and limited practical effectiveness. Here, we propose ALDEN, a novel attack that effectively and efficiently extracts private data from RAGs. First, we employ active learning to diversify malicious queries and improve data extraction rates. Second, we observe that the data distribution of the underlying knowledge base provides valuable guidance for query generation and introduce a decay-based dynamic algorithm to estimate the corresponding topic distribution. By combining them together, we demonstrate that ALDEN substantially outperforms state-of-the-art methods through comprehensive evaluations.

</details>


### [282] [Query-Conditioned Graph Retrieval for Contextualized LLM Reasoning in Personalized Wearable Data](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.18763)
*Zhenyu Lu,Mahyar Abbasian,Amir M. Rahmani*

Main category: cs.IR

TL;DR: 本文提出WAG框架，通过构建个性化知识图谱实现可穿戴设备数据的查询自适应上下文检索，解决LLM分析长时程、多模态、高度个性化可穿戴数据时的上下文选择难题。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: LLM在分析可穿戴设备数据时面临关键挑战：上下文选择困境——提供不足限制推理能力，而包含全部数据则导致效率低下和生成质量下降。

Method: WAG将可穿戴指标和用户特定信号组织为个性化知识图谱，通过查询条件子图检索支持下游生成。检索过程整合：1）全局关系，通过层次贝叶斯建模捕获先验知识及人群/个体层面模式；2）局部关系，反映短期信号偏差；3）查询开放度信号控制检索广度。

Result: 在超过10,000个真实可穿戴数据集查询上，WAG在基于LLM和人类评估中均取得约70%胜率，显著优于基线和标准RAG方法。

Conclusion: 结构化、查询自适应的上下文检索方法对LLM驱动的可穿戴数据分析具有显著有效性。

Abstract: Large language models (LLMs) are increasingly applied to analyzing wearable sensing data, which are long-term, multimodal, and highly personalized. A key challenge is context selection: providing insufficient context limits reasoning, while including all available data leads to inefficiency and degraded generation quality. We propose Wearable As Graph (WAG), a graph-based context retrieval framework that enables query-adaptive reasoning over wearable data with LLMs. WAG organizes wearable metrics and user-specific signals into a personalized knowledge graph, and retrieves a query-conditioned subgraph to support downstream generation. The retrieval process integrates global relationships, capturing prior knowledge and population- and individual-level patterns via hierarchical Bayesian modeling, with local relationships that reflect short-term signal deviations. A query openness signal further controls retrieval breadth. We evaluate WAG on over 10,000 data-grounded queries from real-world wearable datasets. Across LLM-based and human evaluations, WAG achieves an approximately 70% win rate over baseline and standard RAG methods, demonstrating the effectiveness of structured, query-adaptive context retrieval for LLM-driven analysis of wearable data.

</details>


### [283] [STAR: Semantic-Tuned and Tail-Adaptive Retriever for Graph-Augmented Generation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.18765)
*Shuai Li,Chen Huang,Duanyu Feng,Wenqiang Lei,See-Kiong Ng*

Main category: cs.IR

TL;DR: STAR是一种针对GraphRAG的语义调优和长尾自适应检索器，通过token级交互学习和路径加权对比学习两项技术，分别解决语义捷径偏差和长尾路径偏差问题，在多跳问答场景下实现了检索和问答性能的稳定提升。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有GraphRAG方法在知识图谱上进行多跳问答时，存在两个关键问题：1）语义捷径偏差（Semantic Shortcut Bias）；2）长尾路径偏差（Long-Tail Path Bias）。这些问题导致语义建模不充分，GraphRAG效果受限。

Method: 提出STAR方法，包含两个核心学习范式：1）Token级交互学习：采用交叉注意力架构和硬路径挖掘机制联合建模查询与路径，缓解语义捷径偏差；2）路径加权对比学习：引入tail-adaptive路径加权的定制化对比学习目标，优化训练过程并缓解长尾路径偏差。

Result: 实验表明，STAR在所有基准数据集上均优于基线方法，检索性能平均提升1.8%，LLM问答性能平均提升2.2%。

Conclusion: STAR通过token级交互学习和路径加权对比学习两项技术，有效解决了GraphRAG中存在的语义捷径偏差和长尾路径偏差问题，在检索和问答任务上均取得了显著提升。

Abstract: To augment Large Language Models (LLMs) for multi-hop question answering, a mainstream solution within Graph Retrieval Augmented Generation (GraphRAG) leverages lightweight retrievers to efficiently extract information from a given Knowledge Graph (KG). However, existing methods often overlook the inherent challenge of sparse semantic information in graphs. Specifically, our experiments reveal that these methods produce biased retrieval Semantic Shortcut Bias and Long-Tail Path Bias, leading to inadequate semantic modeling and limited GraphRAG effectiveness. To address these issues, we propose STAR, a semantic-tuned and tail-adaptive retriever for GraphRAG. STAR integrates two key learning paradigms: token-level interaction learning and path-weighted contrastive learning. The former employs a cross-attention architecture and a hard path mining mechanism to jointly model the query and path, thereby mitigating the Semantic Shortcut Bias. The latter introduces a tailored contrastive learning objective that utilizes tail-adaptive path weighting, designed to optimize the training process and ease the Long-Tail Path Bias. Extensive experiments demonstrate that STAR consistently outperforms baselines, achieving average retrieval performance gains of 1.8\% and LLM QA performance improvements of 2.2\% across all benchmark datasets. Our code is available at https://anonymous.4open.science/r/STAR-C583.

</details>


### [284] [Retrieve Only Relevant Tables Whether Few or Many: Adaptive Table Retrieval Method](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.18766)
*Taehee Kim,Seungbin Yang,Jihwan Kim,Jaegul Choo*

Main category: cs.IR

TL;DR: 针对文本转SQL任务中的表格检索问题，提出自适应表格检索方法，通过动态阈值替代固定k值策略，并结合滑动窗口重排序高效处理大规模语料。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有表格检索方法使用固定k值策略选择表格，但最佳表格数量因查询而异且无法预知，导致检索结果非过少（缺失关键证据）即过多（引入无关表格）。

Method: 提出自适应阈值机制选择性检索表格，并集成滑动窗口重排序算法以高效处理大规模表格语料库。

Result: 在Spider、BIRD和Spider 2.0数据集上的广泛实验表明，该方法有效解决了top-k检索策略的局限性，提升了检索及下游任务的性能。

Conclusion: 该方法在检索和下游任务中均表现出有效改进，通过自适应机制解决了固定top-k策略的固有局限。

Abstract: Retrieving relevant tables from extensive databases for a given natural language query is essential for accurately answering questions in tasks such as text-to-SQL. Existing table retrieval approaches select a pre-determined set of k tables with the highest similarity to the query. However, the number of required tables varies across queries and cannot be known in advance. Enforcing a fixed number of retrieved tables regardless of the query may either retrieve an undersized set, failing to obtain all necessary evidence, or retrieve an oversized pool, including irrelevant tables. To address this issue, we propose an adaptive table retrieval method that adjusts the number of tables retrieved according to the requirements of each query. Specifically, we utilize an adaptive thresholding mechanism to selectively retrieve tables and integrate a sliding-window reranking algorithm to efficiently process a large table corpus. Extensive experiments on Spider, BIRD, and Spider 2.0 demonstrate that our method effectively addresses the limitations of the top-k retrieval strategy, improving performance in retrieval and downstream tasks. Our code and data are available at https://github.com/sbY99/Adaptive-Table-Retrieval.

</details>


### [285] [DualView: Adaptive Local-Global Fusion for Multi-Hop Document Reranking](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.18767)
*Litong Zhang,Jiaxin Li,Kuo Zhao*

Main category: cs.IR

TL;DR: 本文提出一种高效的"双视图级联重排序"框架，用于多跳问答的文档重排序。该框架基于E5-base-v2候选文档，包含局部评分器、全局评分器和自适应门控机制三个核心组件。在MuSiQue数据集上实现了99.4%的Top-4召回率和97.8%的全命中准确率，延迟仅4.0ms，显著优于参数量更大的跨编码器模型。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 多跳问答需要从多个文档中聚合信息，核心挑战在于高效识别最小相关文档集的同时保持高召回率。现有方法在效率和精度之间难以平衡，尤其是处理需要跨文档推理的复杂查询时。

Method: 提出双视图级联重排序框架，包含三个组件：(1) 局部评分器(Local Scorer)：使用堆叠交叉注意力机制进行细粒度查询-文档相关性评估；(2) 全局评分器(Global Scorer)：通过基于Transformer的上下文聚合建模文档间依赖关系；(3) 自适应门控(Adaptive Gate)：基于查询语义动态融合两个视图。在离线缓存嵌入的固定候选集重排序设置下运行。

Result: 在MuSiQue数据集上达到99.4%的Top-4召回率和97.8%的全命中准确率；延迟仅4.0ms(249 QPS)，比参数量600M的跨编码器(BGE-Large: 92.0%召回率、Jina-v3: 90.1%召回率)快5-6倍。消融实验证实局部和全局两个视图对多跳性能均有显著贡献。

Conclusion: 双视图架构能有效捕捉查询-文档细粒度相关性和文档间依赖关系，自适应门控机制实现了视图的动态融合。该框架在保持高精度的同时显著提升了推理效率，为多跳问答的高效文档检索提供了一种轻量级解决方案。

Abstract: Multi-hop question answering requires aggregating information from multiple documents, a critical capability for knowledge-intensive applications. A fundamental challenge lies in efficiently identifying the minimal relevant document set from retrieved candidates while maintaining high recall.
  We present an efficient dual-view cascaded reranking framework for multi-hop document reranking. Operating as a lightweight post-retrieval stage over E5-base-v2 candidates, our architecture comprises: (1) a Local Scorer employing stacked cross-attention for fine-grained query-document relevance; and (2) a Global Scorer modeling inter-document dependencies via Transformer-based context aggregation. These views are dynamically fused through an Adaptive Gate conditioned on query semantics.
  Under the fixed candidate set reranking setting with offline cached embeddings, our model achieves competitive results, particularly outstanding on MuSiQue with 99.4% Top-4 Recall and 97.8% Full Hit accuracy at 4.0 ms latency (249 QPS). It substantially outperforms 600M-parameter cross-encoders (BGE-Large: 92.0% Recall, Jina-v3: 90.1% Recall) while maintaining 5 to 6 times lower latency. Ablation studies validate that both Local and Global views contribute substantially to multi-hop performance.

</details>


### [286] [ClusterRAG: Cluster-Based Collaborative Filtering for Personalized Retrieval-Augmented Generation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.18769)
*Gibson Nkhata,Uttamasha Anjally Oyshi,Quan Mai,Susan Gauch*

Main category: cs.IR

TL;DR: ClusterRAG proposes a cluster-based collaborative filtering approach for personalized RAG, addressing high retrieval costs and user collaborative signals by organizing users into semantic clusters and performing dual-level retrieval, demonstrating consistent performance gains on the LaMP benchmark.


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: Existing RAG approaches face high retrieval costs and fail to leverage collaborative signals from similar users for personalized generation.

Method: ClusterRAG uses density-based clustering to organize users into semantically coherent groups, then performs two-level retrieval: cluster-level similarity followed by fine-grained document-level ranking, combining the target user's profile with profiles from top similar users.

Result: Jointly leveraging the target user's profile and profiles from top similar users consistently achieved the best performance across diverse tasks on the LaMP benchmark.

Conclusion: ClusterRAG was validated on the LaMP benchmark, showing effective integration with various dense retrievers, rankers, and both fine-tuned and zero-shot language models.

Abstract: Personalized Retrieval-Augmented Generation (RAG) relies on accurately selecting user-relevant documents. In practice, existing RAG approaches often suffer from high retrieval costs and overlook that collaborative signals from similar users can enhance personalized generation for the current user. We propose ClusterRAG, a Cluster-Based Collaborative Filtering for Personalized Retrieval-Augmented Generation. ClusterRAG represents users through their profile documents, organizes users into semantically coherent clusters using density-based clustering, and performs retrieval at both the cluster and document levels via cluster-level similarity and fine-grained ranking. Extensive experiments on the LaMP benchmark demonstrate that jointly leveraging the target user's profile and profiles from top similar users consistently yields the best performance across diverse tasks. Further analysis shows that ClusterRAG integrates seamlessly with different dense retrievers and rankers, and remains effective when paired with both fine-tuned and zero-shot language models.

</details>


### [287] [Agentic GraphRAG: Navigating Unstructured Financial Data with Collaborative AI](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.18770)
*Arthur Capozzi,Dirk Helbing*

Main category: cs.IR

TL;DR: 本文提出一种用于商业注册数据专家分析的协作式智能体GraphRAG框架，通过Neo4j知识图谱三阶段构建（确定性结构化摄取、LLM非结构化提取、身份解析去重）结合模块化智能体（零样本路由、有限反射、安全图访问、状态合成）实现复杂查询处理，在瑞士700万+商业公报数据集上验证了相对于向量RAG的显著性能提升。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 公共注册系统虽正式可访问，但因将结构化记录与非结构化法律文本混合而难以实际使用，传统关键词和纯向量检索在多跳、时序及实体中心调查场景中存在明显局限。

Method: 提出三阶段流水线构建Neo4j知识图谱：(i)确定性摄取结构化强节点；(ii)基于LLM从非结构化通知中提取弱节点；(iii)确定性身份解析与去重。在此基础上构建分析模块化智能体，集成零样本意图路由、有限反射循环、安全工具介导的图访问及状态感知响应合成，并引入人类在环仪表板以支持可解释性和审计追踪。

Result: 在瑞士官方商业公报（超过700万份七年多的多语言出版物）上验证了该框架。构建了涵盖实体解析精度、工具路由行为、答案质量及多轮对话性能的多层评估协议。实验表明，该智能体GraphRAG系统在自动化、人工策划及对话基准测试中均显著优于标准智能体向量RAG基线，在正确性、答案相关性、信息召回率、轮次成功率及上下文保持准确性方面均有大幅提升。

Conclusion: 该架构具有模块化、可复现且可迁移的特性，可应用于其他商业公报及公共部门注册系统。该研究为复杂商业注册数据的智能分析提供了新的技术路径。

Abstract: We present a collaborative agentic GraphRAG framework for expert analysis of commercial registry data. Public registries are often formally accessible, yet difficult to use in practice because they combine structured records with large volumes of unstructured legal text. This limits conventional keyword and vector-only retrieval, especially for multi-hop, temporal, and entity-centric investigations. Our approach builds a Neo4j knowledge graph through a three-phase pipeline: (i) deterministic ingestion of strong nodes from verified structured fields, (ii) LLM-based extraction of weak nodes from unstructured notices, and (iii) deterministic identity resolution and deduplication. On top of this graph, we introduce an analytical modular agent that integrates zero-shot intent routing, a bounded reflection loop, secure tool-mediated graph access, and state-aware response synthesis. A human-in-the-loop dashboard exposes evidence and execution traces to support transparency and auditability. We evaluate the framework on the Swiss Official Gazette of Commerce, a multilingual corpus of more than seven million publications over seven years. We further contribute a multi-tier evaluation protocol covering entity-resolution precision, tool-routing behavior, answer quality, and multi-turn conversational performance. Across automated, human-curated, and conversational benchmarks, the proposed agentic GraphRAG system consistently outperforms a standard agentic vector-RAG baseline, with strong gains in correctness, answer relevance, information recall, turn success rate, and context carryover accuracy. The architecture is modular, reproducible, and transferable to other commercial gazettes and public-sector registry systems.

</details>


### [288] [LWGR: Lagrangian-Constrained Personalized World Knowledge for Generative Recommendation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.18771)
*Lingyu Mu,Hao Deng,Haibo Xing,Kaican Lin,Zhitong Zhu,Yu Zhang,Xiaoyi Zeng,Zhengxiao Liu,Zheng Lin,Jinxin Hu*

Main category: cs.IR

TL;DR: Summary generation failed


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: Motivation analysis unavailable

Method: Method extraction failed

Result: Result analysis unavailable

Conclusion: Conclusion extraction failed

Abstract: Recent progress in large language model (LLM) based generative recommendation (GR) shows that leveraging LLM world knowledge can substantially improve performance. However, existing methods rely on fixed, manually designed instructions to generate semantic knowledge and directly incorporate it into GR, which has two limitations. First, fixed instructions cannot capture the multidimensional heterogeneity of user interests. Second, uncontrollable knowledge fusion may conflict with behavioral signals and harm recommendations. To address these limitations, we propose LWGR, a framework that leverages Lagrangian constraints to transfer users' personalized world knowledge from LLMs into generative recommendation. LWGR enhances GR along two axes: knowledge extraction and fusion. It builds personalized soft instructions to extract behavior-relevant LLM world knowledge, and formulates knowledge fusion as an optimization problem with explicitly bounded performance degradation, which is solved by a Lagrangian primal-dual method to selectively incorporate beneficial knowledge. We further design two training strategies for different LLM scales and a deployment scheme that combines nearline precomputation with lightweight online serving. Experiments on multiple public datasets and one industrial dataset show that LWGR outperforms eight state-of-the-art baselines by up to 11.23% and brings a 1.35% revenue lift on a large-scale advertising platform, demonstrating its effectiveness and practicality.

</details>


### [289] [Improving Retrieval-Augmented Generation without Taxonomy-based Error Categorization](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.18772)
*Gongbo Zhang,Yifan Peng,Chunhua Weng*

Main category: cs.IR

TL;DR: 本文提出RePAIR，一种无需显式错误分类即可提升RAG性能的响应-动作学习范式。该方法直接建立缺陷输出与纠正行动之间的映射，在多个基准上验证了其有效性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有agentic RAG系统隐式假设可靠的评论家反馈，主要关注规划策略，但忽视了错误纠正过程本身的鲁棒性问题，包括错误类别对齐错误及无效/错误的纠正。

Method: RePAIR采用响应-动作学习范式，将缺陷RAG输出直接映射到错误缓解的行动计划，无需依赖细粒度错误分类体系或显式评论家监督。

Result: RePAIR在多个基准测试中一致性地提升了agentic RAG性能。

Conclusion: RePAIR通过端到端的响应-动作学习范式，无需显式错误分类即可提升RAG系统的鲁棒性，为agentic RAG的错误纠正提供了新思路。

Abstract: Retrieval-Augmented Generation (RAG) improves the factual accuracy of large language model (LLM) outputs by grounding generation in external knowledge. Recent agentic RAG systems extend this paradigm with critical agents to evaluate model responses and iteratively refine outputs. However, most prior work implicitly assumes reliable critic feedback and focuses on planning strategies, while paying limited attention to the robustness of the error-correction process itself, which can be impacted by misaligned error categories and ineffective or incorrect corrections. Here, we hypothesize that RAG performance can be improved without explicit error categorization. We propose RePAIR, a response-action learning paradigm that directly maps flawed RAG outputs to error-mitigating action plans without relying on fine-grained error taxonomies and explicit critic supervision. Across multiple benchmarks, RePAIR consistently improves agentic RAG performance.

</details>


### [290] [M3DocDep: Multi-modal, Multi-page, Multi-document Dependency Chunking with Large Vision-Language Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.18774)
*Joongmin Shin,Jeongbae Park,Jaehyung Seo,Heuiseok Lim*

Main category: cs.IR

TL;DR: M3DocDep是一个基于大视觉语言模型(LVLM)的文档依赖恢复与分块流水线，通过在分块前先构建完整的文档依赖树，解决长篇多页工业文档中跨页关系遗漏和分块碎片化的问题。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有文本中心分块器和生成式层次解析器常遗漏跨页父子关系、图表-标题绑定及边界线索，导致长篇多页工业文档的分块碎片化或冗余，降低检索和答案质量。

Method: M3DocDep采用LVLM架构：1) SharedDet作为统一的文档解析+OCR预处理层；2) 边界感知的SoftROI池化提取多模态块嵌入；3) 双仿射头评分候选父子边；4) MST约束解码全局有效依赖树；5) 构建带章节路径和页码标注的树引导分块。

Result: 在DHP基准上STEDS提升28.5%~39.6%；语料库级RAG基准上检索nDCG提升1.1%~15.3%，QA ANLS提升4.5%~15.3%。

Conclusion: M3DocDep通过在分块前恢复文档依赖关系，为长篇多页多模态文档生成更连贯的检索单元，显著提升检索和问答质量。

Abstract: In long, multi-page industrial documents, retrieval-augmented generation (RAG) depends heavily on whether chunk boundaries follow the document's true structure. Existing text-centric chunkers and generative hierarchy parsers often miss cross-page parent-child relations, figure/table-caption bindings, and boundary cues, which leads to fragmented or redundant chunks and degrades both retrieval and answer quality. We propose M3DocDep, an LVLM-based pipeline that first recovers block-level dependencies and then constructs chunks along the recovered document tree. The pipeline uses SharedDet as a common DP+OCR preprocessing layer, extracts multimodal block embeddings with boundary-aware SoftROI pooling, scores candidate parent-child edges with a biaffine head, decodes a globally valid dependency tree with MST constraints, and builds tree-guided chunks annotated with section paths and page ranges. Under a shared-block evaluation protocol, M3DocDep improves STEDS by +28.5 to +39.6 percent on DHP benchmarks, retrieval nDCG by +1.1 to +15.3 percent, and QA ANLS by +4.5 to +15.3 percent on corpus-level RAG benchmarks. These results show that recovering document dependencies before chunking yields more coherent retrieval units for long, multi-page multimodal documents.

</details>


### [291] [Query-Aware Flow Diffusion for Graph-Based RAG with Retrieval Guarantees](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.18775)
*Zhuoping Zhou,Davoud Ataee Tarzanagh,Sima Didari,Wenjun Hu,Baruch Gutow,Oxana Verkholyak,Masoud Faraki,Heng Hao,Hankyu Moon,Seungjai Min*

Main category: cs.IR

TL;DR: Summary generation failed


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: Motivation analysis unavailable

Method: Method extraction failed

Result: Result analysis unavailable

Conclusion: Conclusion extraction failed

Abstract: Graph-based Retrieval-Augmented Generation (RAG) systems leverage interconnected knowledge structures to capture complex relationships that flat retrieval struggles with, enabling multi-hop reasoning. Yet most existing graph-based methods suffer from (i) heuristic designs lacking theoretical guarantees for subgraph quality or relevance and/or (ii) the use of static exploration strategies that ignore the query's holistic meaning, retrieving neighborhoods or communities regardless of intent. We propose Query-Aware Flow Diffusion RAG (QAFD-RAG), a training-free framework that dynamically adapts graph traversal to each query's holistic semantics. The central innovation is query-aware traversal: during graph exploration, edges are dynamically weighted by how well their endpoints align with the query's embedding, guiding flow along semantically relevant paths while avoiding structurally connected but irrelevant regions. These query-specific reasoning subgraphs enable the first statistical guarantees for query-aware graph retrieval, showing that QAFD-RAG recovers relevant subgraphs with high probability under mild signal-to-noise conditions. The algorithm converges exponentially fast, with complexity scaling with the retrieved subgraph size rather than the full graph. Experiments on question answering and text-to-SQL tasks demonstrate consistent improvements over state-of-the-art graph-based RAG methods.

</details>


### [292] [A Reproducibility Analysis of PO4ISR: Diagnosing and Mitigating Semantic Drift in LLM-Based Session Recommendation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.18780)
*Aditya Tiwari,Konduri Naga Lakshmi Rekha,Rajesh Kumar Mundotiya*

Main category: cs.IR

TL;DR: 本文对PO4ISR进行可复现性研究，发现其在复杂语义领域存在上下文漂移导致的性能退化问题。提出增强版PO4ISR++，通过反思性提示和一致性排序检测实现动态跨域适应，在Games和Bundle数据集上分别获得54%和96%的性能提升。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有推理型LLM推荐模型(如PO4ISR)在跨语义领域的可复现性未被充分验证；标准推理提示在长会话场景中存在严重上下文漂移(contextual drift)，导致在Games、Bundle等复杂语义数据集上性能下降。

Method: 集成反思性提示(reflexive prompting)和一致性排序检测(consistent rank detection)，动态适应跨域线索，替代原有静态提示策略。

Result: 在ML-1M、Games、Bundle三个数据集上验证。PO4ISR++在Games上提升54%，Bundle上提升96%，恢复了原始模型在新域上的性能。

Conclusion: PO4ISR++通过自适应动态提示策略有效解决了跨域泛化问题，开源 artifacts 将促进 LLM 推荐研究的可复现性发展。

Abstract: Reasoning-based Large Language Models (LLMs) like PO4ISR have set new benchmarks in session-based recommendation. However, the reproducibility of their reasoning capabilities across diverse semantic domains remains unexplored. In this work, we conduct a rigorous reproducibility study of PO4ISR to assess its generalization limits. Our analysis reveals a critical failure mode: standard reasoning prompts suffer from severe contextual drift in long sessions, leading to performance degradation on semantically complex datasets like Games and Bundle. To quantify and resolve this stability gap, we introduce PO4ISR++, a robustness-enhanced implementation that integrates reflexive prompting and consistent rank detection. Unlike the original static prompting strategy, our approach dynamically adapts to cross-domain cues. We benchmark both the original implementation and our robust variant on ML-1M, Games, and Bundle. Our results confirm that while the original model struggles in new domains, our reproducible extension restores performance, yielding a stabilized gain of up to 54% on Games and 96% on Bundle. We release open-source artifacts, including the reproduced baseline and our enhanced framework, to facilitate reliable future research in LLM-based recommendation.

</details>


### [293] [Trust or Abstain? A Self-Aware RAG Approach](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.18792)
*Xi Zhu,Ziqi Wang,Kai Mei,Wujiang Xu,Minghao Guo,Bangji Yang,Jiajun Fan,Dimitris N. Metaxas*

Main category: cs.IR

TL;DR: Summary generation failed


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: Motivation analysis unavailable

Method: Method extraction failed

Result: Result analysis unavailable

Conclusion: Conclusion extraction failed

Abstract: Retrieval-augmented generation (RAG) improves large language models (LLMs) by incorporating external evidence, but it also introduces knowledge conflicts when retrieved contextual knowledge (CK) and parametric knowledge (PK) disagree or are both unreliable. Existing approaches mainly coordinate which source to use, without explicitly asking whether each answer path is correct. We argue that faithful RAG requires LLM self-awareness, namely the ability to recognize the limits of its own knowledge and reasoning. To ground this problem, we construct a model-specific, ground-truth-aligned knowledge-conflict benchmark by evaluating LLM backbones on PK-only and CK-conditioned answer paths over approximately 69K query-context instances per backbone, drawn from five conflict-QA datasets. We then introduce SABER, a Self-Aware Belief Estimator for RAG that requires no LLM fine-tuning. SABER combines a self-prior with PK-side and CK-side conditional reasoning representations from multi-trace inference, then estimates reliability beliefs with two lightweight predictors to drive a 4-cell decision over trust PK, trust CK, trust either, or abstain. Across four LLM backbones, SABER improves end-to-end accuracy and conflict-specific faithfulness over ten inference-time and fine-tuning baselines, with the largest gains on conflict-heavy datasets. Under abstention, SABER's risk-coverage curve Pareto-dominates every prompt-based abstainer, providing a tunable balance between coverage and answer risk. Our code is available at https://github.com/xizhu1022/SABER.

</details>


### [294] [Towards FairRAG: Preventing Representational Harm in Retrieval-Augmented Generation by Enforcing Fair Exposure at Retrieval Time](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.18806)
*Riddhi Tikoo*

Main category: cs.IR

TL;DR: 该论文研究RAG系统中检索排序对偏差传播的影响，提出Representative Stochastic排序器，通过考虑检索阶段已存在的表示偏差来恢复公平性，验证了检索是缓解下游偏差的关键环节。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: LLM集成到高风险领域时，模型幻觉是关键问题；RAG虽能缓解幻觉，但其多组件流水线会引入偏差；现有排序策略未考虑检索阶段已存在的表示偏差。

Method: 使用TREC 2022公平排序数据集（包含标记为受保护或非受保护的维基百科文章），针对四个场景问答提示让LLM识别相关引文文章；对四种排序方法（Standard、Stochastic、Forced-Exposure、Representative Stochastic）的检索排名和生成输出进行暴露偏差和效用评估。

Result: Representative Stochastic排序器实现了统计显著的近乎平等的曝光率；所有排序方法的生成人口统计 parity与曝光 parity高度一致；证明RAG系统中的表示偏差由检索阶段引入并传播到生成阶段。

Conclusion: Representative Stochastic排序器可恢复RAG系统中的公平性；RAG系统的表示偏差由检索阶段引入并传播到生成阶段；检索排序是缓解下游偏差的关键干预点。

Abstract: As Large Language Model (LLM) integration has accelerated in high-stakes domains, model hallucination is a critical issue. Retrieval-augmented generation (RAG) is a technique for addressing hallucination; however, RAG's multi-component pipeline introduces vulnerabilities where biases can be introduced. This study considers two previously developed utility-focused ranking strategies (Standard and Stochastic) alongside two proposed exposure-aware approaches (Forced-Exposure and Representative Stochastic). Using the TREC 2022 Fair Ranking Dataset, which contains Wikipedia articles annotated as protected or non-protected, the LLM was asked to identify relevant articles with citations for four scenario-based Q&A prompts. The retrieval rankings and the generated outputs were evaluated for exposure bias and utility across all ranking methods. Overall, the Representative Stochastic ranker resulted in a statistically significant near-parity average exposure, acknowledging that relevance scores initially produced during retrieval are already shaped by representational bias, whereas the other rankers assume those scores are unbiased. Across all the methods of document ranking, generation demographic parity closely mirrored the exposure parity, reinforcing that representational bias in RAG systems is driven by retrieval and propagates to generation. These findings highlight that retrieval ranking is a critical point for mitigating downstream bias and propose a Representative Stochastic ranker that reintroduces fairness in RAG systems.

</details>


### [295] [The 99% Success Paradox: When Near-Perfect Retrieval Equals Random Selection](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.18857)
*Vyzantinos Repantis,Harshvardhan Singh,Tony Joseph,Cien Zhang,Akash Vishwakarma,Svetlana Karslioglu,Michael Wyatt Thot,Ameya Gawde*

Main category: cs.IR

TL;DR: 本文揭示了传统检索指标（如成功率）在LLM时代可能产生误导——表面高成功率可能掩盖随机级选择性。通过引入机会校正指标BoR，证明了在高召回深度下检索增益趋于消失，并建议将BoR与传统指标联合报告以指导检索深度选择。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 传统信息检索系统假设人类能自行过滤不相关信息，因此优化目标聚焦于找到更多相关文档，而非保持结果的简洁性。然而大语言模型（LLM）不具备这种过滤能力，导致传统指标（如成功率>99%）可能掩盖实际的选择性不足。

Method: 提出了Bits-over-Random (BoR)指标，公式为BoR = log₂(P_obs/P_rand)，其中P_rand是所选成功规则的超几何基线（如覆盖率规则：top-K中至少1个相关文档）。BoR通过机会校正来揭示隐藏在表面高成功率下的随机级性能。

Result: 在20 Newsgroups数据集上，BM25和SPLADE均报告K=100时覆盖率>99%，但BoR≈0，说明此时性能等同于随机基线。仅当期望覆盖率比(K·R̄_q/N)超过3-5时，基线效应主导，选择性崩溃。RAG下游任务的LLM准确率在K=100时显著下降，而BEIR/SciFact和MS MARCO上BoR保持正值（41个系统在0.2 bits内聚类，尽管召回率差距达13点）。工具选择场景同样显示小规模目录下选择性消失。

Conclusion: 建议将BoR与传统的召回率、精确率等指标一起报告；在选择检索深度K时需要重新考虑——当额外检索带来的选择性收益微乎其微却徒增计算成本时，应慎选K值。

Abstract: For most of the history of information retrieval (IR), search results were designed for human consumers who could scan, filter, and discard irrelevant information on their own. This shaped retrieval systems to optimize for finding and ranking more relevant documents, but not keeping results clean and minimal, as the human was the final filter. However, LLMs have changed that by lacking this filtering ability.
  To address this, we introduce Bits-over-Random (BoR), a chance-corrected measure of retrieval selectivity that reveals when high success rates mask random-level performance. We measure selectivity as $BoR = \log_{2}\left(\frac{\mathrm{P}_{obs}}{\mathrm{P}_{rand}}\right)$, where $\mathrm{P}_{rand}$ is the hypergeometric baseline for the chosen success rule (here, coverage: $ \geq1 $ relevant in top-$K$).
  On the 20 Newsgroups dataset, BM25 and SPLADE both report $>99$% success at $K=100$ (coverage), yet $BoR \approx 0$, indicating random-level selectivity at that depth. When the expected coverage ratio $\left(\frac{K \cdot \bar{R}_{q}}{N}\right)$ exceeds 3-5, the baseline dominates and selectivity collapses. Downstream retrieval-augmented generation (RAG) evaluation confirms this pattern: LLM accuracy can degrade substantially at $K=100$, consistent with the near-zero BoR ceiling. In contrast, BoR remains positive on BEIR/SciFact and on MS MARCO (where 41 systems cluster within 0.2 bits of the theoretical ceiling despite a 13-point recall gap), confirming baseline predictions across sparse and large-scale settings. We further show that the collapse boundary applies to LLM agent tool selection, where small catalog sizes cause selectivity to vanish even with perfect selectors. These findings suggest reporting BoR alongside traditional metrics and reconsidering depth choices when additional retrieval provides negligible selectivity gains while inflating computational costs.

</details>


### [296] [Understanding Wacky Weights: A Dissection of SPLADE's Learned Term Importance](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.19628)
*Gregory Polyakov,Harrisen Scells,Carsten Eickhoff*

Main category: cs.IR

TL;DR: 系统研究了SPLADE模型中怪异权重的来源、流行程度及其对检索有效性的贡献，发现词表规模和稀疏正则化是关键影响因素。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: SPLADE模型中的怪异权重现象限制了模型可解释性，但缺乏系统性研究。

Method: 复现SPLADE-v2并通过改变损失函数、数据集和骨干Transformer进行消融实验；提出怪异性形式化定义和新度量指标。

Result: 较大词表增加怪异token流行率；严格稀疏正则化降低流行率；怪异权重主要用于域内效能而非泛化。

Conclusion: 怪异权重是SPLADE模型实现域内检索效能的代价之一，可通过调整词表规模和稀疏正则化来控制其流行程度。

Abstract: Learned sparse retrieval models such as SPLADE combine the effectiveness of neural architectures with the efficiency of inverted indices. As these models assign weights to terms from a fixed vocabulary, interpretability is often touted as a major benefit of these models. However, the emergence of wacky weights, i.e., expansion terms that appear semantically unrelated to the input, limits interpretability. While prior research has anecdotally observed this phenomenon, there is a lack of systematic understanding regarding their origins, prevalence, and contribution to retrieval effectiveness. In this paper, we reproduce SPLADE-v2 to systematically investigate wacky weights across the SPLADE family of models. We present a comprehensive dissection of wacky weights, providing a formal definition of wackiness based on the lexical utility of expansion terms. Furthermore, we introduce a novel measure to compare the prevalence of these tokens across models with varying vocabularies and sparsity levels. Beyond reproducing the original SPLADE-v2, we train it with various loss functions, datasets, and backbone transformers to isolate the factors contributing to wackiness. Our results show that larger vocabularies are associated with a higher prevalence of wacky tokens, while stricter sparsity regularizers are associated with lower prevalence. Finally, we find that wacky weights are used primarily for in-domain effectiveness rather than out-of-domain generalization.

</details>


### [297] [Mask-to-Correct$^+$: Leveraging Retriever Diversity for Masking-guided Faithful Fact Correction](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.18776)
*Payel Santra,Lavisha Sharma,Madhusudan Ghosh,Partha Basuchowdhuri*

Main category: cs.IR

TL;DR: 本文提出M2C框架（及其增强版M2C+），一种无需训练、基于检索增强生成（RAG）的事实修正方法，通过多样性感知掩码识别错误span，并利用集成多ranker的策略提升稳健性，在不使用gold evidence的情况下SARI分数提升最高达14%。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有事实修正方法依赖人工标注的claim-evidence对，存在数据稀缺、领域偏置等问题；同时忽略了对修正语义忠实性的评估；此外RAG系统的性能高度依赖检索器选择。

Method: 提出M2C框架：(1)采用多样性感知掩码(diversity-aware masking)识别claims中的错误span；(2)利用检索到的evidence评估修正的忠实性；(3)进一步提出M2C+，通过ensemble策略整合多个ranker的修正结果，降低检索偏差。

Result: 在benchmark数据集上，提出的框架一致性地超越所有baseline，无需使用gold evidence即可实现最高14%的SARI分数提升。

Conclusion: 训练无关、无需人工标注的RAG框架能有效进行事实修正，ensemble策略增强了系统的鲁棒性和跨领域泛化能力。

Abstract: The rapid spread of misinformation on social media highlights the need for robust, automated fact correction frameworks. However, existing works rely on supervised learning from manually annotated claim-evidence pairs, which are scarce and prone to biases, limiting their generalization across domains. Moreover, these methods overlook semantic faithfulness in their correction process. To address these challenges, we propose Mask-to-Correct (M$_2$C), a training-free, inference-only Retrieval Augmented Generation (RAG) based framework that leverages diversity-aware masking to identify erroneous spans of claims and evaluate the faithfulness of corrections using retrieved evidence. However, the effectiveness of RAG heavily depends on the choice of retriever, which may vary across queries. To mitigate this, we further introduce M$_2$C$^+$, an ensemble-based framework that combines corrections across multiple rankers to reduce retrieval bias and improve robustness. Extensive experiments on the benchmark datasets demonstrate that our proposed frameworks consistently outperform all baselines, achieving up to 14% improvement in SARI scores, without using gold evidence.

</details>


### [298] [KadiAssistant: A conversational AI Agent for information retrieval in Kadi4Mat](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.18850)
*Adrian Cierpka,Mohammad Shafiqul Islam,Johannes Steinhülb,Eric Dietriche Sesso Domtchoueng,Michael Selzer,Arnd Koeppe*

Main category: cs.IR

TL;DR: KadiAssistant是一个隐私优先的AI助手，集成了自托管大语言模型和隐私保护语义搜索功能，使研究者能够高效访问、聚合和综合来自异构、隐私敏感型研究数据平台的信息，同时尊重细粒度访问权限。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 跨学科领域（如材料科学）的研究数据分布在不同组织、格式、词汇和标准中，导致知识连接和访问困难；研究数据隐私敏感度各异（私有"热数据"与公开"冷数据"），且高效存储和共享需要领域知识和技术专长；现有数据平台缺乏兼顾隐私保护与高效信息检索的解决方案。

Method: KadiAssistant结合自托管大语言模型(LLM)与隐私保护语义搜索（受检索增强生成RAG启发），可访问Kadi平台上的文件和记录元数据，具备跨术语和标准的筛选、聚合和信息结构化能力。

Result: 该助手能够尊重细粒度访问权限，将信息筛选聚合为高度可用的答案，有效弥合术语和标准差异，降低研究者访问门槛。

Conclusion: KadiAssistant强化了FAIR数据原则中"可发现性"(Findable)支柱，为隐私敏感型研究数据生态系统提供了高效、安全的AI辅助信息检索方案。

Abstract: We introduce KadiAssistant, a privacy-by-design AI assistant integrated into the Kadi research data ecosystem, enabling researchers to efficiently access, aggregate, and synthesize information from heterogeneous, privacy-sensitive research data. Interdisciplinary fields such as materials science bring together disciplines with their own terminology and standards. While this convergence fuels innovation, it also makes it increasingly difficult to connect and access knowledge, as data are distributed across disciplines, organizations, and individuals. For example, battery research combines electrochemical measurements, materials characterization data, physics-based simulations, and manufacturing parameters, each using different formats, vocabularies, and standards. Efficiently storing and sharing such heterogeneous data via research data platforms, such as Kadi4Mat, demands domain knowledge, technical expertise, and familiarity with metadata schemas and interfaces. Research data also vary in sensitivity: newly generated 'warm' data are often private, whereas published 'cold' data are usually openly accessible. The Kadi ecosystem offers fine-grained access control needed for sensitive data. A solution for efficient information retrieval in Kadi must therefore respect the fine-grained access permissions. To address these intertwined challenges of information retrieval, strong data privacy, and complex access control, KadiAssistant combines a self-hosted large language model (LLM) with a privacy-preserving semantic search, inspired by retrieval-augmented generation, that can access files and record metadata on Kadi. This allows the assistant to screen, aggregate, and structure information into a highly informative answer. KadiAssistant therefore bridges terminology and standards, lowers access barriers for researchers, and strengthens the Findable pillar of FAIR data principles.

</details>


### [299] [SynGR: Unleashing the Potential of Cross-Modal Synergy for Generative Recommendation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.18920)
*Wei Chen,Xingyu Guo,Shuang Li,Fuwei Zhang,Meng Yuan,Jing Fan,Zhao Zhang,Deqing Wang,Fuzhen Zhuang*

Main category: cs.IR

TL;DR: SynGR是一个协同生成式推荐框架，通过显式建模跨模态依赖关系并约束对主导模态的过度依赖，捕获跨模态协同信息中蕴含的emergent item semantics（涌现性物品语义），从而超越表面特征匹配，实现更优的推荐性能。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有生成式推荐方法虽引入多模态信号，但主要依赖alignment-centric fusion（对齐中心融合），未能充分探索跨模态协同信息。这类协同信息编码了emergent item properties（涌现性物品属性）——即无法从单一模态推断的跨模态组合特征，对用户偏好具有重要指导作用。

Method: SynGR的核心设计：(1) 显式鼓励在生成过程中利用跨模态依赖关系；(2) 约束模型对主导模态的过度依赖，防止单一模态主导生成过程；(3) 使模型能够捕获超越共享信号和模态特定信号的emergent item semantics。

Result: 在三个基准数据集上的广泛实验表明，SynGR实现了优越的推荐性能。

Conclusion: 该研究证明了跨模态协同信息在生成式推荐中的重要价值，为多模态生成推荐提供了新的范式：通过约束主导模态影响，显式建模模态间协同效应，可有效捕获物品的涌现性语义特征。

Abstract: Generative Recommendation (GR) has emerged as a promising paradigm by formulating item recommendation as a sequence-to-sequence generation task over item identifiers. Recent studies have incorporated multimodal signals to provide richer token-level evidence for generation. However, existing approaches largely rely on alignment-centric fusion and underexplore synergistic information across modalities. In practice, synergistic information plays a critical role in capturing emergent item properties that cannot be inferred from any single modality alone. Such properties encode intrinsic item semantics and guide user preferences, enabling models to move beyond surface-level feature matching. To address this limitation, we propose \textbf{SynGR}, a synergistic generative recommendation framework that explicitly encourages the exploitation of cross-modal dependencies during generation. By constraining overreliance on dominant modalities, SynGR enables the model to capture emergent item semantics beyond shared or modality-specific signals. Extensive experiments across three benchmark datasets demonstrate that SynGR achieves superior performance.

</details>


### [300] [Code-Guided Reasoning for Small Language Models: Evaluating Executable MCQA Scaffolds](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.18827)
*Prateek Biswas,Dhaval Patel,Vedant Khandelwal,Shuxin Lin,Amit Sheth*

Main category: cs.IR

TL;DR: Code-Guided Reasoning (CGR) is a new evaluation protocol for measuring when executable reasoning scaffolds improve small language model performance on multiple-choice QA. CGR shows assisted inference yields +28.10 percentage points improvement over direct answering (66.21% vs 38.11%), though with caveats including regressions, extraction brittleness, and budget costs.


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: Current MCQA benchmarks only evaluate SLMs as direct answerers, but real-world deployed systems use external scaffolds (tools, code, repeated calls). Existing benchmarks lack standardized evaluation of when these reasoning scaffolds actually improve performance.

Method: The authors standardize CGR with six components: normalized item interface, direct solver prompt, generator prompt, Python scaffold, solver-call/extraction helpers, and three-channel result record. Experiments compare direct vs. assisted inference across 20,498 MCQA instances using six solver models, with pair-bootstrap confidence intervals.

Result: Assisted inference achieves 66.21% macro accuracy vs. 38.11% direct accuracy (+28.10 pp, bootstrap CI: [20.32, 36.43]). Under stricter conditions (Ab > 30% direct-signal gate), the gain is +14.11 pp. Notable limitations: Time-MQA shows regressions, answer extraction is brittle, and some generated programs violate the no-hard-coding instruction.

Conclusion: CGR enables measurable performance improvements through executable scaffolds, but comes with costs (larger inference budget, brittle extraction, regressions in Time-MQA, and rare hard-coding violations). The provided trace package allows full result interpretation.

Abstract: Multiple-choice QA benchmarks usually evaluate small language models (SLMs) as direct answerers, but deployed language-model systems increasingly rely on external scaffolds such as tools, code, and repeated model calls. We introduce Code-Guided Reasoning (CGR), an evaluation protocol and generated-program resource for measuring when executable reasoning scaffolds improve SLM performance on MCQA tasks. CGR standardizes six components: a normalized item interface, a direct solver prompt, a generator prompt, a Python scaffold, solver-call and extraction helpers, and a three-channel result record. On 20,498 retained result rows from a locally prepared MCQA bundle and six metadata-registered solver models, the observed non-zero-baseline partition shows 66.21% macro assisted accuracy versus 38.11% direct accuracy, a +28.10 percentage-point difference with a pair-bootstrap interval of [20.32, 36.43]. Under a stricter Ab > 30% direct-signal gate, the macro difference is +14.11 points. These estimates are descriptive. Assisted inference uses a larger solver-call budget, answer extraction is brittle, Time-MQA contains the observed regressions, and some generated programs violate the no-hard-coding instruction. CGR provides the trace package needed to interpret these results, including direct, assisted, and generator-side answers, partition definitions, generated programs, response metadata, and audits.

</details>

### [301] [Beyond Nutrition Labels: How Analogical Reasoning Shapes Synthetic Media Disclosure Design](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.19045)
*Claire R. Leibowicz*

Main category: cs.CY

TL;DR: 本文通过23位专家访谈和13个案例研究，探讨AI政策和实践者如何在复杂的社会技术约束下设计合成媒体披露机制。研究识别了关键披露目标（过程透明度和伤害减少）以及两个核心张力（规范性vs中立性、主动性vs精确性），并发现类比推理（如营养标签、Prop 65警告）在管理这些张力中起重要作用。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 随着合成媒体的激增，AI决策者越来越多地采用披露机制来帮助受众评估媒体可信度。然而，虽然用户对披露的理解已有研究，但上游决策过程仍缺乏探索。本研究旨在填补这一空白，深入理解AI政策制定者和实践者如何设计合成媒体披露机制。

Method: 采用混合研究方法：(1) 23位专家深度访谈；(2) 13个参与PAI合成媒体框架组织的案例研究。研究者系统分析了披露设计目标及其实践中面临的挑战。

Result: 研究识别了关键披露目标：过程透明度与伤害减少；发现两组核心张力：规范性vs中立性、主动性vs精确性；揭示类比推理（如营养标签、Prop 65警告）在管理（但未完全解决）这些张力中的重要作用。

Conclusion: 研究强调了聚焦AI透明度决策者及其类比推理使用的学术研究必要性，以支持AI时代媒体受众做出明智判断。

Abstract: As synthetic media proliferates, AI policymakers and practitioners have increasingly turned to disclosures--signals describing how media has been created or modified by AI--to help audiences evaluate media credibility. While there is a growing body of research on user interpretations, the upstream decision-making processes that affect users remain underexplored. This study therefore examines how AI policymakers and practitioners design synthetic media disclosures under complex sociotechnical constraints. Drawing on 23 expert interviews and 13 case studies from organizations participating in the Partnership on AI's Synthetic Media Framework, analysis identifies key disclosure goals, including process transparency and harm reduction, and two central tensions that emerge when pursuing those goals: normativity versus neutrality and proactivity versus precision. Findings highlight the role of analogical reasoning, from nutrition labels to Prop 65 warnings, in managing, but not resolving tensions. Ultimately, this study emphasizes the need for scholarship focused on AI transparency decision-makers and their use of analogical reasoning to support audiences encountering media in the AI age.

</details>


### [302] [Automated Grading of Handwritten Mathematics Using Vision-Capable LLMs](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.19043)
*Jacob Levine,Miguel Aenlle,Craig Zilles,Matthew West,Mariana Silva*

Main category: cs.CY

TL;DR: Summary generation failed


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: Motivation analysis unavailable

Method: Method extraction failed

Result: Result analysis unavailable

Conclusion: Conclusion extraction failed

Abstract: Automated grading systems have enabled scalable assessment for many response types, but handwritten mathematics remains a barrier due to the complexity of multi-step solutions. Vision-capable large language models (LLMs) offer new opportunities here, yet their reliability in authentic instructional settings remains poorly understood. We present an empirical evaluation of an LLM-based grader for handwritten mathematical work using instructor-defined rubrics. Extending a prior pipeline for typed responses, we integrate transcription and rubric-based evaluation of photographic submissions within a single LLM call, evaluating on student work from two university STEM courses. Comparing AI grading decisions against human-assigned ground truth at the rubric-item level, we observe high overall accuracy, with most errors -- 87\% in the best model -- attributable to transcription failures rather than rubric misapplication. We categorize common error modes, including image quality issues, hallucinated content, and incorrect handling of equivalent expressions. These findings highlight both the promise and limitations of LLM-based grading for handwritten mathematics, providing guidance for system design, prompt refinement, and deployment in educational settings.

</details>


### [303] [Going PLACES: Participatory Localized Red Teaming for Text-to-Image Safety in the Global South](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.19190)
*Charvi Rastogi,Mukul Bhutani,Minsuk Kahng,Shamsuddeen Hassan Muhammad,Evgeniia Razumovskaia,Priyanka Suresh,Ibrahim Said Ahmad,Charu Kalia,Yaaseen Mahomed,Madhurima Maji,Minjae Lee,Alicia Parrish,Jessica Quaye,Vijay Janapa Reddi,Aishwarya Verma,Lora Aroyo*

Main category: cs.CY

TL;DR: 本研究通过在全球南方的加纳、尼日利亚和印度（卡纳塔克邦和旁遮普邦）开展本地化社区中心红队研究，提出了一个超越西方中心主义的T2I安全框架。研究成果PLACES数据集包含超过26,000个模型失败案例，揭示了现有安全框架中的文化盲点，强调需要采用深度本地化和参与式的方法来改进AI安全研究。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有T2I安全框架主要基于西方中心视角校准，导致对世界其他地区存在显著安全漏洞，忽视了文化多元主义和历史上代表性不足的群体视角。

Method: 1）二次城市中心聚焦：选择全球南方的二级城市而非主流中心；2）社区参与式研究：开展社区参与和培训研讨会以情境化当地规范；3）合作数据收集：与加纳、尼日利亚、印度（卡纳塔克邦和旁遮普邦）的大学合作建立数据集。

Result: 1）建立了PLACES数据集，包含超过26,000个T2I模型失败案例；2）提示语在社会文化和语言属性上呈现高度多样性；3）发现由当地文化和语言细微差异驱动的新型对抗模式；4）识别出围绕特定主题（如印度宗教）的区域独特聚类；5）发现现有安全框架的结构性情境差距，包括规范性失谐（如违反宗教规范、忽视当地习俗、不祥符号学）的新型危害。

Conclusion: 研究主张扩展T2I安全需要超越单纯的数据规模，采用深度本地化的参与式方法进行数据收集和情境化。

<tool>
<invoke name="Structure">
<parameter name="conclusion">扩展T2I安全需要超越数据规模，采用深度本地化、参与式的数据收集和情境化方法。

Abstract: Despite the global deployment of text-to-image (T2I) models, their safety frameworks are largely calibrated to a Western-centric default, creating significant vulnerabilities for the rest of the world. To embrace cultural pluralism and bring historically under-represented perspectives in T2I safety, we conduct localised community-centered red teaming studies in the Global South. Our two-fold approach prioritizes localization and participation, by focusing on secondary urban centers in these regions, and conducting community engagement and training workshops to contextualize local norms. As a result, we present PLACES, a dataset comprising over 26,000 examples of T2I model failures collected in partnership with universities in Ghana, Nigeria, and two regions of India (Karnataka and Punjab). Analysis of prompts collected reveals a wide-ranging diversity in socio-cultural and linguistic attributes, when compared to existing geography-agnostic crowdsourced red-teaming data. We observe unique adversarial patterns enabled by local cultural and linguistic nuances, and distinct clusters within region around specific themes, such as religion in India. Moreover, we uncover structural contextual gaps in existing safety frameworks by identifying novel harms showing normative dissonance (e.g., violating religious norms, ignoring local customs, and ominous symbolism). This work argues that expanding T2I safety requires moving beyond mere scale to incorporate deeply localised, participatory methodologies for data collection and contextualization. Content warning: This paper includes examples containing potentially harmful or offensive content.

</details>

### [304] [Example-Driven Intent Synthesis for Constrained Data Bundle Retrieval: Focused Text Snippet Extraction and Beyond](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.19246)
*Whanhee Cho,Kuangfei Long,Mahmood Jasim,Matteo Brucato,Alexandra Meliou,Peter J. Haas,Anna Fariha*

Main category: cs.DB

TL;DR: Summary generation failed


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: Motivation analysis unavailable

Method: Method extraction failed

Result: Result analysis unavailable

Conclusion: Conclusion extraction failed

Abstract: Selecting a bundle of items that collectively satisfies constraints is a fundamental task across databases, recommender systems, and text summarization. Unlike traditional retrieval that returns individual or top-k items, bundle retrieval is inherently combinatorial and, in general, NP-hard. Although package queries can efficiently retrieve bundles given a well-formed query, two key user-centric challenges remain: (1) expressing and tuning multi-dimensional bundle intent through a user-friendly interface, and (2) ensuring feasibility when the query yields empty results. We introduce Ex2Bundle, an Example-driven Bundle retrieval framework that enables users to specify their intent through example bundles and automatically synthesizes package queries that capture the intent implicit in those example bundles via aggregate constraints. Ex2Bundle also addresses a challenge unique to bundle retrieval: when inferred aggregate constraints are infeasible over the target data, our data-aware constraint relaxation minimally adjusts the constraint bounds while preserving alignment with user intent. We instantiate a specific application of focused text snippet extraction by example to demonstrate the efficacy of the Ex2Bundle framework. Extensive experiments over real-world datasets and a user study demonstrate that Ex2Bundle improves usability and consistently returns intent-aligned bundles even under distributional shifts of the target database.

</details>


### [305] [Leveraging I/O Stalls for Efficient Scheduling in ANNS](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.19335)
*Juncheng Zhang,Yuanming Ren,Yongkun Li,Patrick P. C. Lee*

Main category: cs.DB

TL;DR: LIOS框架通过利用搜索线程在磁盘I/O等待期间的空闲周期来执行索引更新操作，解决磁盘图索引在高并发查询与更新场景下的CPU资源浪费问题。该方案将更新任务拆分为可恢复子任务，并动态控制更新比例以维持查询延迟在目标范围内，最终在FreshDiskANN和OdinANN系统中实现插入2.68倍、删除2.18倍的加速效果。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 磁盘图索引需同时处理延迟敏感的查询和吞吐量密集的更新操作。论文观察到超过40%的搜索线程CPU时间消耗在磁盘I/O等待上，这些空闲周期对线程级调度不可见，但本可用于执行其他任务。现有系统难以有效利用这类隐性空闲资源。

Method: LIOS提出三项核心技术：(i) 将每个更新操作拆分为可恢复的子任务，确保其可在一个I/O stall窗口内完成；(ii) 对更新子任务的预期超时进行边界控制；(iii) 动态调整分配给更新的空闲时间比例，使端到端搜索延迟退化逼近用户指定目标。框架已集成至FreshDiskANN和OdinANN两个更新优化的ANNS系统。

Result: 实验结果表明，LIOS在插入操作中实现最高2.68倍加速，删除操作中实现最高2.18倍加速，同时将搜索延迟退化控制在用户指定目标附近。

Conclusion: 通过巧妙利用I/O stall期间的空闲周期，LIOS有效提升了磁盘图索引系统的更新吞吐量，且对查询延迟的影响可控。该框架具有良好的通用性，可适配多种更新优化的ANNS系统。

Abstract: Disk-based graph indexes for approximate nearest neighbor search (ANNS) must serve latency-sensitive queries and throughput-demanding updates concurrently. We observe that over 40% of search-thread CPU time is spent stalling on disk I/O; such idle cycles are invisible to thread-level scheduling yet available for other work. We present LIOS(Leverage I/O Stall), a framework that executes index updates inside search-side I/O stall windows. LIOS introduces three techniques: (i) splitting each update into resumable subtasks small enough to fit within a single stall window; (ii) bounding the expected overrun of update subtasks to a given threshold; and (iii) dynamically adjusting the fraction of idle time devoted to updates to drive end-to-end search latency degradation toward a user-specified target. We integrate LIOS into two update-optimized ANNS systems, FreshDiskANN and OdinANN. LIOS achieves speedups of up to 2.68$\times$ in insertion and 2.18$\times$ in deletion, with search latency degradation maintained near the user-specified target.

</details>
